# VNB catalog: theorems, axioms, definitions, assertions

Auto-generated by `(catalog)`.  2492 results — 990 proven, 563 support (PSS), 941 stated without proof (199 axioms, 621 definitions, 121 assertions).

> **Trust.**  This catalog lists *what* is proven; how much each
> proof still leans on unproved facts is the companion ledger
> [`PROOF-DEBT.md`](PROOF-DEBT.md).  In short: a proof's *bill*
> is the set of `asserted` facts it rests on; an empty bill prints
> `proven modulo 0` (unconditional relative to the kernel -- the
> strongest report), and a non-empty bill carries a `[trust: K]`
> tier equal to its **weakest** leaf's warrant, where `none`
> (a leaf with no warrant at all) is weakest and `proof` strongest.
> The ledger heads with the full glossary and tier table.

## Proven theorems

- `abelian-group-as-monoid-functorial` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-abelian-group(a, b, f) implies is-hom-monoid(abelian-group-as-monoid(a), abelian-group-as-monoid(b), f))
- `abelian-group-idempotent-is-id` — forall([s], is-abelian-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], (opr(s))(a, a) = a implies a = iden(s)))
- `abelian-group-is-group` — forall([s], is-abelian-group(s) implies is-group(s))
- `abelian-group-opr-comm` — forall([s], is-abelian-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], (opr(s))(a, b) = (opr(s))(b, a)))
- `abs-seq-in-fun` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr)], vnb-lambda(n_, nn, abs(f(n_))) in fun(nn, rr))
- `affine-continuous-at` — forall([c, lam, t_], c in rr and lam in rr and t_ in rr implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(x, rr, c + lam * x), t_))
- `affine-lam-in-fun` — forall([c, lam], c in rr and lam in rr implies vnb-lambda(x, rr, c + lam * x) in fun(rr, rr))
- `ag-cancel-right` — forall([s], is-abelian-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s), c in carr(s)], (opr(s))(a, c) = (opr(s))(b, c) implies a = b))
- `anti-lam-in-fun` — forall([cf in fun(nn, rr), m in nn], vnb-lambda(x, rr, series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(k, nn, (recip(succ(k)) * cf(k)) * x ^ succ(k)), m)) in fun(rr, rr))

- `antiderivable-add` — forall([phi, psi, a, b], is-antiderivable(phi, a, b) implies is-antiderivable(psi, a, b) implies is-antiderivable(vnb-lambda(x, rr, phi(x) + psi(x)), a, b))
- `antiderivable-affine-subst` — forall([phi, cc, lam, p_, q_, aa, bb], phi in fun(rr, rr) implies cc in rr implies lam in rr implies not(lam = 0) implies p_ in rr implies q_ in rr implies p_ < q_ implies is-antiderivable(phi, aa, bb) implies forall([t_ in ccint(p_, q_)], cc + lam * t_ in ccint(aa, bb)) implies forall([t_], t_ in rr and p_ < t_ and t_ < q_ implies aa < cc + lam * t_ and cc + lam * t_ < bb) implies is-antiderivable(vnb-lambda(x, rr, phi(cc + lam * x)), p_, q_))
- `antiderivable-affine-subst-pos` — forall([phi, cc, lam, p_, q_], phi in fun(rr, rr) implies cc in rr implies lam in rr implies 0 < lam implies p_ in rr implies q_ in rr implies p_ < q_ implies is-antiderivable(phi, cc + lam * p_, cc + lam * q_) implies is-antiderivable(vnb-lambda(x, rr, phi(cc + lam * x)), p_, q_))
- `antiderivable-affine-subst-pos-transfer` — forall([phi, psi, cc, lam, p_, q_], phi in fun(rr, rr) implies psi in fun(rr, rr) implies cc in rr implies lam in rr implies 0 < lam implies p_ in rr implies q_ in rr implies p_ < q_ implies is-antiderivable(phi, cc + lam * p_, cc + lam * q_) implies forall([x_ in rr], psi(x_) == phi(cc + lam * x_)) implies is-antiderivable(psi, p_, q_))

- `antiderivable-sub` — forall([phi, psi, a, b], is-antiderivable(phi, a, b) implies is-antiderivable(psi, a, b) implies is-antiderivable(vnb-lambda(x, rr, phi(x) - psi(x)), a, b))
- `antiderivable-subinterval` — forall([phi, a, b, lo, hi], is-antiderivable(phi, a, b) implies lo in rr implies hi in rr implies a <= lo implies lo < hi implies hi <= b implies is-antiderivable(phi, lo, hi))
- `antiderivable-uniform-limit` — forall([phifam in fun(nn, fun(rr, rr)), psi in fun(rr, rr), a in rr, b in rr], forall([k_ in nn], is-antiderivable(phifam(k_), a, b)) implies forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([n_ in nn], forall([k_ in nn], n_ <= k_ implies forall([x_ in rr], x_ in ccint(a, b) implies abs(psi(x_) - (phifam(k_))(x_)) <= eps)))) implies is-antiderivable(psi, a, b))
- `antiderivative-add` — forall([f, g, phi, psi, a, b], is-antiderivative(f, phi, a, b) implies is-antiderivative(g, psi, a, b) implies is-antiderivative(vnb-lambda(x, rr, f(x) + g(x)), vnb-lambda(x, rr, phi(x) + psi(x)), a, b))

- `antiderivative-endpoints` — forall([f, phi, a, b], is-antiderivative(f, phi, a, b) implies a in rr and b in rr and a < b)
- `antiderivative-family-uniform-limit` — forall([fam in fun(nn, fun(rr, rr)), phifam in fun(nn, fun(rr, rr)), psi in fun(rr, rr), a in rr, b in rr], a < b implies forall([k_ in nn], is-antiderivative(fam(k_), phifam(k_), a, b) and (fam(k_))(a) = 0) implies forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([n_ in nn], forall([k_ in nn], n_ <= k_ implies forall([x_ in rr], x_ in ccint(a, b) implies abs(psi(x_) - (phifam(k_))(x_)) <= eps)))) implies forsome([f in fun(rr, rr)], is-antiderivative(f, psi, a, b) and forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([n_ in nn], forall([k_ in nn], n_ <= k_ implies forall([x_ in rr], x_ in ccint(a, b) implies abs(f(x_) - (fam(k_))(x_)) <= eps))))))
- `antiderivative-fn-in-fun` — forall([f, phi, a, b], is-antiderivative(f, phi, a, b) implies phi in fun(rr, rr))
- `antiderivative-increment-bound` — forall([h in fun(rr, rr), phi in fun(rr, rr), a in rr, b in rr, m_ in rr], is-antiderivative(h, phi, a, b) implies forall([x_ in rr], x_ in ccint(a, b) implies abs(phi(x_)) <= m_) implies forall([u in rr, v in rr], u in ccint(a, b) implies v in ccint(a, b) implies u < v implies abs(h(v) - h(u)) <= m_ * (v - u)))
- `antiderivative-integrand-transfer` — forall([f, phi, psi, a, b], psi in fun(rr, rr) implies is-antiderivative(f, phi, a, b) implies forall([x_ in rr], psi(x_) == phi(x_)) implies is-antiderivative(f, psi, a, b))
- `antiderivative-lipschitz` — forall([h in fun(rr, rr), phi in fun(rr, rr), a in rr, b in rr, m_ in rr], is-antiderivative(h, phi, a, b) implies forall([x_ in rr], x_ in ccint(a, b) implies abs(phi(x_)) <= m_) implies forall([u in rr, v in rr], u in ccint(a, b) implies v in ccint(a, b) implies abs(h(v) - h(u)) <= m_ * abs(v - u)))
- `antiderivative-map-in-fun` — forall([f, phi, a, b], is-antiderivative(f, phi, a, b) implies f in fun(rr, rr))
- `antiderivative-pair-close` — forall([f in fun(rr, rr), g in fun(rr, rr), phi in fun(rr, rr), psi in fun(rr, rr), a in rr, b in rr, m_ in rr], is-antiderivative(f, phi, a, b) implies is-antiderivative(g, psi, a, b) implies f(a) = g(a) implies forall([x_ in rr], x_ in ccint(a, b) implies abs(phi(x_) - psi(x_)) <= m_) implies forall([x_ in rr], x_ in ccint(a, b) implies abs(f(x_) - g(x_)) <= m_ * (b - a)))
- `antiderivative-scale` — forall([c, f, phi, a, b], c in rr implies is-antiderivative(f, phi, a, b) implies is-antiderivative(vnb-lambda(x, rr, c * f(x)), vnb-lambda(x, rr, c * phi(x)), a, b))
- `antiderivative-shift-const` — forall([f, phi, a, b, c], f in fun(rr, rr) implies phi in fun(rr, rr) implies a in rr implies b in rr implies c in rr implies is-antiderivative(f, phi, a, b) implies is-antiderivative(vnb-lambda(x_, rr, f(x_) - c), phi, a, b))
- `antiderivative-sub` — forall([f, g, phi, psi, a, b], is-antiderivative(f, phi, a, b) implies is-antiderivative(g, psi, a, b) implies is-antiderivative(vnb-lambda(x, rr, f(x) - g(x)), vnb-lambda(x, rr, phi(x) - psi(x)), a, b))
- `antiderivative-subinterval` — forall([f, phi, a, b, lo, hi], is-antiderivative(f, phi, a, b) implies lo in rr implies hi in rr implies a <= lo implies lo < hi implies hi <= b implies is-antiderivative(f, phi, lo, hi))
- `antiderivative-transfer-ptwise-eq` — forall([f in fun(rr, rr), psi in fun(rr, rr), g, phi, a, b], is-antiderivative(g, phi, a, b) implies forall([x_ in rr], f(x_) = g(x_)) implies forall([x_ in rr], psi(x_) = phi(x_)) implies is-antiderivative(f, psi, a, b))
- `ascoli-dense-bridge` — forall([s, fam], is-compact(s) implies fam in fun(nn, fun(pts(s), rr)) implies forall([k in nn], is-continuous(s, rr-ms, fam(k))) implies is-equicontinuous(s, rr-ms, fam) implies forsome([dseq], is-dense-seq(s, dseq) and converges-on(s, fam, dseq)) implies forsome([g in fun(pts(s), rr)], is-continuous(s, rr-ms, g) and converges-uniformly(s, fam, g)))
- `bdd-fn-reflect` — forall([a in rr, b in rr], 0 <= a implies 0 <= b implies (/)(a, 1 + a) <= (/)(b, 1 + b) implies a <= b)
- `bdd-metric-converges-bwd` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([f in fun(nn, pts(s)), lm_ in pts(s)], converges-to(bdd-metric(s), f, lm_) implies converges-to(s, f, lm_)))
- `bdd-metric-converges-fwd` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([f in fun(nn, pts(s)), lm_ in pts(s)], converges-to(s, f, lm_) implies converges-to(bdd-metric(s), f, lm_)))
- `bdd-metric-converges-iff` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([f in fun(nn, pts(s)), lm_ in pts(s)], converges-to(bdd-metric(s), f, lm_) iff converges-to(s, f, lm_)))
- `bdd-metric-dist-le` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([x_ in pts(s), y_ in pts(s)], (dist(bdd-metric(s)))(x_, y_) <= (dist(s))(x_, y_)))
- `bdd-metric-dist-reflect` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([x_ in pts(s), y_ in pts(s), e_ in rr], 0 <= e_ implies (dist(bdd-metric(s)))(x_, y_) <= (/)(e_, 1 + e_) implies (dist(s))(x_, y_) <= e_))
- `bdd-metric-weight-bound` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([u_ in pts(s), v_ in pts(s), wn in rr], 0 <= wn implies wn * (dist(bdd-metric(s)))(u_, v_) in rr and 0 <= wn * (dist(bdd-metric(s)))(u_, v_) and wn * (dist(bdd-metric(s)))(u_, v_) <= wn))
- `bernstein-approx-bound` — forall([f_, n_, x_, hh_, delta_, mm_, cc_], f_ in fun(rr, rr) implies n_ in nn implies not(n_ = 0) implies x_ in ccint(0, 1) implies hh_ in rr implies 0 < hh_ implies delta_ in rr implies 0 < delta_ implies mm_ in rr implies 0 <= mm_ implies cc_ in rr implies 0 <= cc_ implies (n_ * n_) * ((delta_ * delta_) * cc_) = mm_ + mm_ implies cc_ * n_ <= hh_ implies forall([p_ in ccint(0, 1), q_ in ccint(0, 1)], abs(p_ - q_) <= delta_ implies abs(f_(p_) - f_(q_)) <= hh_) implies forall([z_ in ccint(0, 1)], abs(f_(z_)) <= mm_) implies abs(f_(x_) - bernstein-poly(f_, n_, x_)) <= hh_ + hh_)
- `bernstein-basis-above` — forall([n_, x_, k_], n_ in nn implies x_ in rr implies k_ in zz implies n_ < k_ implies (bernstein-basis(n_, x_))(k_) = 0)
- `bernstein-basis-antiderivable` — forall([n_, k_, a, b], n_ in nn implies k_ in nn implies a in rr and b in rr and a < b implies is-antiderivable(vnb-lambda(x, rr, (bernstein-basis(n_, x))(k_)), a, b))
- `bernstein-basis-degree-zero-diag` — forall([x_, k_], x_ in rr implies k_ in nn implies k_ = 0 implies (bernstein-basis(0, x_))(k_) = 1)
- `bernstein-basis-degree-zero-off` — forall([x_, k_], x_ in rr implies k_ in nn implies not(k_ = 0) implies (bernstein-basis(0, x_))(k_) = 0)
- `bernstein-basis-in-fun` — forall([n_ in nn, x_ in rr], bernstein-basis(n_, x_) in fun(zz, rr))
- `bernstein-basis-nonneg` — forall([n_ in nn, x_, k_], x_ in rr implies 0 <= x_ implies x_ <= 1 implies k_ in nn implies 0 <= (bernstein-basis(n_, x_))(k_))
- `bernstein-basis-null` — forall([n_, x_, k_], n_ in nn implies x_ in rr implies k_ in zz implies k_ < 0 implies (bernstein-basis(n_, x_))(k_) = 0)
- `bernstein-basis-ptwise-in-rr` — forall([n_, x_, k_], n_ in nn implies x_ in rr implies k_ in zz implies (bernstein-basis(n_, x_))(k_) in rr)
- `bernstein-basis-scaled-antiderivable` — forall([c, n_, k_, a, b], c in rr implies n_ in nn implies k_ in nn implies a in rr and b in rr and a < b implies is-antiderivable(vnb-lambda(x, rr, c * (bernstein-basis(n_, x))(k_)), a, b))
- `bernstein-basis-succ` — forall([n_, x_, k_], n_ in nn implies x_ in rr implies k_ in nn implies (bernstein-basis(succ(n_), x_))(k_) = x_ * (bernstein-basis(n_, x_))(k_ - 1) + (1 - x_) * (bernstein-basis(n_, x_))(k_))
- `bernstein-basis-unfold` — forall([n_, x_], bernstein-basis(n_, x_) == comb-kk(normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field), x_, 1 - x_, n_))
- `bernstein-ccint-approximant-antiderivable` — forall([f, n_, a, b], f in fun(rr, rr) implies n_ in nn implies not(n_ = 0) implies a in rr and b in rr and a < b implies is-antiderivable(vnb-lambda(x, rr, bernstein-poly(compose(f, vnb-lambda(x_, rr, a + (b - a) * x_)), n_, (x - a) * recip(b - a))), a, b))
- `bernstein-moment-1` — forall([n_ in nn, x_, h_], x_ in rr implies forall([k_ in nn], h_(k_) = k_ * (bernstein-basis(n_, x_))(k_)) implies series-partial-sum(h_, succ(n_)) = n_ * x_)
- `bernstein-moment-2` — forall([n_ in nn, x_, h_], x_ in rr implies forall([k_ in nn], h_(k_) = (k_ * (k_ - 1)) * (bernstein-basis(n_, x_))(k_)) implies series-partial-sum(h_, succ(n_)) = (n_ * (n_ - 1)) * (x_ * x_))
- `bernstein-partition` — forall([n_ in nn, x_ in rr], series-partial-sum(bernstein-basis(n_, x_), succ(n_)) = 1)
- `bernstein-poly-antiderivable` — forall([f, n_, a, b], f in fun(rr, rr) implies n_ in nn implies not(n_ = 0) implies a in rr and b in rr and a < b implies is-antiderivable(vnb-lambda(x, rr, bernstein-poly(f, n_, x)), a, b))
- `bernstein-poly-unit-coordinate-antiderivable` — forall([g, n_, a, b], g in fun(rr, rr) implies n_ in nn implies not(n_ = 0) implies a in rr and b in rr and a < b implies is-antiderivable(vnb-lambda(x, rr, bernstein-poly(g, n_, (x - a) * recip(b - a))), a, b))
- `bernstein-term-antiderivable` — forall([n_ in nn, a, b, k_, j_], a in rr and b in rr and a < b implies k_ in nn implies j_ in nn implies is-antiderivable(vnb-lambda(x, rr, x ^ j_ * (bernstein-basis(n_, x))(k_)), a, b))
- `bernstein-term-bound` — forall([f_, n_, x_, hh_, delta_, mm_, cc_, l_], f_ in fun(rr, rr) implies n_ in nn implies not(n_ = 0) implies x_ in ccint(0, 1) implies hh_ in rr implies 0 < hh_ implies delta_ in rr implies 0 < delta_ implies mm_ in rr implies 0 <= mm_ implies cc_ in rr implies 0 <= cc_ implies (n_ * n_) * ((delta_ * delta_) * cc_) = mm_ + mm_ implies forall([p_ in ccint(0, 1), q_ in ccint(0, 1)], abs(p_ - q_) <= delta_ implies abs(f_(p_) - f_(q_)) <= hh_) implies forall([z_ in ccint(0, 1)], abs(f_(z_)) <= mm_) implies l_ in nn implies (bernstein-basis(n_, x_))(l_) * (f_(x_) - f_((/)(l_, n_))) <= (bernstein-basis(n_, x_))(l_) * (hh_ + cc_ * ((l_ - n_ * x_) * (l_ - n_ * x_))) and (bernstein-basis(n_, x_))(l_) * ((0 - 1) * (hh_ + cc_ * ((l_ - n_ * x_) * (l_ - n_ * x_)))) <= (bernstein-basis(n_, x_))(l_) * (f_(x_) - f_((/)(l_, n_))))
- `bernstein-uniform-approximation` — forall([f_, eps_], f_ in fun(rr, rr) implies forall([x in ccint(0, 1)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f_, x)) implies pos-rr(eps_) implies forsome([cap_ in nn], forall([n_, x_], n_ in nn implies cap_ <= n_ implies x_ in ccint(0, 1) implies abs(f_(x_) - bernstein-poly(f_, n_, x_)) <= eps_)))
- `bernstein-uniform-approximation-ccint` — forall([fn_, a, b, ep_], fn_ in fun(rr, rr) implies a in rr and b in rr and a < b implies forall([x_ in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, fn_, x_)) implies pos-rr(ep_) implies forsome([cap_ in nn], forall([n_], n_ in nn and cap_ <= n_ implies forall([x_ in ccint(a, b)], abs(fn_(x_) - bernstein-poly(compose(fn_, vnb-lambda(x, rr, a + (b - a) * x)), n_, (x_ - a) * recip(b - a))) <= ep_))))
- `bernstein-variance` — forall([n_, x_, h_], n_ in nn implies x_ in rr implies forall([k_ in nn], h_(k_) = ((k_ - n_ * x_) * (k_ - n_ * x_)) * (bernstein-basis(n_, x_))(k_)) implies series-partial-sum(h_, succ(n_)) = (n_ * x_) * (1 - x_))
- `bernstein-variance-bound` — forall([n_, x_, h_], n_ in nn implies not(n_ = 0) implies x_ in rr implies forall([k_ in nn], h_(k_) = ((k_ - n_ * x_) * (k_ - n_ * x_)) * (bernstein-basis(n_, x_))(k_)) implies (/)(1, n_) * series-partial-sum(h_, succ(n_)) <= 1)
- `bernstein-weighted-step` — forall([n_, x_, w_, p_, q_, s_], n_ in nn implies x_ in rr implies forall([k_ in zz], w_(k_) in rr) implies forall([k_ in nn], q_(k_) in rr) implies forall([k_ in nn], s_(k_) in rr) implies forall([k_ in nn], p_(k_) = w_(k_) * (bernstein-basis(succ(n_), x_))(k_)) implies forall([k_ in nn], q_(k_) = w_(succ(k_)) * (bernstein-basis(n_, x_))(k_)) implies forall([k_ in nn], s_(k_) = w_(k_) * (bernstein-basis(n_, x_))(k_)) implies series-partial-sum(p_, succ(succ(n_))) = x_ * series-partial-sum(q_, succ(n_)) + (1 - x_) * series-partial-sum(s_, succ(n_)))
- `big-union-mono` — forall([a_, b_], a_ subset b_ implies big-union(u_, a_, u_) subset big-union(u_, b_, u_))
- `bijection-in-fun` — forall([x, y, phi in bijection(x, y)], phi in fun(x, y))
- `bijection-injective` — forall([x, y, phi in bijection(x, y), a in x, b in x], phi(a) = phi(b) implies a = b)
- `bijection-is-injection` — forall([x, y, phi in bijection(x, y)], phi in injection(x, y))
- `bijection-surjective` — forall([x, y, phi in bijection(x, y), w in y], forsome([z in x], phi(z) = w))
- `binomial-sum-value` — forall([n_, r, x_, y_], n_ in nn implies is-commutative-ring(r) implies x_ in carr(r) implies y_ in carr(r) implies sum(r, comb-kk(r, x_, y_, n_), succ(n_)) = ring-power(r, (add(r))(x_, y_), n_))
- `binomial-theorem` — forall([n in nn, r], is-commutative-ring(r) implies forall([x in carr(r), y in carr(r)], ring-power(r, (add(r))(x, y), n) = sum(r, comb-kk(r, x, y, n), succ(n))))
- `block-family-combinatorial` — forall([v in set, f in fun(nn, v), cov], forall([k in nn], is-finite-cover(cov(k), v)) implies forsome([blk in fun(nn, inf-subsets(nn))], forall([k in nn], blk(succ(k)) subset blk(k)) and forall([k in nn], forsome([u in cov(k)], forall([i in blk(k)], f(i) in u)))))
- `border-identity` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([p in nn], border(a, one(a), identmat(a, p), p, p) = identmat(a, succ(p))))
- `border-invertible` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([n, u], n in nn implies is-invertible-mat(a, n, u) implies is-invertible-mat(a, succ(n), border(a, one(a), u, n, n))))
- `border-is-diagonal` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([b, e, p, q], p in nn implies q in nn implies is-diagonal(a, p, q, e) implies is-diagonal(a, succ(p), succ(q), border(a, b, e, p, q))))
- `border-mult` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([p, q, r, x, y, b, d], p in nn implies q in nn implies r in nn implies x in mat(p, q, carr(a)) implies y in mat(q, r, carr(a)) implies b in carr(a) implies d in carr(a) implies matmul(a, border(a, b, x, p, q), border(a, d, y, q, r)) = border(a, (mul(a))(b, d), matmul(a, x, y), p, r)))
- `border-staircase` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([b, w, p, q, kp], p in nn implies q in nn implies kp in nn implies b in carr(a) implies not(b = zero(a)) implies smith-staircase(a, p, q, w, kp) implies smith-staircase(a, succ(p), succ(q), border(a, b, w, p, q), succ(kp))))
- `bordered-eq-border` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([p, q, c], p in nn implies q in nn implies c in mat(succ(p), succ(q), carr(a)) implies forall([j in interval(1, succ(q))], not(j = 1) implies entry(c, 1, j) = zero(a)) implies forall([i in interval(1, succ(p))], not(i = 1) implies entry(c, i, 1) = zero(a)) implies c = border(a, entry(c, 1, 1), submat(c, p, q), p, q)))
- `bordering` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([p, q, b, w, g, e], p in nn implies q in nn implies b in carr(a) implies w in mat(p, q, carr(a)) implies g in mat(succ(p), succ(q), carr(a)) implies g = border(a, b, w, p, q) implies mat-equiv(a, p, q, w, e) implies mat-equiv(a, succ(p), succ(q), g, border(a, b, e, p, q))))
- `bt-add-in-carr` — forall([r], is-commutative-ring(r) implies forall([a in carr(r), b in carr(r)], (add(r))(a, b) in carr(r)))
- `c-int-add` — forall([phi, psi, a, b], is-antiderivable(phi, a, b) implies is-antiderivable(psi, a, b) implies c-int(vnb-lambda(x, rr, phi(x) + psi(x)), a, b) = c-int(phi, a, b) + c-int(psi, a, b))
- `c-int-add-bounds` — forall([phi, a, b, c], is-antiderivable(phi, a, c) implies b in rr implies a < b implies b < c implies c-int(phi, a, c) = c-int(phi, a, b) + c-int(phi, b, c))
- `c-int-add-continuous` — forall([phi, psi, a, b], phi in fun(rr, rr) implies psi in fun(rr, rr) implies a in rr implies b in rr implies a < b implies forall([x_ in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, phi, x_)) implies forall([x_ in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, psi, x_)) implies c-int(vnb-lambda(x, rr, phi(x) + psi(x)), a, b) = c-int(phi, a, b) + c-int(psi, a, b))
- `c-int-change-of-variable` — forall([phi, amap, ader, p_, q_], amap in fun(rr, rr) implies ader in fun(rr, rr) implies p_ in rr implies q_ in rr implies p_ < q_ implies is-antiderivable(phi, amap(p_), amap(q_)) implies forall([u_ in ccint(p_, q_)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, amap, u_)) implies forall([w_], w_ in rr and p_ < w_ and w_ < q_ implies is-diff-at(amap, w_, ader(w_))) implies forall([u_ in ccint(p_, q_)], amap(u_) in ccint(amap(p_), amap(q_))) implies forall([w_], w_ in rr and p_ < w_ and w_ < q_ implies amap(p_) < amap(w_) and amap(w_) < amap(q_)) implies c-int(vnb-lambda(x, rr, phi(amap(x)) * ader(x)), p_, q_) = c-int(phi, amap(p_), amap(q_)))
- `c-int-change-of-variable-transfer` — forall([phi, psi, amap, ader, p_, q_], amap in fun(rr, rr) implies ader in fun(rr, rr) implies p_ in rr implies q_ in rr implies p_ < q_ implies psi in fun(rr, rr) implies is-antiderivable(phi, amap(p_), amap(q_)) implies forall([u_ in ccint(p_, q_)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, amap, u_)) implies forall([w_], w_ in rr and p_ < w_ and w_ < q_ implies is-diff-at(amap, w_, ader(w_))) implies forall([u_ in ccint(p_, q_)], amap(u_) in ccint(amap(p_), amap(q_))) implies forall([w_], w_ in rr and p_ < w_ and w_ < q_ implies amap(p_) < amap(w_) and amap(w_) < amap(q_)) implies forall([x_ in rr], psi(x_) == phi(amap(x_)) * ader(x_)) implies c-int(psi, p_, q_) = c-int(phi, amap(p_), amap(q_)))
- `c-int-defined-for-continuous` — forall([f, a, b], f in fun(rr, rr) implies a in rr implies b in rr implies a < b implies forall([x_ in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x_)) implies c-int(f, a, b) in rr)
- `c-int-in-rr` — forall([phi, a, b], is-antiderivable(phi, a, b) implies c-int(phi, a, b) in rr)
- `c-int-or-additive` — forall([phi, p, q, a, b, c], is-antiderivable(phi, p, q) implies a in ccint(p, q) implies b in ccint(p, q) implies c in ccint(p, q) implies c-int-or(phi, a, c) = c-int-or(phi, a, b) + c-int-or(phi, b, c))
- `c-int-or-anti` — forall([phi, a, b], is-antiderivable(phi, a, b) implies c-int-or(phi, a, b) = c-int(phi, a, b))
- `c-int-or-degenerate` — forall([phi, a], c-int-or(phi, a, a) = 0)
- `c-int-or-flip` — forall([phi, a, b], is-antiderivable(phi, a, b) implies c-int-or(phi, b, a) = -c-int(phi, a, b))
- `c-int-or-in-rr` — forall([phi, a, b], c-int-or(phi, a, b) in rr)
- `c-int-or-none` — forall([phi, a, b], not(is-antiderivable(phi, a, b)) implies not(is-antiderivable(phi, b, a)) implies c-int-or(phi, a, b) = 0)
- `c-int-or-reverse` — forall([phi, a, b], c-int-or(phi, b, a) = -c-int-or(phi, a, b))
- `c-int-or-scale` — forall([lam, a, b], lam in rr implies a in rr implies b in rr implies 0 < lam implies 0 < a implies 0 < b implies c-int-or(vnb-lambda(z_, rr, recip-star(z_)), lam * a, lam * b) = c-int-or(vnb-lambda(z_, rr, recip-star(z_)), a, b))
- `c-int-or-scale-ordered` — forall([lam, a, b], lam in rr implies a in rr implies b in rr implies 0 < lam implies 0 < a implies a < b implies c-int-or(vnb-lambda(z_, rr, recip-star(z_)), lam * a, lam * b) = c-int-or(vnb-lambda(z_, rr, recip-star(z_)), a, b))
- `c-int-or-value` — forall([f, phi, p, q, a, b], is-antiderivative(f, phi, p, q) implies a in ccint(p, q) implies b in ccint(p, q) implies c-int-or(phi, a, b) = f(b) - f(a))
- `c-int-scale` — forall([c, phi, a, b], c in rr implies is-antiderivable(phi, a, b) implies c-int(vnb-lambda(x, rr, c * phi(x)), a, b) = c * c-int(phi, a, b))
- `c-int-scale-continuous` — forall([c, phi, a, b], c in rr implies phi in fun(rr, rr) implies a in rr implies b in rr implies a < b implies forall([x_ in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, phi, x_)) implies c-int(vnb-lambda(x, rr, c * phi(x)), a, b) = c * c-int(phi, a, b))
- `c-int-value` — forall([f, phi, a, b], is-antiderivative(f, phi, a, b) implies c-int(phi, a, b) = f(b) - f(a))
- `c-int-value-continuous` — forall([f, a, b], f in fun(rr, rr) implies a in rr implies b in rr implies a < b implies forall([x_ in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x_)) implies forsome([g_], is-antiderivative(g_, f, a, b) and c-int(f, a, b) = g_(b) - g_(a)))
- `c-metric-canonical-summable` — forall([s], is-c-metric-space(s) implies forall([u_, v_], u_ in pts(s) implies v_ in pts(s) implies series-converges(vnb-lambda(k, nn, (vnb-lambda(n, nn, (/)(1, 2 ^ (n + 1))))(k) * min(1, ((dists(s))(k))(u_, v_))))))
- `c-metric-carrier` — forall([s, w], pts(c-metric-w(s, w)) == pts(s))
- `c-metric-dist-converges` — forall([s], is-c-metric-space(s) implies forall([w], summable-weight(w) implies forall([u_, v_], u_ in pts(s) implies v_ in pts(s) implies series-converges-to(vnb-lambda(k, nn, w(k) * min(1, ((dists(s))(k))(u_, v_))), (dist(c-metric-w(s, w)))(u_, v_)))))
- `c-metric-dist-real` — forall([s], is-c-metric-space(s) implies forall([k_ in nn, u_ in pts(s), v_ in pts(s)], ((dists(s))(k_))(u_, v_) in rr))
- `c-metric-distance` — forall([s, w, u_, v_], u_ in pts(s) implies v_ in pts(s) implies (dist(c-metric-w(s, w)))(u_, v_) == series-limit(vnb-lambda(k, nn, w(k) * min(1, ((dists(s))(k))(u_, v_)))))
- `c-metric-pseudometric-at` — forall([s], is-c-metric-space(s) implies forall([k_ in nn], is-pseudometric((dists(s))(k_), pts(s))))
- `c-metric-summable` — forall([s], is-c-metric-space(s) implies forall([w], summable-weight(w) implies forall([u_, v_], u_ in pts(s) implies v_ in pts(s) implies series-converges(vnb-lambda(k, nn, w(k) * min(1, ((dists(s))(k))(u_, v_)))))))
- `c-metric-term-bound` — forall([s], is-c-metric-space(s) implies forall([k_, u_, v_, wk], k_ in nn implies u_ in pts(s) implies v_ in pts(s) implies wk in rr implies 0 <= wk implies wk * min(1, ((dists(s))(k_))(u_, v_)) in rr and 0 <= wk * min(1, ((dists(s))(k_))(u_, v_)) and wk * min(1, ((dists(s))(k_))(u_, v_)) <= wk))
- `c-metric-term-seq-in-fun` — forall([s], is-c-metric-space(s) implies forall([w], summable-weight(w) implies forall([u_, v_], u_ in pts(s) implies v_ in pts(s) implies vnb-lambda(k, nn, w(k) * min(1, ((dists(s))(k))(u_, v_))) in fun(nn, rr))))
- `caratheodory-witness-bound` — forall([h in fun(rr, rr), phi in fun(rr, rr), psi in fun(rr, rr), a in rr, b in rr, t_ in rr, m_ in rr], is-antiderivative(h, phi, a, b) implies forall([x_ in rr], x_ in ccint(a, b) implies abs(phi(x_)) <= m_) implies t_ in ccint(a, b) implies psi(t_) = phi(t_) implies forall([x_ in rr], h(x_) - h(t_) = psi(x_) * (x_ - t_)) implies forall([x_ in rr], x_ in ccint(a, b) implies abs(psi(x_)) <= m_))
- `caratheodory-witness-pair-bound` — forall([f1 in fun(rr, rr), f2 in fun(rr, rr), p1 in fun(rr, rr), p2 in fun(rr, rr), w1 in fun(rr, rr), w2 in fun(rr, rr), a in rr, b in rr, t_ in rr, m_ in rr], is-antiderivative(f1, p1, a, b) implies is-antiderivative(f2, p2, a, b) implies forall([x_ in rr], x_ in ccint(a, b) implies abs(p1(x_) - p2(x_)) <= m_) implies t_ in ccint(a, b) implies w1(t_) = p1(t_) implies w2(t_) = p2(t_) implies forall([x_ in rr], f1(x_) - f1(t_) = w1(x_) * (x_ - t_)) implies forall([x_ in rr], f2(x_) - f2(t_) = w2(x_) * (x_ - t_)) implies forall([x_ in rr], x_ in ccint(a, b) implies abs(w1(x_) - w2(x_)) <= m_))
- `card-empty-le` — forall([x_], x_ = empty-set implies card(x_) <= 0)
- `card-pair` — forall([a_ in set, b_ in set], not(a_ = b_) implies card({a_, b_}) = 2)
- `card-star-bij` — forall([n_ in nn, a_, ph, et], ph in bijection(a_, ord-segment(n_)) and et in bijection(ord-segment(n_), a_) implies card-star(a_) = n_)
- `card-star-empty` — card-star(empty-set) = 0
- `card-star-from-body` — forall([a_, al], al in ord and forsome([phi], phi in bijection(a_, ord-segment(al))) and forall([beta], ord-lt(beta, al) implies not(forsome([psi], psi in bijection(a_, ord-segment(beta))))) implies card-star(a_) = al)
- `card-star-segment` — forall([n_ in nn], card-star(ord-segment(n_)) = n_)
- `card-subset-mono` — forall([b_ in set], card(b_) in nn implies forall([a_], a_ subset b_ implies card(a_) <= card(b_)))
- `card-union-bound` — forall([a_ in set], card(a_) in nn implies forall([b_ in set], card(b_) in nn implies card(union(a_, b_)) <= card(a_) + card(b_)))
- `card-union-nn` — forall([a_ in set], card(a_) in nn implies forall([b_ in set], card(b_) in nn implies card(union(a_, b_)) in nn))
- `card-union-singleton-bound` — forall([s_ in set, y_ in set], card(s_) in nn implies card(union(pair(y_, y_), s_)) in nn and card(union(pair(y_, y_), s_)) <= succ(card(s_)))
- `card-union-singleton-nn` — forall([s_ in set, y_ in set], card(s_) in nn implies card(union(pair(y_, y_), s_)) in nn)
- `cartesian-nth` — forall([x_, aa, bb], x_ in cartesian(aa, bb) implies nth(1, x_) in aa and nth(2, x_) in bb)
- `cc-abs-im-le-magnitude` — forall([z_ in cc], abs(imag-part(z_)) <= magnitude(z_))
- `cc-abs-re-le-magnitude` — forall([z_ in cc], abs(real-part(z_)) <= magnitude(z_))
- `cc-cauchy-of-dominated` — forall([f in fun(nn, cc), p in fun(nn, rr)], is-cauchy-seq(cc-ms, f) implies forall([m_ in nn, n_ in nn], abs(p(m_) - p(n_)) <= magnitude(f(m_) - f(n_))) implies is-cauchy-seq(rr-ms, p))
- `cc-complete` — is-complete(cc-ms)
- `cc-conjugate-neg` — forall([a in cc], conjugate(-a) = -conjugate(a))
- `cc-converges-magnitude-null` — forall([s_ in fun(nn, cc), lv in cc, m in fun(nn, rr)], converges-to(cc-ms, s_, lv) implies forall([j_ in nn], m(j_) = magnitude(s_(j_) - lv)) implies converges-to(rr-ms, m, 0))
- `cc-converges-of-coords` — forall([f in fun(nn, cc), g in fun(nn, rr), q_ in fun(nn, rr), lv in cc], forall([n_ in nn], real-part(f(n_)) = g(n_)) implies forall([n_ in nn], imag-part(f(n_)) = q_(n_)) implies converges-to(rr-ms, g, real-part(lv)) implies converges-to(rr-ms, q_, imag-part(lv)) implies converges-to(cc-ms, f, lv))
- `cc-im-sub` — forall([z_, w_], z_ in cc implies w_ in cc implies imag-part(z_ - w_) = imag-part(z_) - imag-part(w_))
- `cc-is-metric-space` — is-metric-space(cc-ms)
- `cc-limit-magnitude-le` — forall([s_ in fun(nn, cc), lv in cc, bd in rr], converges-to(cc-ms, s_, lv) implies forall([n_ in nn], magnitude(s_(n_)) <= bd) implies magnitude(lv) <= bd)
- `cc-magnitude-closed` — forall([z in cc], magnitude(z) in rr)
- `cc-magnitude-le-add` — forall([p_ in cc, q_ in cc], magnitude(p_) <= magnitude(p_ - q_) + magnitude(q_))
- `cc-magnitude-le-re-im` — forall([z_ in cc], magnitude(z_) <= abs(real-part(z_)) + abs(imag-part(z_)))
- `cc-magnitude-mul` — forall([a, b], a in cc and b in cc implies magnitude(a * b) = magnitude(a) * magnitude(b))
- `cc-magnitude-neg` — forall([z in cc], magnitude(-z) = magnitude(z))
- `cc-magnitude-nonneg` — forall([z in cc], 0 <= magnitude(z))
- `cc-magnitude-sq-add-le` — forall([p_ in cc, q_ in cc], magnitude(p_ + q_) * magnitude(p_ + q_) <= (magnitude(p_) * magnitude(p_) + magnitude(p_) * magnitude(p_)) + (magnitude(q_) * magnitude(q_) + magnitude(q_) * magnitude(q_)))
- `cc-magnitude-sub-sym` — forall([p_ in cc, q_ in cc], magnitude(p_ - q_) = magnitude(q_ - p_))
- `cc-magnitude-sub-triangle` — forall([p_ in cc, q_ in cc], magnitude(p_ - q_) <= magnitude(p_) + magnitude(q_))
- `cc-magnitude-triangle` — forall([a, b], a in cc and b in cc implies magnitude(a + b) <= magnitude(a) + magnitude(b))
- `cc-magnitude-zero-iff` — forall([z in cc], magnitude(z) = 0 iff z = 0)
- `cc-ms-dist` — forall([u_, v_], u_ in cc implies v_ in cc implies (dist(cc-ms))(u_, v_) == magnitude(u_ - v_))
- `cc-plus-conj-is-2re` — forall([z in cc], z + conjugate(z) = 2 * real-part(z))
- `cc-re-im-decompose` — forall([z in cc], z = real-part(z) + 1i * imag-part(z))
- `cc-re-im-of` — forall([z in cc, a in rr, b in rr], z = a + b * 1i implies real-part(z) = a and imag-part(z) = b)
- `cc-re-sq-le-mod-sq` — forall([z in cc], real-part(z) * real-part(z) <= z * conjugate(z))
- `cc-re-sub` — forall([z_, w_], z_ in cc implies w_ in cc implies real-part(z_ - w_) = real-part(z_) - real-part(w_))
- `cc-sub-in-cc` — forall([a, b], a in cc implies b in cc implies a - b in cc)
- `cc-sub-zero-eq` — forall([a, b], a in cc implies b in cc implies a - b = 0 implies a = b)
- `ccint-affine-in` — forall([a, b, t_], a in rr and b in rr and a < b implies t_ in ccint(0, 1) implies a + (b - a) * t_ in ccint(a, b))
- `ccint-affine-inverse` — forall([a, b, x_], a in rr and b in rr and a < b and x_ in rr implies a + (b - a) * ((x_ - a) * recip(b - a)) = x_)
- `ccint-coaffine-in` — forall([a, b, x_], a in rr and b in rr and a < b implies x_ in ccint(a, b) implies (x_ - a) * recip(b - a) in ccint(0, 1))
- `ccint-creep` — forall([a, b, g_], a in rr implies b in rr implies a <= b implies g_ subset rr implies a in g_ implies rr-upper-bound(g_, b) implies forall([t_ in ccint(a, b)], forsome([d_ in rr], 0 < d_ and (forsome([w_ in g_], t_ - d_ < w_) implies forall([y_], y_ in ccint(a, b) and y_ <= t_ + d_ implies y_ in g_)))) implies b in g_)
- `ccint-membership` — forall([a, b, x], x in ccint(a, b) iff x in rr and a <= x and x <= b)
- `ccint-parts` — forall([lo_, hi_, pt_ in ccint(lo_, hi_)], pt_ in rr and lo_ <= pt_ and pt_ <= hi_)
- `ccint-subset-rr` — forall([a, b], ccint(a, b) subset rr)
- `cd-body-unique` — forall([a_, al, be], al in ord and forsome([phi], phi in bijection(a_, ord-segment(al))) and forall([beta], ord-lt(beta, al) implies not(forsome([psi], psi in bijection(a_, ord-segment(beta))))) implies be in ord and forsome([phi], phi in bijection(a_, ord-segment(be))) and forall([beta], ord-lt(beta, be) implies not(forsome([psi], psi in bijection(a_, ord-segment(beta))))) implies al = be)
- `cd-seg-body` — forall([n_ in nn], n_ in ord and forsome([phi], phi in bijection(ord-segment(n_), ord-segment(n_))) and forall([beta], ord-lt(beta, n_) implies not(forsome([psi], psi in bijection(ord-segment(n_), ord-segment(beta))))))
- `change-of-variable-antiderivable` — forall([phi, amap, ader, p_, q_], amap in fun(rr, rr) implies ader in fun(rr, rr) implies p_ in rr implies q_ in rr implies p_ < q_ implies is-antiderivable(phi, amap(p_), amap(q_)) implies forall([u_ in ccint(p_, q_)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, amap, u_)) implies forall([w_], w_ in rr and p_ < w_ and w_ < q_ implies is-diff-at(amap, w_, ader(w_))) implies forall([u_ in ccint(p_, q_)], amap(u_) in ccint(amap(p_), amap(q_))) implies forall([w_], w_ in rr and p_ < w_ and w_ < q_ implies amap(p_) < amap(w_) and amap(w_) < amap(q_)) implies is-antiderivable(vnb-lambda(x, rr, phi(amap(x)) * ader(x)), p_, q_))
- `change-of-variable-antiderivative` — forall([fw, phi, amap, ader, p_, q_], amap in fun(rr, rr) implies ader in fun(rr, rr) implies p_ in rr implies q_ in rr implies p_ < q_ implies is-antiderivative(fw, phi, amap(p_), amap(q_)) implies forall([u_ in ccint(p_, q_)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, amap, u_)) implies forall([w_], w_ in rr and p_ < w_ and w_ < q_ implies is-diff-at(amap, w_, ader(w_))) implies forall([u_ in ccint(p_, q_)], amap(u_) in ccint(amap(p_), amap(q_))) implies forall([w_], w_ in rr and p_ < w_ and w_ < q_ implies amap(p_) < amap(w_) and amap(w_) < amap(q_)) implies is-antiderivative(compose(fw, amap), vnb-lambda(x, rr, phi(amap(x)) * ader(x)), p_, q_))
- `chosen-centre-is-centre` — forall([s, r, u in ball-cover(s, r)], choice(centres(s, u, r)) in pts(s) and ball(s, choice(centres(s, u, r)), r) = u)  _[warrant: proof]_
- `cips-expand-sum` — forall([v], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([x_ in vec(v), y_ in vec(v)], (ip(v))((vadd(v))(x_, y_), (vadd(v))(x_, y_)) = ((ip(v))(x_, x_) + (ip(v))(x_, y_)) + (conjugate((ip(v))(x_, y_)) + (ip(v))(y_, y_))))
- `cips-expand-two` — forall([v, a_, b_, x_, y_], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies a_ in cc implies b_ in cc implies x_ in vec(v) implies y_ in vec(v) implies (ip(v))((vadd(v))((act(v))(a_, x_), (act(v))(b_, y_)), (vadd(v))((act(v))(a_, x_), (act(v))(b_, y_))) = ((a_ * conjugate(a_)) * (ip(v))(x_, x_) + (a_ * conjugate(b_)) * (ip(v))(x_, y_)) + ((b_ * conjugate(a_)) * conjugate((ip(v))(x_, y_)) + (b_ * conjugate(b_)) * (ip(v))(y_, y_)))
- `cips-ip-add-right` — forall([v], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([x_ in vec(v), y_ in vec(v), z_ in vec(v)], (ip(v))(x_, (vadd(v))(y_, z_)) = (ip(v))(x_, y_) + (ip(v))(x_, z_)))
- `cips-ip-conj-homog-right` — forall([v], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([a_ in cc, x_ in vec(v), y_ in vec(v)], (ip(v))(x_, (act(v))(a_, y_)) = conjugate(a_) * (ip(v))(x_, y_)))
- `cips-ip-zero-right` — forall([v], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([x_ in vec(v)], (ip(v))(x_, vzero(v)) = 0))
- `cips-minkowski` — forall([v, x_, y_], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies x_ in vec(v) implies y_ in vec(v) implies ip-norm(v, (vadd(v))(x_, y_)) <= ip-norm(v, x_) + ip-norm(v, y_))
- `cips-schwarz` — forall([v, x_, y_], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies x_ in vec(v) implies y_ in vec(v) implies (ip(v))(x_, y_) * conjugate((ip(v))(x_, y_)) <= (ip(v))(x_, x_) * (ip(v))(y_, y_))
- `clamp-compose-continuous-at` — forall([f in fun(rr, rr), a in rr, b in rr, t_ in rr], a <= b implies forall([x_ in rr], x_ in ccint(a, b) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x_)) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(z_, rr, f(clamp(a, b, z_))), t_))
- `clamp-compose-fixes` — forall([f in fun(rr, rr), a in rr, b in rr, x in rr], a <= x implies x <= b implies (vnb-lambda(z_, rr, f(clamp(a, b, z_))))(x) = f(x))
- `clamp-compose-lam-in-fun` — forall([f in fun(rr, rr), a in rr, b in rr], vnb-lambda(z_, rr, f(clamp(a, b, z_))) in fun(rr, rr))
- `clamp-fixes` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, x in rr], a <= x implies x <= b implies clamp(a, b, x) = x)
- `clamp-in-ccint` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, x in rr], a <= b implies clamp(a, b, x) in ccint(a, b))
- `clamp-in-rr` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, x in rr], clamp(a, b, x) in rr)
- `clamp-lipschitz` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, y in rr, x in rr], abs(clamp(a, b, y) - clamp(a, b, x)) <= abs(y - x))
- `class-min-pivot` — forall([a], is-euclidean-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, p in mat(m, n, carr(a))], 1 in interval(1, m) implies 1 in interval(1, n) implies forsome([i0, j0], i0 in interval(1, m) and j0 in interval(1, n) and not(entry(p, i0, j0) = zero(a))) implies forsome([b], mat-equiv(a, m, n, p, b) and not(entry(b, 1, 1) = zero(a)) and forall([c], mat-equiv(a, m, n, p, c) implies forall([i, j], i in interval(1, m) implies j in interval(1, n) implies not(entry(c, i, j) = zero(a)) implies (gauge(a))(entry(b, 1, 1)) <= (gauge(a))(entry(c, i, j)))))))
- `classmin-transport` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, p, q], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies mat-equiv(a, m, n, p, q) implies entry(q, 1, 1) = entry(p, 1, 1) implies forall([c], mat-equiv(a, m, n, p, c) implies forall([ii, jj], ii in interval(1, m) implies jj in interval(1, n) implies not(entry(c, ii, jj) = zero(a)) implies (gauge(a))(entry(p, 1, 1)) <= (gauge(a))(entry(c, ii, jj)))) implies forall([c], mat-equiv(a, m, n, q, c) implies forall([ii, jj], ii in interval(1, m) implies jj in interval(1, n) implies not(entry(c, ii, jj) = zero(a)) implies (gauge(a))(entry(q, 1, 1)) <= (gauge(a))(entry(c, ii, jj))))))
- `clear-col-upto` — forall([a], is-euclidean-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, p in mat(m, n, carr(a))], 1 in interval(1, m) implies 1 in interval(1, n) implies not(entry(p, 1, 1) = zero(a)) implies forall([c], mat-equiv(a, m, n, p, c) implies forall([ii, jj], ii in interval(1, m) implies jj in interval(1, n) implies not(entry(c, ii, jj) = zero(a)) implies (gauge(a))(entry(p, 1, 1)) <= (gauge(a))(entry(c, ii, jj)))) implies forall([k in nn], forsome([q], mat-equiv(a, m, n, p, q) and entry(q, 1, 1) = entry(p, 1, 1) and forall([i in interval(1, m)], not(i = 1) implies i <= k implies entry(q, i, 1) = zero(a)) and forall([c in interval(1, n)], entry(q, 1, c) = entry(p, 1, c))))))
- `clear-first-col` — forall([a], is-euclidean-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, p in mat(m, n, carr(a))], m in nn implies n in nn implies 1 in interval(1, m) implies 1 in interval(1, n) implies not(entry(p, 1, 1) = zero(a)) implies forall([c], mat-equiv(a, m, n, p, c) implies forall([ii, jj], ii in interval(1, m) implies jj in interval(1, n) implies not(entry(c, ii, jj) = zero(a)) implies (gauge(a))(entry(p, 1, 1)) <= (gauge(a))(entry(c, ii, jj)))) implies forsome([q], mat-equiv(a, m, n, p, q) and entry(q, 1, 1) = entry(p, 1, 1) and forall([i in interval(1, m)], not(i = 1) implies entry(q, i, 1) = zero(a)) and forall([c in interval(1, n)], entry(q, 1, c) = entry(p, 1, c)))))
- `clear-first-row` — forall([a], is-euclidean-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, p in mat(m, n, carr(a))], m in nn implies n in nn implies 1 in interval(1, m) implies 1 in interval(1, n) implies not(entry(p, 1, 1) = zero(a)) implies forall([c], mat-equiv(a, m, n, p, c) implies forall([ii, jj], ii in interval(1, m) implies jj in interval(1, n) implies not(entry(c, ii, jj) = zero(a)) implies (gauge(a))(entry(p, 1, 1)) <= (gauge(a))(entry(c, ii, jj)))) implies forsome([q], mat-equiv(a, m, n, p, q) and entry(q, 1, 1) = entry(p, 1, 1) and forall([j in interval(1, n)], not(j = 1) implies entry(q, 1, j) = zero(a)))))
- `clear-pivot-cross` — forall([a], is-euclidean-ring(a) implies forall([p, q, m], p in nn implies q in nn implies m in mat(succ(p), succ(q), carr(a)) implies forsome([i0, j0], i0 in interval(1, succ(p)) and j0 in interval(1, succ(q)) and not(entry(m, i0, j0) = zero(a))) implies forsome([c2], mat-equiv(a, succ(p), succ(q), m, c2) and c2 = border(a, entry(c2, 1, 1), submat(c2, p, q), p, q) and not(entry(c2, 1, 1) = zero(a)))))
- `clear-row-upto` — forall([a], is-euclidean-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, p in mat(m, n, carr(a))], 1 in interval(1, m) implies 1 in interval(1, n) implies not(entry(p, 1, 1) = zero(a)) implies forall([c], mat-equiv(a, m, n, p, c) implies forall([ii, jj], ii in interval(1, m) implies jj in interval(1, n) implies not(entry(c, ii, jj) = zero(a)) implies (gauge(a))(entry(p, 1, 1)) <= (gauge(a))(entry(c, ii, jj)))) implies forall([k in nn], forsome([q], mat-equiv(a, m, n, p, q) and entry(q, 1, 1) = entry(p, 1, 1) and forall([j in interval(1, n)], not(j = 1) implies j <= k implies entry(q, 1, j) = zero(a))))))
- `collapse-at-hi` — forall([k_ in nn, j_ in nn], k_ < j_ implies (collapse-at(k_))(j_) = pred(j_))
- `collapse-at-in-nn` — forall([k_ in nn, j_ in nn], (collapse-at(k_))(j_) in nn)
- `collapse-at-in-seg` — forall([n_, k_, j_], n_ in nn implies k_ in nn implies j_ in nn implies k_ in ord-segment(succ(n_)) implies j_ in ord-segment(succ(n_)) implies not(j_ = k_) implies (collapse-at(k_))(j_) in ord-segment(n_))
- `collapse-at-inj` — forall([k_, a_, b_], k_ in nn implies a_ in nn implies b_ in nn implies not(a_ = k_) implies not(b_ = k_) implies (collapse-at(k_))(a_) = (collapse-at(k_))(b_) implies a_ = b_)
- `collapse-at-lo` — forall([k_ in nn, j_ in nn], not(k_ < j_) implies (collapse-at(k_))(j_) = j_)
- `collapse-at-lt` — forall([n_, k_, j_], n_ in nn implies k_ in nn implies j_ in nn implies k_ <= n_ implies j_ <= n_ implies not(j_ = k_) implies (collapse-at(k_))(j_) < n_)
- `comm-monoid-is-monoid` — forall([s], is-comm-monoid(s) implies is-monoid(s))
- `commutative-ring-additive-ag-functorial` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-commutative-ring(a, b, f) implies is-hom-abelian-group(commutative-ring-additive-ag(a), commutative-ring-additive-ag(b), f))
- `commutative-ring-is-ring` — forall([s], is-commutative-ring(s) implies is-ring(s))
- `commutative-ring-multiplicative-cm-functorial` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-commutative-ring(a, b, f) implies is-hom-comm-monoid(commutative-ring-multiplicative-cm(a), commutative-ring-multiplicative-cm(b), f))
- `compact-countable-product` — forall([ms], is-ms-sequence(ms) implies forall([n in nn], is-compact(ms(n))) implies is-compact(product-metric(ms)))
- `compact-implies-complete` — forall([s], is-compact(s) implies is-complete(s))
- `compact-implies-totally-bounded` — forall([s], is-compact(s) implies totally-bounded(s))
- `compact-metric-is-separable` — forall([s], is-compact(s) implies forsome([x], x in pts(s)) implies is-separable(s))
- `comparison-test` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr), g in fun(nn, rr)], forall([n in nn], 0 <= f(n) and f(n) <= g(n)) and series-converges(g) implies series-converges(f))
- `complement-in-subset` — forall([a_, b_], complement-in(b_, a_) subset b_)
- `complex-inner-product-space-as-module-functorial` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-complex-inner-product-space(a, b, f) implies is-hom-module(complex-inner-product-space-as-module(a), complex-inner-product-space-as-module(b), f))
- `compose-apply` — forall([a, b, c, f, g], g in fun(a, b) and f in fun(b, c) implies forall([x in a], (compose(f, g))(x) = f(g(x))))
- `compose-continuous-at` — forall([g, f, a], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, a) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, g, f(a)) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, compose(g, f), a))
- `compose-type` — forall([a, b, c, f, g], a in set implies g in fun(a, b) and f in fun(b, c) implies compose(f, g) in fun(a, c))
- `const-continuous-at` — forall([c in rr, a in rr], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(x, rr, c), a))
- `const-lam-in-fun` — forall([c in rr], vnb-lambda(x, rr, c) in fun(rr, rr))
- `cont-transfer-ptwise-eq` — forall([f, g, a], f in fun(rr, rr) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, g, a) implies forall([x in rr], f(x) = g(x)) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, a))
- `continuous-abs-bounded-on-ccint` — forall([f, a, b], f in fun(rr, rr) implies a in rr implies b in rr implies a <= b implies forall([x in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x)) implies forsome([m_ in rr], 0 <= m_ and forall([z_ in ccint(a, b)], abs(f(z_)) <= m_)))
- `continuous-at-iff-sequential` — forall([s, t, f, a], is-metric-space(s) implies is-metric-space(t) implies f in fun(pts(s), pts(t)) implies a in pts(s) implies is-continuous-at(s, t, f, a) iff forall([sq in sqn(pts(s))], converges-to(s, sq, a) implies converges-to(t, compose(f, sq), f(a))))
- `continuous-at-implies-sequential` — forall([s, t, f, a], is-metric-space(s) implies is-metric-space(t) implies f in fun(pts(s), pts(t)) implies a in pts(s) implies is-continuous-at(s, t, f, a) implies forall([sq in sqn(pts(s))], converges-to(s, sq, a) implies converges-to(t, compose(f, sq), f(a))))
- `continuous-at-local` — forall([f in fun(rr, rr), g in fun(rr, rr), t_ in rr, rho in rr], 0 < rho implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, g, t_) implies forall([x_ in rr], abs(x_ - t_) <= rho implies f(x_) = g(x_)) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, t_))
- `continuous-bounded-above-on-ccint` — forall([f, a, b], f in fun(rr, rr) implies a in rr implies b in rr implies a <= b implies forall([x in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x)) implies forsome([k_ in rr], forall([z_ in ccint(a, b)], f(z_) <= k_)))
- `continuous-is-antiderivable` — forall([f, a, b], f in fun(rr, rr) implies a in rr implies b in rr implies a < b implies forall([x_ in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x_)) implies is-antiderivable(f, a, b))
- `continuous-uniformly-continuous-on-ccint` — forall([f, a, b, eps], f in fun(rr, rr) implies a in rr implies b in rr implies a <= b implies pos-rr(eps) implies forall([x in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x)) implies forsome([d_ in rr], 0 < d_ and forall([p_ in ccint(a, b), q_ in ccint(a, b)], abs(p_ - q_) <= d_ implies abs(f(p_) - f(q_)) <= eps)))
- `converges-dist-null-bwd` — forall([t], is-metric-space(t) implies forall([f in fun(nn, pts(t)), lv in pts(t)], converges-to(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(j_, nn, (dist(t))(f(j_), lv)), 0) implies converges-to(t, f, lv)))
- `converges-dist-null-fwd` — forall([t], is-metric-space(t) implies forall([f in fun(nn, pts(t)), lv in pts(t)], converges-to(t, f, lv) implies converges-to(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(j_, nn, (dist(t))(f(j_), lv)), 0)))
- `converges-iff-dist-null` — forall([t], is-metric-space(t) implies forall([f in fun(nn, pts(t)), lv in pts(t)], converges-to(t, f, lv) iff converges-to(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(j_, nn, (dist(t))(f(j_), lv)), 0)))
- `converges-to-transfer` — forall([t], is-metric-space(t) implies forall([f in fun(nn, pts(t)), h in fun(nn, pts(t)), lv in pts(t)], converges-to(t, f, lv) implies forall([j_ in nn], h(j_) = f(j_)) implies converges-to(t, h, lv)))
- `coord-block-estimate` — forall([s, g, b, p, delta, m0], converges-along(s, g, b, p) implies strictly-mono-nn(delta) implies m0 in nn implies forall([j in nn], m0 <= j implies delta(j) in b) implies converges-to(s, subseq(g, delta), p))
- `deg-add-le` — forall([a_, f_, g_, n_], is-ring(a_) implies f_ in carr(poly(a_)) implies g_ in carr(poly(a_)) implies n_ in nn implies deg(a_, f_) <= n_ implies deg(a_, g_) <= n_ implies deg(a_, monalg-add(a_, nn-add-monoid, f_, g_)) <= n_)
- `deg-bound-apply` — forall([a_, p_, n_, k_], deg-bound(a_, p_, n_) implies k_ in nn implies n_ < k_ implies p_(k_) = zero(a_))
- `deg-is` — forall([a_, p_, m_], p_ in carr(poly(a_)) implies m_ in nn implies deg-bound(a_, p_, m_) implies forall([j_ in nn], deg-bound(a_, p_, j_) implies m_ <= j_) implies deg(a_, p_) = m_)
- `deg-unfold` — forall([a_, p_], deg(a_, p_) == iota(n_, n_ in nn and deg-bound(a_, p_, n_) and forall([j_ in nn], deg-bound(a_, p_, j_) implies n_ <= j_)))
- `deg-well-defined` — forall([a_, p_ in carr(poly(a_))], deg(a_, p_) in nn and deg-bound(a_, p_, deg(a_, p_)) and forall([j_ in nn], deg-bound(a_, p_, j_) implies deg(a_, p_) <= j_))
- `deriv-affine` — forall([c, lam, a], c in rr and lam in rr and a in rr implies is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(x, rr, c + lam * x), a, lam))
- `deriv-anti-monomial` — forall([c in rr, m in nn, pt in rr], is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(x, rr, (recip(succ(m)) * c) * x ^ succ(m)), pt, c * pt ^ m))
- `deriv-chain` — forall([f, g, a, l, m], is-diff-at(f, a, l) implies is-diff-at(g, f(a), m) implies is-diff-at(compose(g, f), a, m * l))
- `deriv-chain-value` — forall([f, g, a, dl, dm], is-diff-at(f, a, dl) implies is-diff-at(g, f(a), dm) implies deriv(compose(g, f), a) = deriv(g, f(a)) * deriv(f, a))
- `deriv-coef-monomial` — forall([c in rr, dg in nn, pt in rr], is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(x, rr, c * x ^ succ(dg)), pt, (succ(dg) * c) * pt ^ dg))
- `deriv-const` — forall([c, a], c in rr and a in rr implies is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(x, rr, c), a, 0))
- `deriv-identity` — forall([a in rr], is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(x, rr, x), a, 1))
- `deriv-inverse` — forall([f, g, a, l], is-diff-at(f, a, l) implies not(l = 0) implies g in fun(rr, rr) implies forall([x in rr], g(f(x)) = x) implies forall([y in rr], f(g(y)) = y) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, g, f(a)) implies is-diff-at(g, f(a), recip(l)))
- `deriv-of-is-diff-at` — forall([f, a, dv], is-diff-at(f, a, dv) implies deriv(f, a) = dv)
- `deriv-polynomial` — forall([n in nn, cf in fun(nn, rr), pt in rr], is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(x, rr, series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(k, nn, cf(k) * x ^ k), succ(n))), pt, series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(j, nn, (succ(j) * cf(succ(j))) * pt ^ j), n)))
- `deriv-pos-strictly-increasing` — forall([f, a, b], f in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and b in rr and a < b implies forall([x in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x)) implies forall([x], x in rr and a < x and x < b implies forsome([l], is-diff-at(f, x, l) and 0 < l)) implies forall([u, v], u in ccint(a, b) and v in ccint(a, b) and u < v implies f(u) < f(v)))
- `deriv-power` — forall([n in nn, a in rr], is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(x, rr, x ^ succ(n)), a, succ(n) * a ^ n))
- `deriv-product` — forall([f, g, a, l, m], is-diff-at(f, a, l) and is-diff-at(g, a, m) implies is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(x, rr, f(x) * g(x)), a, l * g(a) + f(a) * m))
- `deriv-right-inverse` — forall([fm_, gm_, am_, lv_], is-diff-at(fm_, am_, lv_) implies not(lv_ = 0) implies gm_ in fun(rr, rr) implies forall([z_ in rr], fm_(gm_(z_)) = z_) implies gm_(fm_(am_)) = am_ implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, gm_, fm_(am_)) implies is-diff-at(gm_, fm_(am_), recip(lv_)))
- `deriv-scalar-mult` — forall([c in rr, f, pt, dl], is-diff-at(f, pt, dl) implies is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(x, rr, c * f(x)), pt, c * dl))
- `deriv-sub` — forall([f, g, a, l, m], is-diff-at(f, a, l) and is-diff-at(g, a, m) implies is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(x, rr, f(x) - g(x)), a, l - m))
- `deriv-sum` — forall([f, g, a, l, m], is-diff-at(f, a, l) and is-diff-at(g, a, m) implies is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(x, rr, f(x) + g(x)), a, l + m))
- `deriv-zero-const-up` — forall([f, a, b], f in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and b in rr and a < b implies forall([x in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x)) implies forall([x], x in rr and a < x and x < b implies is-diff-at(f, x, 0)) implies forall([u, v], u in ccint(a, b) and v in ccint(a, b) and u < v implies f(u) = f(v)))
- `deriv-zero-implies-constant` — forall([f, a, b], f in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and b in rr and a < b implies forall([x in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x)) implies forall([x], x in rr and a < x and x < b implies is-diff-at(f, x, 0)) implies forall([u, v], u in ccint(a, b) and v in ccint(a, b) implies f(u) = f(v)))
- `derivative-unique` — forall([f, a, l, m], is-diff-at(f, a, l) and is-diff-at(f, a, m) implies l = m)
- `descent-remainder` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies is-commutative-ring(scal(md)) implies forall([n in nn, u in mat(succ(n), 1, vec(md)), c in mat(1, succ(n), carr(scal(md))), c0 in mat(1, succ(n), carr(scal(md))), q in carr(scal(md)), b in carr(scal(md))], entry(c0, 1, succ(n)) = b implies entry(c, 1, succ(n)) = (mul(scal(md)))(q, b) implies forsome([y in span(md, n, block(u, n, 1))], entry(matact(md, c, u), 1, 1) = (vadd(md))(y, (act(md))(q, entry(matact(md, c0, u), 1, 1))) and y = (vadd(md))(entry(matact(md, c, u), 1, 1), (act(md))((neg(scal(md)))(q), entry(matact(md, c0, u), 1, 1))))))
- `diagonalization` — forall([s in fun(nn, inf-subsets(nn))], forall([k in nn], s(succ(k)) subset s(k)) implies forsome([f], strictly-mono-nn(f) and forall([k in nn, j in nn], k <= j implies f(j) in s(k))))
- `diff-at-in-fun` — forall([f, a, dl], is-diff-at(f, a, dl) implies f in fun(rr, rr))
- `diff-at-local` — forall([f in fun(rr, rr), psi in fun(rr, rr), t_ in rr, lv in rr, rho in rr], 0 < rho implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, psi, t_) implies psi(t_) = lv implies forall([x_ in rr], abs(x_ - t_) <= rho implies f(x_) - f(t_) = psi(x_) * (x_ - t_)) implies is-diff-at(f, t_, lv))
- `diff-at-ptwise-limit` — forall([fam in fun(nn, fun(rr, rr)), phifam in fun(nn, fun(rr, rr)), f in fun(rr, rr), phi in fun(rr, rr), t_ in rr, rho in rr], 0 < rho implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, phi, t_) implies forall([k in nn, y_ in rr], abs(y_ - t_) <= rho implies (fam(k))(y_) - (fam(k))(t_) = (phifam(k))(y_) * (y_ - t_)) implies forall([y_ in rr], abs(y_ - t_) <= rho implies converges-to(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(k_, nn, (fam(k_))(y_)), f(y_))) implies forall([y_ in rr], abs(y_ - t_) <= rho implies converges-to(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(k_, nn, (phifam(k_))(y_)), phi(y_))) implies is-diff-at(f, t_, phi(t_)))
- `diff-at-value-in-rr` — forall([f, a, dl], is-diff-at(f, a, dl) implies dl in rr)
- `diff-at-witness-family` — forall([fam, lfam, t_], fam in fun(nn, fun(rr, rr)) implies lfam in fun(nn, rr) implies t_ in rr implies forall([k_ in nn], is-diff-at(fam(k_), t_, lfam(k_))) implies forsome([wfam in fun(nn, fun(rr, rr))], forall([k_ in nn], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, wfam(k_), t_) and (wfam(k_))(t_) = lfam(k_) and forall([x_ in rr], (fam(k_))(x_) - (fam(k_))(t_) = (wfam(k_))(x_) * (x_ - t_)))))
- `diff-implies-continuous` — forall([f, a, l], is-diff-at(f, a, l) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, a))
- `diff-transfer-ptwise-eq` — forall([f, g, a, dl], f in fun(rr, rr) implies is-diff-at(g, a, dl) implies forall([x_ in rr], f(x_) == g(x_)) implies is-diff-at(f, a, dl))
- `difference-membership` — forall([u, b, x], x in difference(u, b) iff x in u and not(x in b))
- `difference-set` — forall([x in set, b], difference(x, b) in set)
- `dir-deriv-homogeneous` — forall([m, f, a, eta, lam, dl], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies f in fun(vec(m), rr) implies a in vec(m) implies eta in vec(m) implies lam in rr implies is-dir-diff-at(m, f, a, eta, dl) implies is-dir-diff-at(m, f, a, (act(m))(lam, eta), lam * dl))
- `dir-deriv-linear` — forall([m, f, a, eta, xi, r, s, dl, dm], is-normed-vector-space(m) and f in fun(vec(m), rr) and a in vec(m) and eta in vec(m) and xi in vec(m) and r in rr and s in rr and is-dir-diff-at(m, f, a, eta, dl) and is-dir-diff-at(m, f, a, xi, dm) implies forall([x_ in vec(m)], forsome([k_], is-dir-diff-at(m, f, x_, (act(m))(r, eta), k_))) and forall([x_ in vec(m)], forsome([k_], is-dir-diff-at(m, f, x_, (act(m))(s, xi), k_))) and is-continuous-at(nvs-metric-space(m), rr-ms, vnb-lambda(x_, vec(m), dir-deriv(m, f, x_, (act(m))(r, eta))), a) and is-continuous-at(nvs-metric-space(m), rr-ms, vnb-lambda(x_, vec(m), dir-deriv(m, f, x_, (act(m))(s, xi))), a) implies is-dir-diff-at(m, f, a, (vadd(m))((act(m))(r, eta), (act(m))(s, xi)), r * dl + s * dm))
- `dir-deriv-mvt` — forall([m, f, a, eta], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies f in fun(vec(m), rr) implies a in vec(m) implies eta in vec(m) implies forall([x in ccint(0, 1)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, seg-curve(m, f, a, eta), x)) implies forall([x], x in rr and 0 < x and x < 1 implies forsome([l], is-diff-at(seg-curve(m, f, a, eta), x, l))) implies forsome([th in rr], 0 < th and th < 1 and forsome([dv], is-dir-diff-at(m, f, (vadd(m))(a, (act(m))(th, eta)), eta, dv) and dv = f((vadd(m))(a, eta)) - f(a))))
- `dir-deriv-value` — forall([m, f, a, eta, dl], is-dir-diff-at(m, f, a, eta, dl) implies dir-deriv(m, f, a, eta) = dl)
- `dir-diff-reparam` — forall([f, g, c, lam, dl], f in fun(rr, rr) implies c in rr implies lam in rr implies is-diff-at(g, c, dl) implies forall([x_ in rr], f(x_) == g(c + lam * x_)) implies is-diff-at(f, 0, dl * lam))
- `dir-diff-scale` — forall([f, g, lam, dl], f in fun(rr, rr) implies lam in rr implies is-diff-at(g, 0, dl) implies forall([x_ in rr], f(x_) == g(lam * x_)) implies is-diff-at(f, 0, lam * dl))
- `dir-diff-shift` — forall([f, g, c, dl], f in fun(rr, rr) implies c in rr implies is-diff-at(g, c, dl) implies forall([x_ in rr], f(x_) == g(c + x_)) implies is-diff-at(f, 0, dl))
- `discrete-space-is-measurable-space` — forall([a_ in set], is-measurable-space(discrete-space(a_)))
- `discrete-space-is-top-space` — forall([a_ in set], is-top-space(discrete-space(a_)))
- `dist-seq-apply` — forall([t, f, lv, n_ in nn], (vnb-lambda(j_, nn, (dist(t))(f(j_), lv)))(n_) == (dist(t))(f(n_), lv))
- `dist-seq-in-fun` — forall([t], is-metric-space(t) implies forall([f in fun(nn, pts(t)), lv in pts(t)], vnb-lambda(j_, nn, (dist(t))(f(j_), lv)) in fun(nn, rr)))
- `dominated-null-series` — forall([g in fun(nn, rr), e in fun(nn, rr), u], forall([j_ in nn], u(j_) in fun(nn, rr)) implies forall([j_ in nn, i_ in nn], 0 <= (u(j_))(i_) and (u(j_))(i_) <= g(i_)) implies series-converges(g) implies forall([i_ in nn], converges-to(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(j_, nn, (u(j_))(i_)), 0)) implies forall([j_ in nn], series-converges-to(u(j_), e(j_))) implies converges-to(rr-ms, e, 0))
- `ds-opens` — forall([a_], opens(discrete-space(a_)) == power(a_))
- `ds-pts` — forall([a_], pts(discrete-space(a_)) == a_)
- `ds-sigma` — forall([a_], sigma(discrete-space(a_)) == power(a_))
- `dyadic-partial-sum` — forall([k in nn], series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(n, nn, (/)(1, 2 ^ (n + 1))), k) = 1 - recip(2 ^ k))
- `dyadic-weight-apply` — forall([k in nn], (vnb-lambda(n, nn, (/)(1, 2 ^ (n + 1))))(k) = recip(2 ^ succ(k)))
- `dyadic-weight-in-fun` — vnb-lambda(n, nn, (/)(1, 2 ^ (n + 1))) in fun(nn, rr)
- `dyadic-weight-pos` — forall([n_ in nn], 0 < (vnb-lambda(n, nn, (/)(1, 2 ^ (n + 1))))(n_))
- `elem-f-action` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, p, k, l], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies k in interval(1, n) implies l in interval(1, n) implies not(k = l) implies forall([i in interval(1, m), c in interval(1, n)], entry(matmul(a, p, elem-f(a, n, k, l)), i, c) = if(c = k, entry(p, i, l), if(c = l, entry(p, i, k), entry(p, i, c))))))
- `elem-f-inverse` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([n, k, l], k in interval(1, n) implies l in interval(1, n) implies not(k = l) implies matmul(a, elem-f(a, n, k, l), elem-f(a, n, l, k)) = identmat(a, n)))
- `elem-f-invertible` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([n, k, l], k in interval(1, n) implies l in interval(1, n) implies not(k = l) implies is-invertible-mat(a, n, elem-f(a, n, k, l))))
- `elem-f-row-action` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, p, k, l], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies k in interval(1, m) implies l in interval(1, m) implies not(k = l) implies forall([i in interval(1, m), c in interval(1, n)], entry(matmul(a, elem-f(a, m, k, l), p), i, c) = if(i = k, entry(p, l, c), if(i = l, entry(p, k, c), entry(p, i, c))))))
- `elem-g-action` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, p, r, k, l], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies r in carr(a) implies k in interval(1, n) implies l in interval(1, n) implies not(k = l) implies forall([i in interval(1, m), c in interval(1, n)], entry(matmul(a, p, elem-g(a, n, r, k, l)), i, c) = if(c = l, (add(a))(entry(p, i, l), (mul(a))(entry(p, i, k), r)), entry(p, i, c)))))
- `elem-g-inverse` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([n, r, k, l], r in carr(a) implies k in interval(1, n) implies l in interval(1, n) implies not(k = l) implies matmul(a, elem-g(a, n, r, k, l), elem-g(a, n, (neg(a))(r), k, l)) = identmat(a, n)))
- `elem-g-invertible` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([n, r, k, l], r in carr(a) implies k in interval(1, n) implies l in interval(1, n) implies not(k = l) implies is-invertible-mat(a, n, elem-g(a, n, r, k, l))))
- `elem-g-param-cong` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([n, r, s, k, l], s in carr(a) implies r = s implies elem-g(a, n, r, k, l) = elem-g(a, n, s, k, l)))
- `elem-g-row-action` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, p, r, k, l], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies r in carr(a) implies k in interval(1, m) implies l in interval(1, m) implies not(k = l) implies forall([i in interval(1, m), c in interval(1, n)], entry(matmul(a, elem-g(a, m, r, k, l), p), i, c) = if(i = k, (add(a))(entry(p, k, c), (mul(a))(r, entry(p, l, c))), entry(p, i, c)))))
- `elem-h-action` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, p, r, k], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies r in carr(a) implies k in interval(1, n) implies forall([i in interval(1, m), c in interval(1, n)], entry(matmul(a, p, elem-h(a, n, r, k)), i, c) = if(c = k, (mul(a))(entry(p, i, k), r), entry(p, i, c)))))
- `elem-h-inverse` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([n, r, s, k], r in carr(a) implies s in carr(a) implies (mul(a))(r, s) = one(a) implies k in interval(1, n) implies matmul(a, elem-h(a, n, r, k), elem-h(a, n, s, k)) = identmat(a, n)))
- `elem-h-row-action` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, p, r, k], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies r in carr(a) implies k in interval(1, m) implies forall([i in interval(1, m), c in interval(1, n)], entry(matmul(a, elem-h(a, m, r, k), p), i, c) = if(i = k, (mul(a))(r, entry(p, k, c)), entry(p, i, c)))))
- `ell-one-dominated-convergence` — forall([a_ in fun(nn, rr), x in sqn(cc), e in fun(nn, rr), xs], forall([j_ in nn], xs(j_) in sqn(cc)) implies series-converges(a_) implies forall([j_ in nn, k_ in nn], magnitude((xs(j_))(k_)) <= a_(k_)) implies forall([k_ in nn], converges-to(cc-ms, vnb-lambda(j_, nn, (xs(j_))(k_)), x(k_))) implies forall([j_ in nn], e(j_) = series-limit(vnb-lambda(k_, nn, magnitude((xs(j_))(k_) - x(k_))))) implies x in ell-one and converges-to(rr-ms, e, 0))
- `ell-one-membership` — forall([y_], y_ in ell-one iff y_ in sqn(cc) and series-converges(vnb-lambda(k_, nn, magnitude(y_(k_)))))
- `ell-two-add-closed` — forall([x_ in ell-two, y_ in ell-two, z_ in sqn(cc)], forall([k_ in nn], z_(k_) = x_(k_) + y_(k_)) implies z_ in ell-two)
- `ell-two-membership` — forall([y_], y_ in ell-two iff y_ in sqn(cc) and series-converges(vnb-lambda(k_, nn, magnitude(y_(k_)) * magnitude(y_(k_)))))
- `ell-two-neg-closed` — forall([x_ in ell-two, z_ in sqn(cc)], forall([k_ in nn], z_(k_) = -x_(k_)) implies z_ in ell-two)
- `ell-two-zero-in` — vnb-lambda(n_, nn, 0) in ell-two
- `empty-subset-any` — forall([b_], empty-set subset b_)
- `eq-sym` — forall([a, b], a = b implies b = a)
- `eq-trans` — forall([a, b, c], a = b implies b = c implies a = c)
- `equicont-dense-conv-implies-unif-cauchy` — forall([s, fam], is-compact(s) implies fam in fun(nn, fun(pts(s), rr)) implies is-equicontinuous(s, rr-ms, fam) implies forsome([dseq], is-dense-seq(s, dseq) and converges-on(s, fam, dseq)) implies is-unif-cauchy(s, fam))
- `equicont-dense-conv-ptwise-cauchy` — forall([s, fam in fun(nn, fun(pts(s), rr))], is-equicontinuous(s, rr-ms, fam) implies forsome([dseq], is-dense-seq(s, dseq) and converges-on(s, fam, dseq)) implies is-ptwise-cauchy(s, fam))
- `equiv-mul-both` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([m, n, a, u, v], a in mat(m, n, carr(r)) implies is-invertible-mat(r, m, u) implies is-invertible-mat(r, n, v) implies mat-equiv(r, m, n, a, matmul(r, matmul(r, u, a), v))))
- `euclidean-division` — forall([s], is-euclidean-ring(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], not(b = zero(s)) implies forsome([q in carr(s), r in carr(s)], a = (add(s))((mul(s))(q, b), r) and (r = zero(s) or succ((gauge(s))(r)) <= (gauge(s))(b)))))
- `euclidean-ideal-has-generator` — forall([s], is-euclidean-ring(s) implies forall([i], is-ideal(s, i) implies forsome([b in i], forall([a in i], a in principal-ideal(s, b)))))
- `euclidean-ring-is-integral-domain` — forall([s], is-euclidean-ring(s) implies is-integral-domain(s))
- `extreme-value-max` — forall([f, a, b], f in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and b in rr and a <= b implies forall([x in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x)) implies forsome([c in ccint(a, b)], forall([x in ccint(a, b)], f(x) <= f(c))))
- `extreme-value-min` — forall([f, a, b], f in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and b in rr and a <= b implies forall([x in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x)) implies forsome([c in ccint(a, b)], forall([x in ccint(a, b)], f(c) <= f(x))))
- `factorial-real-pos` — forall([n in nn], factorial(n) in rr and 0 < factorial(n))
- `field-additive-ag-functorial` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-field(a, b, f) implies is-hom-abelian-group(field-additive-ag(a), field-additive-ag(b), f))
- `field-as-euclidean-ring-functorial` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-field(a, b, f) implies is-hom-euclidean-ring(field-as-euclidean-ring(a), field-as-euclidean-ring(b), f))
- `field-as-integral-domain-functorial` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-field(a, b, f) implies is-hom-integral-domain(field-as-integral-domain(a), field-as-integral-domain(b), f))
- `field-id-add` — forall([f_], add(field-as-integral-domain(f_)) == add(f_))
- `field-id-carr` — forall([f_], carr(field-as-integral-domain(f_)) == carr(f_))
- `field-id-mul` — forall([f_], mul(field-as-integral-domain(f_)) == mul(f_))
- `field-id-neg` — forall([f_], neg(field-as-integral-domain(f_)) == neg(f_))
- `field-id-one` — forall([f_], one(field-as-integral-domain(f_)) == one(f_))
- `field-id-zero` — forall([f_], zero(field-as-integral-domain(f_)) == zero(f_))
- `field-is-field-ring` — forall([s_], is-field(s_) implies is-field-ring(field-as-integral-domain(s_)))
- `fin-subset-monoid-is-comm-monoid` — forall([a_ in set], is-comm-monoid(fin-subset-monoid(a_)))
- `fin-subsets-has-empty` — forall([a_], empty-set in fin-subsets(a_))
- `fin-subsets-is-set` — forall([a_ in set], fin-subsets(a_) in set)
- `fin-subsets-membership` — forall([a_, s_], s_ in fin-subsets(a_) iff s_ in set and forall([z in s_], z in a_) and card(s_) in nn)
- `fin-subsets-union-closed` — forall([a_, s_, u_], s_ in fin-subsets(a_) implies u_ in fin-subsets(a_) implies union(s_, u_) in fin-subsets(a_))
- `finite-ball-subcover-r-net` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([r], r in rr and 0 <= r and not(0 = r) implies forsome([f], f subset ball-cover(s, r) and card(f) in nn and is-open-cover(s, f)) implies forsome([n], card(n) in nn and is-r-net(s, n, pts(s), r))))  _[warrant: proof]_
- `finsum-fiber` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([t in set], card(t) in nn implies forall([phi in fun(s, t), f in fun(s, carr(ag))], finsum(ag, f, s) = finsum(ag, vnb-lambda(t_, t, finsum(ag, f, {s_ in s: phi(s_) = t_})), t)))))
- `finsupp-membership` — forall([a_, m_, f_], f_ in finsupp(a_, m_) iff f_ in fun(carr(m_), carr(a_)) and card(supp(a_, m_, f_)) in nn)
- `finsupp-unfold` — forall([a_, m_], finsupp(a_, m_) == {f_ in fun(carr(m_), carr(a_)): card(supp(a_, m_, f_)) in nn})
- `free-length-le-generators` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies is-euclidean-ring(scal(md)) implies forall([n, m, u, v], n in nn implies m in nn implies u in mat(n, 1, vec(md)) implies v in mat(m, 1, vec(md)) implies generates(md, n, u) implies rel-free(md, m, v) implies m <= n))
- `free-transport` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([m, v, qm], v in mat(m, 1, vec(md)) implies is-invertible-mat(scal(md), m, qm) implies rel-free(md, m, v) implies rel-free(md, m, matact(md, qm, v))))
- `fsm-carr` — forall([a_], carr(fin-subset-monoid(a_)) == fin-subsets(a_))
- `fsm-iden` — forall([a_], iden(fin-subset-monoid(a_)) == empty-set)
- `fsm-opr` — forall([a_], opr(fin-subset-monoid(a_)) == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(fin-subsets(a_), fin-subsets(a_)), union(x_, y_)))
- `fun-apply-type-c` — forall([f, a_, b_, x_], f in fun(a_, b_) implies x_ in a_ implies f(x_) in b_)
- `fun-codomain-subset` — forall([g_, a_, b_, c_], g_ in fun(a_, b_) implies b_ subset c_ implies g_ in fun(a_, c_))
- `fun-into-seg-zero-absurd` — forall([a, f, x], f in fun(a, ord-segment(0)) implies x in a implies falsity)
- `generalized-mvt` — forall([f, g, a, b], f in fun(rr, rr) and g in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and b in rr and a < b implies forall([x in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x) and is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, g, x)) implies forall([x], x in rr and a < x and x < b implies forsome([l], is-diff-at(f, x, l)) and forsome([m], is-diff-at(g, x, m))) implies forsome([theta in rr], a < theta and theta < b and forsome([l, m], is-diff-at(f, theta, l) and is-diff-at(g, theta, m) and l * (g(b) - g(a)) = m * (f(b) - f(a)))))
- `generates-implies-spans-vec` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([n in nn, u in mat(n, 1, vec(md))], generates(md, n, u) implies spans(md, n, u, vec(md))))
- `generates-transport` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([n, u, pm], u in mat(n, 1, vec(md)) implies is-invertible-mat(scal(md), n, pm) implies generates(md, n, u) implies generates(md, n, matact(md, pm, u))))
- `good-step` — forall([m, s, f, t, x], is-normed-vector-space(m) and is-bounded-linear-functional-on(m, s, f) and good-sub(m, s, f, t) and x in vec(m) and not(x in t) implies good-sub(m, s, f, span-add-one(m, t, x)))
- `greedy-chain-is-chain` — forall([phi, grd, porel], is-partial-order(grd, porel) implies is-chain(grd, porel, greedy-chain(phi, grd, porel)))
- `group-assoc` — forall([s], is-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s), c in carr(s)], (opr(s))((opr(s))(a, b), c) = (opr(s))(a, (opr(s))(b, c))))
- `group-cancel-left` — forall([s], is-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s), c in carr(s)], (opr(s))(c, a) = (opr(s))(c, b) implies a = b))
- `group-identity-in` — forall([s], is-group(s) implies iden(s) in carr(s))
- `group-inv-in` — forall([s], is-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], (inv(s))(a) in carr(s)))
- `group-left-id` — forall([s], is-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], (opr(s))(iden(s), a) = a))
- `group-left-inv` — forall([s], is-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], (opr(s))((inv(s))(a), a) = iden(s)))
- `hahn-banach` — forall([m, s, f], is-normed-vector-space(m) and is-finite-dimensional(normed-vector-space-as-module(m)) and is-submodule(m, s) and is-bounded-linear-functional-on(m, s, f) implies forsome([g_], is-linear-functional-on(m, vec(m), g_) and extends-on(s, g_, f) and forall([w_ in vec(m)], abs(g_(w_)) <= dual-norm-on(m, s, f) * (vnrm(m))(w_))))
- `hahn-banach-extend-one` — forall([m, s, f, v], is-normed-vector-space(m) and is-submodule(m, s) and is-bounded-linear-functional-on(m, s, f) and v in vec(m) and not(v in s) implies forsome([g_], is-linear-functional-on(m, span-add-one(m, s, v), g_) and extends-on(s, g_, f) and forall([w_ in span-add-one(m, s, v)], abs(g_(w_)) <= dual-norm-on(m, s, f) * (vnrm(m))(w_))))
- `hb-good-has-maximal` — forall([m, s, f], is-finite-dimensional(normed-vector-space-as-module(m)) implies is-submodule(m, s) implies is-bounded-linear-functional-on(m, s, f) implies forsome([t], good-sub(m, s, f, t) and forall([u], good-sub(m, s, f, u) and t subset u implies t = u)))
- `heine-borel-baby` — forall([a, b, f_], a in rr implies b in rr implies a <= b implies forall([v_ in f_], is-open(rr-ms, v_)) implies ccint(a, b) subset big-union(u_, f_, u_) implies forsome([g_], g_ subset f_ and g_ in set and card(g_) in nn and ccint(a, b) subset big-union(u_, g_, u_)))
- `ident-lam-in-fun` — vnb-lambda(x, rr, x) in fun(rr, rr)
- `identity-continuous-at` — forall([a in rr], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(x, rr, x), a))
- `identmat-invertible` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([n], is-invertible-mat(a, n, identmat(a, n))))
- `imag-part-in-rr` — forall([z in cc], imag-part(z) in rr)
- `injection-is-injective-star` — forall([x, y, f in injection(x, y)], injective-star(f))
- `integral-domain-cancel-zero` — forall([s], is-integral-domain(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], (mul(s))(a, b) = zero(s) implies not(b = zero(s)) implies a = zero(s)))
- `integral-domain-is-commutative-ring` — forall([s], is-integral-domain(s) implies is-commutative-ring(s))
- `integral-domain-nontrivial` — forall([s], is-integral-domain(s) implies not(one(s) = zero(s)))
- `interior-max-deriv-zero` — forall([f, a, b, theta, l], f in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and b in rr and theta in rr and a < theta and theta < b implies forall([x in ccint(a, b)], f(x) <= f(theta)) implies is-diff-at(f, theta, l) implies l = 0)
- `interior-min-deriv-zero` — forall([f, a, b, theta, l], f in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and b in rr and theta in rr and a < theta and theta < b implies forall([x in ccint(a, b)], f(theta) <= f(x)) implies is-diff-at(f, theta, l) implies l = 0)
- `intersection-complement-in-empty` — forall([a_, b_], intersection(a_, complement-in(b_, a_)) = empty-set)
- `interval-elt-in-nn` — forall([a, b, i in interval(a, b)], i in nn)
- `interval-hi` — forall([a, b, i in interval(a, b)], i <= b)
- `interval-in-set` — forall([a, b], interval(a, b) in set)
- `interval-lo` — forall([a, b, i in interval(a, b)], a <= i)
- `interval-unfold` — forall([a, b], {i in nn: a <= i and i <= b} == interval(a, b))
- `interval-widen` — forall([b in nn, c in nn, a, i in interval(a, b)], b <= c implies i in interval(a, c))
- `inverse-is-invertible` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([n, pm, qm], pm in mat(n, n, carr(a)) implies qm in mat(n, n, carr(a)) implies matmul(a, pm, qm) = identmat(a, n) implies matmul(a, qm, pm) = identmat(a, n) implies is-invertible-mat(a, n, qm)))
- `invertible-mat-is-mat` — forall([a, n, u], is-invertible-mat(a, n, u) implies u in mat(n, n, carr(a)))
- `is-subsequence-iff-typed-subsqn` — forall([s, y, f], is-subsequence(s, y, f) iff f in fun(nn, pts(s)) and subsqn(y, f))
- `ivt` — forall([f, a, b, c], f in fun(rr, rr) implies a in rr implies b in rr implies c in rr implies a <= b implies forall([x in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x)) implies f(a) <= c and c <= f(b) implies forsome([p_ in rr], p_ in ccint(a, b) and f(p_) = c))
- `lam-slot-add-apply` — forall([dm_, a_, b_], a_ in dm_ implies b_ in dm_ implies (vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(dm_, dm_), x_ + y_))(a_, b_) == a_ + b_)
- `lam-slot-mul-apply` — forall([dm_, a_, b_], a_ in dm_ implies b_ in dm_ implies (vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(dm_, dm_), x_ * y_))(a_, b_) == a_ * b_)
- `lam-slot-neg-apply` — forall([dm_, a_ in dm_], (vnb-lambda(x_, dm_, -x_))(a_) == -a_)
- `lastcoeff-set-is-ideal` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies is-commutative-ring(scal(md)) implies forall([p in nn, u in mat(succ(p), 1, vec(md)), sm], is-submodule(md, sm) implies is-ideal(scal(md), lastcoeff-set(md, p, u, sm))))
- `lastcoeff-zero-in-span` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([p in nn, u in mat(succ(p), 1, vec(md)), c in mat(1, succ(p), carr(scal(md)))], entry(c, 1, succ(p)) = zero(scal(md)) implies entry(matact(md, c, u), 1, 1) in span(md, p, block(u, p, 1))))
- `leadcoef-nonzero` — forall([a_, p_], is-ring(a_) implies p_ in carr(poly(a_)) implies not(p_ = zero(poly(a_))) implies not(leadcoef(a_, p_) = zero(a_)))
- `leadcoef-nonzero-ptwise` — forall([a_, p_ in carr(poly(a_))], forsome([k_ in nn], not(p_(k_) = zero(a_))) implies not(leadcoef(a_, p_) = zero(a_)))
- `leadcoef-unfold` — forall([a_, p_], leadcoef(a_, p_) == p_(deg(a_, p_)))
- `log-above-any` — forall([y_ in rr], forsome([b_ in rr], 0 < b_ and y_ <= log(b_)))
- `log-below-any` — forall([y_ in rr], forsome([a_ in rr], 0 < a_ and log(a_) <= y_))
- `log-deriv` — forall([c_ in rr], 0 < c_ implies is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(x_, rr, log(x_)), c_, recip-star(c_)))
- `log-diff` — forall([u, v], u in rr implies v in rr implies 0 < u implies 0 < v implies c-int-or(vnb-lambda(z_, rr, recip-star(z_)), u, v) = log(v) - log(u))
- `log-in-rr` — forall([x_], log(x_) in rr)
- `log-injective-pos` — forall([u_, v_], u_ in rr implies v_ in rr implies 0 < u_ implies 0 < v_ implies log(u_) = log(v_) implies u_ = v_)
- `log-lam-in-fun` — vnb-lambda(x_, rr, log(x_)) in fun(rr, rr)
- `log-mul` — forall([x_, y_], x_ in rr implies y_ in rr implies 0 < x_ implies 0 < y_ implies log(x_ * y_) = log(x_) + log(y_))
- `log-neg-below-one` — forall([x_ in rr], 0 < x_ implies x_ < 1 implies log(x_) < 0)
- `log-one` — log(1) = 0
- `log-pos-above-one` — forall([x_ in rr], 1 < x_ implies 0 < log(x_))
- `log-r-exp` — forall([y_ in rr], log(r-exp(y_)) = y_)
- `log-recip` — forall([x_ in rr], 0 < x_ implies log(recip-star(x_)) = -log(x_))
- `log-rpow-star` — forall([x_, s_ in rr], 0 < x_ implies log(rpow-star(x_, s_)) = s_ * log(x_))
- `log-strictly-increasing` — forall([u, v], u in rr implies v in rr implies 0 < u implies u < v implies log(u) < log(v))
- `log-surjective` — forall([y_ in rr], forsome([x_ in rr], 0 < x_ and log(x_) = y_))
- `log-two-pos` — 0 < log(2)
- `log-unbounded-above` — forall([n_ in nn], forsome([b_ in rr], 0 < b_ and n_ * log(2) <= log(b_)))
- `log-unfold` — forall([x_], log(x_) == c-int-or(vnb-lambda(z_, rr, recip-star(z_)), 1, x_))
- `makeset-card-bound` — forall([n in nn, a, l in tuples(a)], length(l) = n implies card(make-set(l)) <= n)
- `makeset-card-bound-strong` — forall([n in nn, a, l in tuples(a)], length(l) = n implies make-set(l) in set and card(make-set(l)) in nn and card(make-set(l)) <= n)
- `makeset-of-empty-tuple` — forall([l_], l_ = [] implies make-set(l_) = empty-set)
- `makeset2-membership` — forall([x_, a_, b_], x_ in {a_, b_} iff x_ in set and (x_ = a_ or x_ = b_))
- `makeset2-split` — forall([a_ in set, b_ in set], {a_, b_} = union(pair(a_, a_), pair(b_, b_)))
- `mat-0-1-nonempty` — forall([x], forsome([p], p in mat(0, 1, x)))
- `mat-1-0-nonempty` — forall([x], forsome([p], p in mat(1, 0, x)))
- `mat-equiv-cod-is-mat` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, x, y], x in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies mat-equiv(a, m, n, x, y) implies y in mat(m, n, carr(a))))
- `mat-equiv-left-mult` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, c, u], c in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies is-invertible-mat(a, m, u) implies mat-equiv(a, m, n, c, matmul(a, u, c))))
- `mat-equiv-refl` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, c in mat(m, n, carr(a))], mat-equiv(a, m, n, c, c)))
- `mat-equiv-right-mult` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, c, v], c in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies is-invertible-mat(a, n, v) implies mat-equiv(a, m, n, c, matmul(a, c, v))))
