- `mat-equiv-target-is-mat` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, c, d], c in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies mat-equiv(a, m, n, c, d) implies d in mat(m, n, carr(a))))
- `mat-equiv-trans` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, c, d, e], c in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies mat-equiv(a, m, n, c, d) implies mat-equiv(a, m, n, d, e) implies mat-equiv(a, m, n, c, e)))
- `mat-ring-is-ring` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([n in nn], is-ring(mat-ring(a, n))))
- `mat-rows-in-nn` — forall([m, n, x, q in mat(m, n, x)], m in nn)
- `mat-unfold` — forall([m, n, x], {p in matrix(x): size(p) = [m, n]} == mat(m, n, x))
- `matact-assoc` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, u], p in mat(m, n, carr(scal(md))) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(scal(md))) implies u in mat(k, l, vec(md)) implies matact(md, matmul(scal(md), p, q), u) = matact(md, p, matact(md, q, u))))
- `matact-empty-vzero` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([c, u], c in mat(1, 0, carr(scal(md))) implies u in mat(0, 1, vec(md)) implies entry(matact(md, c, u), 1, 1) = vzero(md)))
- `matact-identmat` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([n, q, u in mat(n, q, vec(md))], matact(md, identmat(scal(md), n), u) = u))
- `matact-row-add` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([n, c1, c2, u], c1 in mat(1, n, carr(scal(md))) implies c2 in mat(1, n, carr(scal(md))) implies u in mat(n, 1, vec(md)) implies entry(matact(md, matadd(scal(md), c1, c2), u), 1, 1) = (vadd(md))(entry(matact(md, c1, u), 1, 1), entry(matact(md, c2, u), 1, 1))))
- `matact-row-peel` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([n in nn, c in mat(1, succ(n), carr(scal(md))), u in mat(succ(n), 1, vec(md))], entry(matact(md, c, u), 1, 1) = (vadd(md))(entry(matact(md, block(c, 1, n), block(u, n, 1)), 1, 1), (act(md))(entry(c, 1, succ(n)), entry(u, succ(n), 1)))))
- `matact-row-scale` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([n, r, c, u], r in carr(scal(md)) implies c in mat(1, n, carr(scal(md))) implies u in mat(n, 1, vec(md)) implies entry(matact(md, matscale(scal(md), r, c), u), 1, 1) = (act(md))(r, entry(matact(md, c, u), 1, 1))))
- `matact-snoc` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([n in nn, c in mat(1, n, carr(scal(md))), u in mat(n, 1, vec(md)), r in carr(scal(md)), x in vec(md)], entry(matact(md, snoc-row(c, n, r), snoc-col(u, n, x)), 1, 1) = (vadd(md))(entry(matact(md, c, u), 1, 1), (act(md))(r, x))))
- `matact-triple-left` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, u, row, col], p in mat(m, n, carr(scal(md))) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(scal(md))) implies u in mat(k, l, vec(md)) implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies entry(matact(md, matmul(scal(md), p, q), u), row, col) = finsum(module-vector-ag(md), vnb-lambda(c, interval(1, k), finsum(module-vector-ag(md), vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), (vnb-lambda(z, cartesian(interval(1, k), interval(1, n)), (act(md))((mul(scal(md)))(entry(p, row, nth(2, z)), entry(q, nth(2, z), nth(1, z))), entry(u, nth(1, z), col))))([c, j])), interval(1, n))), interval(1, k))))
- `matact-triple-right` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, u, row, col], p in mat(m, n, carr(scal(md))) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(scal(md))) implies u in mat(k, l, vec(md)) implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies entry(matact(md, p, matact(md, q, u)), row, col) = finsum(module-vector-ag(md), vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), finsum(module-vector-ag(md), vnb-lambda(c, interval(1, k), (vnb-lambda(z, cartesian(interval(1, k), interval(1, n)), (act(md))((mul(scal(md)))(entry(p, row, nth(2, z)), entry(q, nth(2, z), nth(1, z))), entry(u, nth(1, z), col))))([c, j])), interval(1, k))), interval(1, n))))
- `matact-unitrow` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([n, i, u in mat(n, 1, vec(md))], i in interval(1, n) implies entry(matact(md, unitrow(scal(md), n, i), u), 1, 1) = entry(u, i, 1)))
- `matact-zerorow` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([n, u in mat(n, 1, vec(md))], entry(matact(md, zeromat(scal(md), 1, n), u), 1, 1) = vzero(md)))
- `matmul-assoc` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, r], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(a)) implies r in mat(k, l, carr(a)) implies matmul(a, matmul(a, p, q), r) = matmul(a, p, matmul(a, q, r))))
- `matunit-col-shift` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, p, k, l], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies k in interval(1, n) implies l in interval(1, n) implies forall([i in interval(1, m), c in interval(1, n)], entry(matmul(a, p, matunit(a, n, k, l)), i, c) = if(c = l, entry(p, i, k), zero(a)))))
- `measure-space-as-measurable-space-functorial` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-measure-space(a, b, f) implies is-hom-measurable-space(measure-space-as-measurable-space(a), measure-space-as-measurable-space(b), f))
- `metric-dist-real` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([x in pts(s), y in pts(s)], (dist(s))(x, y) in rr))
- `metric-pos` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([x in pts(s), y in pts(s)], 0 <= (dist(s))(x, y)))
- `metric-self-zero` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([x in pts(s)], (dist(s))(x, x) = 0))
- `metric-sym` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([x in pts(s), y in pts(s)], (dist(s))(x, y) = (dist(s))(y, x)))
- `metric-top-functorial` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-metric-space(a, b, f) implies is-hom-metrizable-top-space(metric-top(a), metric-top(b), f))
- `metric-top-is-metrizable-top-space` — forall([md], is-metric-space(md) implies is-metrizable-top-space(metric-top(md)))
- `metric-top-is-top-space` — forall([md], is-metric-space(md) implies is-top-space(metric-top(md)))
- `metric-top@preimage` — forall([s, f, v], preimage(metric-top(s), f, v) == preimage(s, f, v))
- `metric-triangle` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([x in pts(s), y in pts(s), z in pts(s)], (dist(s))(x, z) <= (dist(s))(x, y) + (dist(s))(y, z)))
- `metric-zero-eq` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([x in pts(s), y in pts(s)], (dist(s))(x, y) = 0 implies x = y))
- `metrizable-iff-bounded-metrizable` — forall([s], is-top-space(s) implies is-metrizable-top-space(s) iff forsome([md], is-bounded-metric-space(md) and metric-top(md) == s))
- `min-degree-entry` — forall([a], is-euclidean-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, p in mat(m, n, carr(a))], forsome([i0, j0], i0 in interval(1, m) and j0 in interval(1, n) and not(entry(p, i0, j0) = zero(a))) implies forsome([is, js], is in interval(1, m) and js in interval(1, n) and not(entry(p, is, js) = zero(a)) and forall([i, j], i in interval(1, m) implies j in interval(1, n) implies not(entry(p, i, j) = zero(a)) implies (gauge(a))(entry(p, is, js)) <= (gauge(a))(entry(p, i, j))))))
- `module-act-neg-one` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([x in vec(md)], (act(md))((neg(scal(md)))(one(scal(md))), x) = (vneg(md))(x)))
- `module-vector-ag-functorial` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-module(a, b, f) implies is-hom-abelian-group(module-vector-ag(a), module-vector-ag(b), f))
- `module-zero-act` — forall([s], is-module(s) implies forall([x in vec(s)], (act(s))(zero(scal(s)), x) = vzero(s)))
- `monalg-add-apply` — forall([a_, m_, f_, g_, x_ in carr(m_)], (monalg-add(a_, m_, f_, g_))(x_) == (add(a_))(f_(x_), g_(x_)))
- `monalg-add-fun` — forall([a_, m_, f_, g_], is-ring(a_) implies is-monoid(m_) implies f_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies g_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies monalg-add(a_, m_, f_, g_) in finsupp(a_, m_))
- `monalg-zero-apply` — forall([a_, m_, x_ in carr(m_)], (monalg-zero(a_, m_))(x_) == zero(a_))
- `monalg-zero-fun` — forall([a_, m_], carr(m_) in set implies zero(a_) in carr(a_) implies monalg-zero(a_, m_) in fun(carr(m_), carr(a_)))
- `monoid-carrier-is-set` — forall([m_], is-monoid(m_) implies carr(m_) in set)
- `monomial-at` — forall([a_, c_, n_ in nn], (monomial(a_, c_, n_))(n_) = c_)
- `monomial-deg` — forall([a_, c_, n_], is-ring(a_) implies c_ in carr(a_) implies not(c_ = zero(a_)) implies n_ in nn implies deg(a_, monomial(a_, c_, n_)) = n_)
- `monomial-in-poly` — forall([a_, c_, n_], is-ring(a_) implies c_ in carr(a_) implies n_ in nn implies monomial(a_, c_, n_) in carr(poly(a_)))
- `monomial-leadcoef` — forall([a_, c_, n_], is-ring(a_) implies c_ in carr(a_) implies not(c_ = zero(a_)) implies n_ in nn implies leadcoef(a_, monomial(a_, c_, n_)) = c_)
- `monomial-off` — forall([a_, c_, n_, k_ in nn], not(k_ = n_) implies (monomial(a_, c_, n_))(k_) = zero(a_))
- `monomial-support` — forall([a_, c_, n_, k_], is-ring(a_) implies c_ in carr(a_) implies not(c_ = zero(a_)) implies n_ in nn implies k_ in supp(a_, nn-add-monoid, monomial(a_, c_, n_)) iff k_ in nn and k_ = n_)
- `monotone-ccint-inverse-exists` — forall([f, a, b], f in fun(rr, rr) implies a in rr implies b in rr implies a <= b implies forall([x in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x)) implies is-strictly-increasing-on(f, ccint(a, b)) implies forall([y in ccint(f(a), f(b))], forsome([p_ in ccint(a, b)], f(p_) = y and forall([q_ in ccint(a, b)], f(q_) = y implies p_ = q_))))
- `monotone-ccint-inverse-order` — forall([f, a, b], is-strictly-increasing-on(f, ccint(a, b)) implies forall([u_ in ccint(a, b), v_ in ccint(a, b)], f(u_) < f(v_) implies u_ < v_))
- `monotone-convergence-rr` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr)], forall([k in nn], f(k) <= f(succ(k))) and forsome([bnd in rr], forall([k in nn], f(k) <= bnd)) implies converges(rr-ms, f))
- `monotone-convergent-bounded-above` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr)], forall([k in nn], f(k) <= f(succ(k))) implies converges(rr-ms, f) implies forsome([bnd in rr], forall([k in nn], f(k) <= bnd)))
- `ms-eq-symm` — forall([x_, y_], x_ = y_ iff y_ = x_)
- `ms-index-2` — forall([i_ in nn], 1 <= i_ implies i_ <= 2 implies i_ = 1 or i_ = 2)
- `ms-nth-2` — forall([a_, b_, i_ in nn], 1 <= i_ implies i_ <= 2 implies nth(i_, [a_, b_]) = a_ or nth(i_, [a_, b_]) = b_)
- `mvt` — forall([f, a, b], f in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and b in rr and a < b implies forall([x in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x)) implies forall([x], x in rr and a < x and x < b implies forsome([l], is-diff-at(f, x, l))) implies forsome([theta in rr], a < theta and theta < b and forsome([l], is-diff-at(f, theta, l) and l * (b - a) = f(b) - f(a))))
- `mvt-abs-bound` — forall([f, a, b, m_], f in fun(rr, rr) implies a in rr implies b in rr implies m_ in rr implies a < b implies forall([x_ in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x_)) implies forall([x_], x_ in rr and a < x_ and x_ < b implies forsome([l_ in rr], is-diff-at(f, x_, l_) and abs(l_) <= m_)) implies abs(f(b) - f(a)) <= m_ * (b - a))
- `mvt-lower-bound` — forall([f, a, b, m], f in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and b in rr and m in rr and a < b implies forall([x in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x)) implies forall([x], x in rr and a < x and x < b implies forsome([l], is-diff-at(f, x, l) and m <= l)) implies m * (b - a) <= f(b) - f(a))
- `mvt-upper-bound` — forall([f, a, b, m], f in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and b in rr and m in rr and a < b implies forall([x in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x)) implies forall([x], x in rr and a < x and x < b implies forsome([l], is-diff-at(f, x, l) and l <= m)) implies f(b) - f(a) <= m * (b - a))
- `neg-continuous-at` — forall([f, x], f in fun(rr, rr) implies x in rr implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(z_, rr, -f(z_)), x))
- `neg-fun-in-fun` — forall([f in fun(rr, rr)], vnb-lambda(z_, rr, -f(z_)) in fun(rr, rr))
- `neq-sym` — forall([a, b], not(a = b) implies not(b = a))
- `nn-2-cancel` — forall([x in nn, y in nn], 2 * x = 2 * y implies x = y)
- `nn-3-cancel` — forall([x in nn, y in nn], 3 * x = 3 * y implies x = y)
- `nn-3-div-square` — forall([p in nn], forsome([m in nn], p * p = 3 * m) implies forsome([k in nn], p = 3 * k))
- `nn-3-not-succ` — forall([x in nn, y in nn], not(3 * x = succ(3 * y)))
- `nn-add-cancel` — forall([a in nn, b in nn, c in nn], a + c = b + c implies a = b)
- `nn-add-le-mono` — forall([c_ in nn, a_ in nn, b_ in nn], b_ <= c_ implies a_ + b_ <= a_ + c_)
- `nn-add-monoid-is-comm-monoid` — is-comm-monoid(nn-add-monoid)
- `nn-even-square` — forall([p in nn], forsome([m in nn], p * p = 2 * m) implies forsome([k in nn], p = 2 * k))
- `nn-flatten` — forall([aa_, h_ in fun(nn, fun(nn, aa_))], forsome([e_ in fun(nn, aa_)], forall([i_, j_], i_ in nn implies j_ in nn implies forsome([m_ in nn], e_(m_) = (h_(i_))(j_)))))
- `nn-in-rr` — forall([k in nn], k in rr)
- `nn-le-add` — forall([b_ in nn, a_ in nn], a_ <= a_ + b_)
- `nn-le-add-left` — forall([a_, b_], a_ in nn implies b_ in nn implies b_ <= a_ + b_)
- `nn-le-add-right` — forall([n in nn, m in nn], m <= m + n)
- `nn-le-antisym` — forall([a_ in nn, b_ in nn], a_ <= b_ implies b_ <= a_ implies a_ = b_)
- `nn-le-imp-neq-succ` — forall([k_ in nn, j_ in nn], j_ <= k_ implies not(j_ = succ(k_)))
- `nn-le-refl` — forall([k in nn], k <= k)
- `nn-le-succ` — forall([k_ in nn], k_ <= succ(k_))
- `nn-le-succ-cases` — forall([k_ in nn, j_ in nn], j_ <= succ(k_) implies j_ <= k_ or j_ = succ(k_))
- `nn-le-trans-guarded` — forall([a_ in nn, b_ in nn, c_ in nn], a_ <= b_ implies b_ <= c_ implies a_ <= c_)
- `nn-le-zero-is-zero` — forall([a_ in nn], a_ <= 0 implies a_ = 0)
- `nn-least-element` — forall([t], t subset nn and forsome([n], n in t) implies forsome([m in t], forall([k in t], m <= k)))
- `nn-lt-double` — forall([k in nn], not(k = 0) implies k < 2 * k)
- `nn-lt-succ-le` — forall([k_ in nn, j_ in nn], k_ < j_ implies succ(k_) <= j_)
- `nn-lt-triple` — forall([k in nn], not(k = 0) implies k < 3 * k)
- `nn-max-closed` — forall([x in nn, y in nn], max(x, y) in nn)
- `nn-min-closed` — forall([x in nn, y in nn], min(x, y) in nn)
- `nn-monotone-step-implies-le` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr)], forall([k in nn], f(k) <= f(succ(k))) implies forall([n in nn, m in nn], m <= n implies f(m) <= f(n)))
- `nn-mul-cancel` — forall([a in nn, b in nn, c in nn], not(c = 0) implies a * c = b * c implies a = b)
- `nn-mul-nonzero` — forall([a in nn], not(a = 0) implies forall([b in nn], not(b = 0) implies not(a * b = 0)))
- `nn-mul-succ` — forall([a in nn, b in nn], a * succ(b) = a * b + a)
- `nn-nested-subset-chain` — forall([t], forall([k in nn], t(succ(k)) subset t(k)) implies forall([k in nn, j in nn], k <= j implies t(j) subset t(k)))
- `nn-nested-subset-chain-j` — forall([fam], forall([k in nn], fam(succ(k)) subset fam(k)) implies forall([j in nn, k in nn], k <= j implies fam(j) subset fam(k)))
- `nn-nonzero-is-succ` — forall([n in nn], not(n = 0) implies forsome([q in nn], n = succ(q)))
- `nn-not-lt-le` — forall([k_ in nn, j_ in nn], not(k_ < j_) implies j_ <= k_)
- `nn-one-in` — 1 in nn
- `nn-one-is-succ-zero` — 1 = succ(0)
- `nn-one-le-succ` — forall([n_ in nn], 1 <= succ(n_))
- `nn-pair-upper-bound` — forall([a, b], a in nn implies b in nn implies forsome([c in nn], a <= c and b <= c))
- `nn-parity` — forall([n in nn], forsome([k in nn], n = 2 * k or n = succ(2 * k)))
- `nn-parity-exclusive` — forall([x in nn, y in nn], not(2 * x = succ(2 * y)))
- `nn-plus-three` — forall([x in nn], x + 3 = succ(succ(succ(x))))
- `nn-plus-two` — forall([x in nn], x + 2 = succ(succ(x)))
- `nn-recip-succ-antitone` — forall([m in nn, n_ in nn], m <= n_ implies recip(n_ + 1) <= recip(m + 1))
- `nn-succ-add` — forall([a_ in nn, b_ in nn], succ(a_) + b_ = succ(a_ + b_))
- `nn-succ-inj` — forall([a in nn, b in nn], succ(a) = succ(b) implies a = b)
- `nn-succ-mono` — forall([a_ in nn, b_ in nn], a_ <= b_ implies succ(a_) <= succ(b_))
- `nn-succ-nonzero` — forall([n in nn], not(succ(n) = 0))
- `nn-succ-not-le` — forall([n_ in nn], not(succ(n_) <= n_))
- `nn-succ-plus-one` — forall([n in nn], succ(n) = n + 1)
- `nn-three-is-succ3-zero` — 3 = succ(succ(succ(0)))
- `nn-three-mul-succ` — forall([k in nn], 3 * succ(k) = succ(succ(succ(3 * k))))
- `nn-trichotomy-3` — forall([n in nn], forsome([k in nn], n = 3 * k or n = succ(3 * k) or n = succ(succ(3 * k))))
- `nn-two-is-succ-succ-zero` — 2 = succ(succ(0))
- `nn-two-mul-succ` — forall([k in nn], 2 * succ(k) = succ(succ(2 * k)))
- `nn-zero-le` — forall([n_ in nn], 0 <= n_)
- `nn-zero-or-succ` — forall([y in nn], y = 0 or forsome([q in nn], y = succ(q)))
- `nnfst-nnpair` — forall([i_, j_], i_ in nn implies j_ in nn implies nnfst(nnpair(i_, j_)) = i_)
- `nnfst-type` — forall([n_ in nn], nnfst(n_) in nn)
- `nnpair-cross` — forall([s_, t_, j_, k_], s_ in nn implies t_ in nn implies j_ in nn implies k_ in nn implies j_ <= s_ implies succ(s_) <= t_ implies succ(trinum(s_) + j_) <= trinum(t_) + k_)
- `nnpair-diag-bound` — forall([s_, j_], s_ in nn implies j_ in nn implies j_ <= s_ implies succ(trinum(s_) + j_) <= trinum(succ(s_)))
- `nnpair-inj` — forall([i_, j_, ii_, jj_], i_ in nn implies j_ in nn implies ii_ in nn implies jj_ in nn implies nnpair(i_, j_) = nnpair(ii_, jj_) implies i_ = ii_ and j_ = jj_)
- `nnpair-onto` — forall([n_ in nn], forsome([i_ in nn, j_ in nn], nnpair(i_, j_) = n_))
- `nnpair-type` — forall([i_, j_], i_ in nn implies j_ in nn implies nnpair(i_, j_) in nn)
- `nnsnd-nnpair` — forall([i_, j_], i_ in nn implies j_ in nn implies nnsnd(nnpair(i_, j_)) = j_)
- `nnsnd-type` — forall([n_ in nn], nnsnd(n_) in nn)
- `no-injection-seg-1-into-seg-0` — forall([f], not(f in injection(ord-segment(succ(0)), ord-segment(0))))
- `no-maximal-strictly-above` — forall([grd, porel], not(forsome([mx_], is-maximal(grd, porel, mx_))) implies forall([x_ in grd], forsome([y_ in grd], is-strictly-below(porel, x_, y_))))
- `noetherian-set-has-maximal` — forall([m], is-noetherian(m) implies forall([sig in set], sig subset power(vec(m)) implies forall([t in sig], is-submodule(m, t)) implies forsome([t0], t0 in sig) implies forsome([t in sig], forall([u], u in sig and t subset u implies t = u))))
- `nonzero-ident-continuous-at` — forall([c in rr], not(c = 0) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(w_, rr, if(w_ = 0, 1, w_)), c))
- `nonzero-ident-lam-in-fun` — vnb-lambda(w_, rr, if(w_ = 0, 1, w_)) in fun(rr, rr)
- `nonzero-ident-nowhere-zero` — forall([x in rr], not((vnb-lambda(w_, rr, if(w_ = 0, 1, w_)))(x) = 0))
- `norm-as-sup` — forall([m, x], is-normed-vector-space(m) and is-finite-dimensional(normed-vector-space-as-module(m)) and x in vec(m) implies forall([f], is-bounded-linear-functional(m, f) implies dual-norm(m, f) <= 1 implies abs(f(x)) <= (vnrm(m))(x)) and forall([d], d in rr and forall([f], is-bounded-linear-functional(m, f) implies dual-norm(m, f) <= 1 implies abs(f(x)) <= d) implies (vnrm(m))(x) <= d))
- `norm-attained-by-functional` — forall([m, x], is-normed-vector-space(m) and is-finite-dimensional(normed-vector-space-as-module(m)) and x in vec(m) implies forsome([g], is-bounded-linear-functional(m, g) and dual-norm(m, g) <= 1 and g(x) = (vnrm(m))(x)))
- `norm-bounded-by-functionals` — forall([m, f, x], is-normed-vector-space(m) and is-bounded-linear-functional(m, f) and x in vec(m) and dual-norm(m, f) <= 1 implies abs(f(x)) <= (vnrm(m))(x))
- `normed-ag-as-abelian-group-functorial` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-normed-ag(a, b, f) implies is-hom-abelian-group(normed-ag-as-abelian-group(a), normed-ag-as-abelian-group(b), f))
- `normed-field-additive-ag-functorial` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-normed-field(a, b, f) implies is-hom-abelian-group(normed-field-additive-ag(a), normed-field-additive-ag(b), f))
