- `normed-field-as-integral-domain-functorial` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-normed-field(a, b, f) implies is-hom-integral-domain(normed-field-as-integral-domain(a), normed-field-as-integral-domain(b), f))
- `normed-field-ring-view-add` — forall([f_], add(normed-field-as-commutative-ring(f_)) == add(f_))
- `normed-field-ring-view-carr` — forall([f_], carr(normed-field-as-commutative-ring(f_)) == carr(f_))
- `normed-field-ring-view-mul` — forall([f_], mul(normed-field-as-commutative-ring(f_)) == mul(f_))
- `normed-field-ring-view-neg` — forall([f_], neg(normed-field-as-commutative-ring(f_)) == neg(f_))
- `normed-field-ring-view-one` — forall([f_], one(normed-field-as-commutative-ring(f_)) == one(f_))
- `normed-field-ring-view-zero` — forall([f_], zero(normed-field-as-commutative-ring(f_)) == zero(f_))
- `normed-vector-space-as-module-functorial` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-normed-vector-space(a, b, f) implies is-hom-module(normed-vector-space-as-module(a), normed-vector-space-as-module(b), f))
- `normed-vector-space-as-normed-ag-functorial` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-normed-vector-space(a, b, f) implies is-hom-normed-ag(normed-vector-space-as-normed-ag(a), normed-vector-space-as-normed-ag(b), f))
- `not-continuous-witness` — forall([s, t, f, a, eps, dl], not(forall([b_ in pts(s)], (dist(s))(a, b_) <= dl implies (dist(t))(f(a), f(b_)) <= eps)) implies forsome([b_ in pts(s)], (dist(s))(a, b_) <= dl and not((dist(t))(f(a), f(b_)) <= eps)))
- `nt-unit-factor` — forall([y_, k_], y_ in rr implies k_ in nn implies y_ * (1 - 0) ^ succ(k_) = y_)
- `nth-deriv-one` — forall([f], nth-deriv(f, 1) == vnb-lambda(x, rr, deriv(f, x)))
- `nth1-pair` — forall([a, b], nth(1, [a, b]) == a)
- `nvs-module-view-act` — forall([m_], act(normed-vector-space-as-module(m_)) == act(m_))
- `nvs-module-view-scal` — forall([m_], scal(normed-vector-space-as-module(m_)) == scal(m_))
- `nvs-module-view-vadd` — forall([m_], vadd(normed-vector-space-as-module(m_)) == vadd(m_))
- `nvs-module-view-vec` — forall([m_], vec(normed-vector-space-as-module(m_)) == vec(m_))
- `nvs-module-view-vneg` — forall([m_], vneg(normed-vector-space-as-module(m_)) == vneg(m_))
- `nvs-module-view-vzero` — forall([m_], vzero(normed-vector-space-as-module(m_)) == vzero(m_))
- `nvs-taylor-remainder-bound` — forall([dm, cm, f, a, h, n], is-normed-vector-space(dm) implies is-finite-dimensional(normed-vector-space-as-module(dm)) implies is-normed-vector-space(cm) implies is-finite-dimensional(normed-vector-space-as-module(cm)) implies f in fun(vec(dm), vec(cm)) implies a in vec(dm) implies h in vec(dm) implies n in nn implies taylor-differentiable-v(cm, vnb-lambda(t, rr, f((vadd(dm))(a, (act(dm))(t, h)))), 0, 1, n) implies forsome([theta], 0 < theta and theta < 1 and factorial(succ(n)) * (vnrm(cm))((vadd(cm))(f((vadd(dm))(a, h)), (vneg(cm))(taylor-poly-v(cm, vnb-lambda(t, rr, f((vadd(dm))(a, (act(dm))(t, h)))), 0, 1, n)))) <= (vnrm(cm))((nth-deriv-v(cm, vnb-lambda(t, rr, f((vadd(dm))(a, (act(dm))(t, h)))), succ(n)))(theta))))
- `nvs-vadd-in-vec` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m), y_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))(x_, y_) in vec(m)))
- `ord-lt-succ-cases` — forall([a_, b_], a_ in ord implies b_ in ord implies ord-lt(b_, succ_ord(a_)) implies ord-lt(b_, a_) or b_ = a_)
- `ord-lt-succ-iff-le` — forall([al in ord, cc in ord], ord-lt(cc, succ_ord(al)) iff ord-le(cc, al))
- `ord-no-injection-into-set` — forall([grd, phi], grd in set implies forall([alpha in ord], phi(alpha) in grd) implies forall([a1_ in ord, a2_ in ord], phi(a1_) = phi(a2_) implies a1_ = a2_) implies falsity)
- `ord-segment-zero-no-members` — forall([k], not(k in ord-segment(0)))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `ord-succ-lt-limit` — forall([lam, b_], limit-ord(lam) implies b_ in ord implies ord-lt(b_, lam) implies ord-lt(succ_ord(b_), lam))
- `ord-well-ordered` — forall([cl], forall([x in cl], x in ord) and forsome([w], w in cl) implies forsome([m in cl], forall([k in cl], ord-le(m, k))))
- `pair-in-cartesian` — forall([x, y, u, v], u in x implies v in y implies [u, v] in cartesian(x, y))
- `pair-tuple-is-set` — forall([x, y, u, v], u in x implies v in y implies [u, v] in set)
- `pigeonhole-segments` — forall([n_ in nn, f_], not(f_ in injection(ord-segment(succ(n_)), ord-segment(n_))))
- `pigeonhole-segments-gen` — forall([m_, n_], m_ in nn implies n_ in nn implies n_ < m_ implies forall([f_], not(f_ in injection(ord-segment(m_), ord-segment(n_)))))
- `pigeonhole-step` — forall([n_ in nn], forall([f_], not(f_ in injection(ord-segment(succ(n_)), ord-segment(n_)))) implies forall([g_], not(g_ in injection(ord-segment(succ(succ(n_))), ord-segment(succ(n_))))))
- `pivot-clears-col` — forall([a], is-euclidean-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, p, j], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies 1 in interval(1, m) implies 1 in interval(1, n) implies j in interval(1, n) implies not(1 = j) implies not(entry(p, 1, 1) = zero(a)) implies forall([c], mat-equiv(a, m, n, p, c) implies forall([ii, jj], ii in interval(1, m) implies jj in interval(1, n) implies not(entry(c, ii, jj) = zero(a)) implies (gauge(a))(entry(p, 1, 1)) <= (gauge(a))(entry(c, ii, jj)))) implies forsome([q], mat-equiv(a, m, n, p, q) and entry(q, 1, 1) = entry(p, 1, 1) and entry(q, 1, j) = zero(a) and forall([ii, c], ii in interval(1, m) implies c in interval(1, n) implies not(c = j) implies entry(q, ii, c) = entry(p, ii, c)))))
- `pivot-clears-row` — forall([a], is-euclidean-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, p, i], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies 1 in interval(1, m) implies 1 in interval(1, n) implies i in interval(1, m) implies not(i = 1) implies not(entry(p, 1, 1) = zero(a)) implies forall([c], mat-equiv(a, m, n, p, c) implies forall([ii, jj], ii in interval(1, m) implies jj in interval(1, n) implies not(entry(c, ii, jj) = zero(a)) implies (gauge(a))(entry(p, 1, 1)) <= (gauge(a))(entry(c, ii, jj)))) implies forsome([q], mat-equiv(a, m, n, p, q) and entry(q, 1, 1) = entry(p, 1, 1) and entry(q, i, 1) = zero(a) and forall([rr, c], rr in interval(1, m) implies c in interval(1, n) implies not(rr = i) implies entry(q, rr, c) = entry(p, rr, c)))))
- `pivot-col-reduce` — forall([a], is-euclidean-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, p, j], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies 1 in interval(1, m) implies 1 in interval(1, n) implies j in interval(1, n) implies not(1 = j) implies not(entry(p, 1, 1) = zero(a)) implies forsome([q in carr(a), r in carr(a)], entry(matmul(a, p, elem-g(a, n, (neg(a))(q), 1, j)), 1, j) = r and (r = zero(a) or succ((gauge(a))(r)) <= (gauge(a))(entry(p, 1, 1))))))
- `pivot-row-reduce` — forall([a], is-euclidean-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, p, i], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies 1 in interval(1, m) implies 1 in interval(1, n) implies i in interval(1, m) implies not(i = 1) implies not(entry(p, 1, 1) = zero(a)) implies forsome([q in carr(a), r in carr(a)], entry(matmul(a, elem-g(a, m, (neg(a))(q), i, 1), p), i, 1) = r and (r = zero(a) or succ((gauge(a))(r)) <= (gauge(a))(entry(p, 1, 1))))))
- `place-min-pivot` — forall([a], is-euclidean-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, p in mat(m, n, carr(a))], 1 in interval(1, m) implies 1 in interval(1, n) implies forsome([i0, j0], i0 in interval(1, m) and j0 in interval(1, n) and not(entry(p, i0, j0) = zero(a))) implies forsome([b], mat-equiv(a, m, n, p, b) and not(entry(b, 1, 1) = zero(a)) and forall([i, j], i in interval(1, m) implies j in interval(1, n) implies not(entry(p, i, j) = zero(a)) implies (gauge(a))(entry(b, 1, 1)) <= (gauge(a))(entry(p, i, j))))))
- `po-reflexive` — forall([grd, porel, x_], is-partial-order(grd, porel) implies x_ in grd implies [x_, x_] in porel)
- `po-transitive` — forall([grd, porel, x_, y_, z_], is-partial-order(grd, porel) implies x_ in grd implies y_ in grd implies z_ in grd implies [x_, y_] in porel implies [y_, z_] in porel implies [x_, z_] in porel)
- `poly-antiderivative` — forall([n in nn, cf in fun(nn, rr), pt in rr], is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(x, rr, series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(k, nn, (recip(succ(k)) * cf(k)) * x ^ succ(k)), succ(n))), pt, series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(k, nn, cf(k) * pt ^ k), succ(n))))
- `poly-carrier` — forall([a_], carr(poly(a_)) == finsupp(a_, nn-add-monoid))
- `poly-dseq-lam-in-fun` — forall([cf in fun(nn, rr), pt in rr], vnb-lambda(j, nn, (succ(j) * cf(succ(j))) * pt ^ j) in fun(nn, rr))
- `poly-fn-antiderivable` — forall([n_ in nn, cf, ph_, a, b], cf in fun(nn, rr) implies a in rr and b in rr and a < b implies ph_ in fun(rr, rr) implies forall([x_ in rr], ph_(x_) == series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(k_, nn, cf(k_) * x_ ^ k_), n_)) implies is-antiderivable(ph_, a, b))
- `poly-is-antiderivable` — forall([n in nn, cf in fun(nn, rr), a, b], a in rr and b in rr and a < b implies is-antiderivable(vnb-lambda(x_, rr, series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(k_, nn, cf(k_) * x_ ^ k_), succ(n))), a, b))
- `poly-is-ring` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies is-ring(poly(a)))
- `poly-lam-in-fun` — forall([cf in fun(nn, rr), n in nn], vnb-lambda(x, rr, series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(k, nn, cf(k) * x ^ k), n)) in fun(rr, rr))
- `poly-membership` — forall([a_, f_], f_ in carr(poly(a_)) iff f_ in sqn(carr(a_)) and card(supp(a_, nn-add-monoid, f_)) in nn)
- `poly-step-identity` — forall([c, u, v], c in rr implies u in rr implies v in rr implies (c * u) * v = u * (c * v))
- `poly-tail-zero` — forall([a_, f_], f_ in carr(poly(a_)) iff f_ in sqn(carr(a_)) and forsome([n_ in nn], forall([k_ in nn], n_ <= k_ implies f_(k_) = zero(a_))))
- `poly-tail-zero-bwd` — forall([a_, f_ in sqn(carr(a_))], forsome([n_ in nn], forall([k_ in nn], n_ <= k_ implies f_(k_) = zero(a_))) implies f_ in carr(poly(a_)))
- `poly-tail-zero-fwd` — forall([a_, f_ in carr(poly(a_))], forsome([n_ in nn], forall([k_ in nn], n_ <= k_ implies f_(k_) = zero(a_))))
- `poly-term-lam-in-fun` — forall([cf in fun(nn, rr), x in rr], vnb-lambda(k, nn, cf(k) * x ^ k) in fun(nn, rr))
- `poly-zero-apply` — forall([a_, k_], is-ring(a_) implies k_ in nn implies (zero(poly(a_)))(k_) = zero(a_))
- `poly-zero-fun` — forall([a_], is-ring(a_) implies zero(poly(a_)) in fun(nn, carr(a_)))
- `poly-zero-iff` — forall([a_, p_], is-ring(a_) implies p_ in carr(poly(a_)) implies p_ = zero(poly(a_)) iff forall([k_ in nn], p_(k_) = zero(a_)))
- `poly-zero-unfold` — forall([a_], zero(poly(a_)) == monalg-zero(a_, nn-add-monoid))
- `pow-lam-in-fun` — forall([n in nn], vnb-lambda(x, rr, x ^ n) in fun(rr, rr))
- `power-antiderivable` — forall([m_, a, b], m_ in nn implies a in rr and b in rr and a < b implies is-antiderivable(vnb-lambda(x, rr, x ^ m_), a, b))
- `power-big-union-closed` — forall([a_, fam_], a_ in set implies fam_ subset power(a_) implies big-union(u_, fam_, u_) in power(a_))
- `power-closed-at` — forall([n in nn, x in rr], x ^ n in rr)
- `power-complement-closed` — forall([a_, b_], a_ in set implies complement-in(a_, b_) in power(a_))
- `power-empty-in` — forall([a_], empty-set in power(a_))
- `power-fun-union-closed` — forall([a_, f_ in fun(nn, power(a_))], big-union(n_, nn, f_(n_)) in power(a_))
- `power-in-rr` — forall([b in rr, n in nn], b ^ n in rr)
- `power-inter-closed` — forall([a_, u_, v_], u_ in power(a_) implies v_ in power(a_) implies intersection(u_, v_) in power(a_))
- `power-mem-in` — forall([a_, b_, z_], b_ in power(a_) implies z_ in b_ implies z_ in a_)
- `power-mem-intro` — forall([a_, b_ in set], forall([z_ in b_], z_ in a_) implies b_ in power(a_))
- `power-mem-sethood` — forall([a_, b_ in power(a_)], b_ in set)
- `power-real-closed` — forall([x in rr, n in nn], x ^ n in rr)
- `power-two-pos` — forall([k in nn], 0 < 2 ^ k)
- `power-whole-in` — forall([a_ in set], a_ in power(a_))
- `power-zero-succ` — forall([m in nn], 0 ^ succ(m) = 0)
- `pred-in-nn` — forall([n_ in nn], not(n_ = 0) implies pred(n_) in nn)
- `pred-of-succ` — forall([m_ in nn], pred(succ(m_)) = m_)
- `pred-property` — forall([n_ in nn], not(n_ = 0) implies pred(n_) in nn and succ(pred(n_)) = n_)
- `pred-succ` — forall([n_ in nn], not(n_ = 0) implies succ(pred(n_)) = n_)
- `prod-lam-in-fun` — forall([g, h], g in fun(rr, rr) implies h in fun(rr, rr) implies vnb-lambda(x, rr, g(x) * h(x)) in fun(rr, rr))
- `product-carrier-coord` — forall([ms, x_ in product-carrier(ms), n_ in nn], x_(n_) in pts(ms(n_)))
- `product-carrier-unfold` — forall([ms], product-carrier(ms) == {x in fun(nn, big-union(n, nn, pts(ms(n)))): forall([n in nn], x(n) in pts(ms(n)))})
- `product-continuous-at` — forall([g, h, a], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, g, a) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, h, a) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(x, rr, g(x) * h(x)), a))
- `product-convergence-coordinatewise` — forall([ms], is-ms-sequence(ms) implies forall([w], summable-weight(w) implies forall([seq in fun(nn, product-carrier(ms)), lv in product-carrier(ms)], converges-to(product-metric-w(ms, w), seq, lv) iff forall([n_ in nn], converges-to(ms(n_), vnb-lambda(k, nn, (seq(k))(n_)), lv(n_))))))
- `product-convergence-coordinatewise-bwd` — forall([ms], is-ms-sequence(ms) implies forall([w], summable-weight(w) implies forall([seq in fun(nn, product-carrier(ms)), lv in product-carrier(ms)], forall([n_ in nn], converges-to(ms(n_), vnb-lambda(k, nn, (seq(k))(n_)), lv(n_))) implies converges-to(product-metric-w(ms, w), seq, lv))))
- `product-convergence-coordinatewise-fwd` — forall([ms], is-ms-sequence(ms) implies forall([w], summable-weight(w) implies forall([seq in fun(nn, product-carrier(ms)), lv in product-carrier(ms)], converges-to(product-metric-w(ms, w), seq, lv) implies forall([n_ in nn], converges-to(ms(n_), vnb-lambda(k, nn, (seq(k))(n_)), lv(n_))))))
- `product-coord-from-weighted-null` — forall([ms], is-ms-sequence(ms) implies forall([w], summable-weight(w) implies forall([seq in fun(nn, product-carrier(ms)), lv in product-carrier(ms), n_ in nn], converges-to(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(j_, nn, w(n_) * (dist(bdd-metric(ms(n_))))((seq(j_))(n_), lv(n_))), 0) implies converges-to(ms(n_), vnb-lambda(k, nn, (seq(k))(n_)), lv(n_)))))
- `product-coord-weighted-null` — forall([ms], is-ms-sequence(ms) implies forall([w], summable-weight(w) implies forall([seq in fun(nn, product-carrier(ms)), lv in product-carrier(ms), n_ in nn], converges-to(ms(n_), vnb-lambda(k, nn, (seq(k))(n_)), lv(n_)) implies converges-to(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(j_, nn, w(n_) * (dist(bdd-metric(ms(n_))))((seq(j_))(n_), lv(n_))), 0))))
- `product-identity-continuous` — forall([ms], is-ms-sequence(ms) implies forall([w], summable-weight(w) implies forall([w2], summable-weight(w2) implies is-continuous(product-metric-w(ms, w), product-metric-w(ms, w2), vnb-lambda(x, pts(product-metric-w(ms, w)), x)))))
- `product-metric-carrier` — forall([ms, w], pts(product-metric-w(ms, w)) == product-carrier(ms))
- `product-metric-default-summable` — summable-weight(vnb-lambda(n, nn, (/)(1, 2 ^ (n + 1))))
- `product-metric-dist-converges` — forall([ms], is-ms-sequence(ms) implies forall([w], summable-weight(w) implies forall([x in product-carrier(ms), y in product-carrier(ms)], series-converges-to(vnb-lambda(n, nn, w(n) * (dist(bdd-metric(ms(n))))(x(n), y(n))), (dist(product-metric-w(ms, w)))(x, y)))))
- `product-metric-distance` — forall([ms, w, x in product-carrier(ms), y in product-carrier(ms)], (dist(product-metric-w(ms, w)))(x, y) == series-limit(vnb-lambda(n, nn, w(n) * (dist(bdd-metric(ms(n))))(x(n), y(n)))))
- `product-of-invertibles-is-invertible` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([n, u1, u2], is-invertible-mat(a, n, u1) implies is-invertible-mat(a, n, u2) implies is-invertible-mat(a, n, matmul(a, u1, u2))))
- `product-term-seq-in-fun` — forall([ms], is-ms-sequence(ms) implies forall([w], summable-weight(w) implies forall([x in product-carrier(ms), y in product-carrier(ms)], vnb-lambda(n, nn, w(n) * (dist(bdd-metric(ms(n))))(x(n), y(n))) in fun(nn, rr))))
- `product-weighted-summable` — forall([ms], is-ms-sequence(ms) implies forall([w], summable-weight(w) implies forall([x in product-carrier(ms), y in product-carrier(ms)], series-converges(vnb-lambda(n, nn, w(n) * (dist(bdd-metric(ms(n))))(x(n), y(n)))))))
- `product-weights-equivalent` — forall([ms], is-ms-sequence(ms) implies forall([w], summable-weight(w) implies forall([w2], summable-weight(w2) implies is-continuous(product-metric-w(ms, w), product-metric-w(ms, w2), vnb-lambda(x, pts(product-metric-w(ms, w)), x)) and is-continuous(product-metric-w(ms, w2), product-metric-w(ms, w), vnb-lambda(x, pts(product-metric-w(ms, w2)), x)))))
- `pseudometric-pos` — forall([dst, crr], is-pseudometric(dst, crr) implies forall([u in crr, v in crr], 0 <= dst(u, v)))
- `pseudometric-self-zero` — forall([dst, crr], is-pseudometric(dst, crr) implies forall([u in crr], dst(u, u) = 0))
- `pseudometric-sym` — forall([dst, crr], is-pseudometric(dst, crr) implies forall([u in crr, v in crr], dst(u, v) = dst(v, u)))
- `pseudometric-triangle` — forall([dst, crr], is-pseudometric(dst, crr) implies forall([u in crr, v in crr, w in crr], dst(u, w) <= dst(u, v) + dst(v, w)))
- `pw-step-identity` — forall([c, u, v], c in rr implies u in rr implies v in rr implies (c + 1) * (u * v) = 1 * (u * v) + u * (c * v))
- `qq-add-cancel` — forall([a in qq, b in qq, c in qq], a + c = b + c implies a = b)
- `qq-field-is-field` — is-field(qq-field)
- `qq-field-mul-inv-type` — vnb-lambda(x_, difference(qq, singleton(0)), recip(x_)) in fun(difference(qq, singleton(0)), difference(qq, singleton(0)))
- `qq-is-ring` — is-ring(qq-ring)
- `qq-line-is-module` — is-module(qq-line)
- `qq-line-is-vector-space` — is-vector-space(qq-line)
- `qq-recip-nonzero` — forall([a_ in qq], not(a_ = 0) implies not(recip(a_) = 0))
- `r-exp-add` — forall([u_, v_], u_ in rr implies v_ in rr implies r-exp(u_ + v_) = r-exp(u_) * r-exp(v_))
- `r-exp-char` — forall([y_, x_], y_ in rr implies x_ in rr implies 0 < x_ implies log(x_) = y_ implies r-exp(y_) = x_)
- `r-exp-continuous-at` — forall([t_ in rr], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(y_, rr, r-exp(y_)), t_))
- `r-exp-deriv` — forall([t_ in rr], is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(y_, rr, r-exp(y_)), t_, r-exp(t_)))
- `r-exp-diff-at-log` — forall([c_, w_], c_ in rr implies w_ in rr implies 0 < c_ implies w_ = log(c_) implies is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(y_, rr, r-exp(y_)), w_, c_))
- `r-exp-in-rr` — forall([y_ in rr], r-exp(y_) in rr)
- `r-exp-lam-in-fun` — vnb-lambda(y_, rr, r-exp(y_)) in fun(rr, rr)
- `r-exp-log` — forall([x_ in rr], 0 < x_ implies r-exp(log(x_)) = x_)
- `r-exp-log-not-global` — not(forall([x_ in rr], r-exp(log(x_)) = x_))
- `r-exp-mono` — forall([u_, v_], u_ in rr implies v_ in rr implies u_ <= v_ implies r-exp(u_) <= r-exp(v_))
- `r-exp-neg` — forall([y_ in rr], r-exp(-y_) = recip-star(r-exp(y_)))
- `r-exp-pos` — forall([y_ in rr], 0 < r-exp(y_))
- `r-exp-prop` — forall([y_ in rr], r-exp(y_) in rr and 0 < r-exp(y_) and log(r-exp(y_)) = y_)
- `r-exp-strictly-increasing` — forall([u_, v_], u_ in rr implies v_ in rr implies u_ < v_ implies r-exp(u_) < r-exp(v_))
- `r-exp-zero` — r-exp(0) = 1
- `real-part-in-rr` — forall([z in cc], real-part(z) in rr)
- `recip-continuous-at` — forall([h, p], h in fun(rr, rr) implies forall([x in rr], not(h(x) = 0)) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, h, p) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(x, rr, recip(h(x))), p))
- `recip-factorial-in-rr` — forall([n in nn], recip(factorial(n)) in rr)
- `recip-lam-in-fun` — forall([h in fun(rr, rr)], forall([x in rr], not(h(x) = 0)) implies vnb-lambda(x, rr, recip(h(x))) in fun(rr, rr))
- `recip-power-two-halves` — forall([k in nn], recip(2 ^ k) = 2 * recip(2 * 2 ^ k))
- `recip-star-antiderivable-pos` — forall([a, b], a in rr implies b in rr implies 0 < a implies a < b implies is-antiderivable(vnb-lambda(z_, rr, recip-star(z_)), a, b))
- `recip-star-continuous-at` — forall([c in rr], not(c = 0) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(z_, rr, recip-star(z_)), c))
- `recip-star-continuous-on-ccint` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, x_ in rr], 0 < a implies x_ in ccint(a, b) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(z_, rr, recip-star(z_)), x_))
- `recip-star-in-rr` — forall([u_ in rr], recip-star(u_) in rr)
- `recip-star-int-additive` — forall([u, v, w], u in rr implies v in rr implies w in rr implies 0 < u implies 0 < v implies 0 < w implies c-int-or(vnb-lambda(z_, rr, recip-star(z_)), u, w) = c-int-or(vnb-lambda(z_, rr, recip-star(z_)), u, v) + c-int-or(vnb-lambda(z_, rr, recip-star(z_)), v, w))
- `recip-star-inverse` — forall([u_ in rr], not(u_ = 0) implies u_ * recip-star(u_) = 1)
- `recip-star-lam-in-fun` — vnb-lambda(z_, rr, recip-star(z_)) in fun(rr, rr)
- `recip-star-scale` — forall([lam, u], lam in rr implies u in rr implies not(lam = 0) implies recip-star(lam * u) * lam = recip-star(u))
- `recip-star-value` — forall([u_ in rr], not(u_ = 0) implies recip-star(u_) = recip(u_))
- `recip-star-zero` — recip-star(0) = 0
- `recip-succ-cancel` — forall([m in nn, c in rr, v in rr], c * v = (succ(m) * (recip(succ(m)) * c)) * v)
- `ring-add-closed` — forall([s], is-ring(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], (add(s))(a, b) in carr(s)))
- `ring-additive-ag-functorial` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-ring(a, b, f) implies is-hom-abelian-group(ring-additive-ag(a), ring-additive-ag(b), f))
- `ring-carrier-closed-mul` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([a in carr(r), b in carr(r)], (mul(r))(a, b) in carr(r)))
- `ring-multiplicative-monoid-functorial` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-ring(a, b, f) implies is-hom-monoid(ring-multiplicative-monoid(a), ring-multiplicative-monoid(b), f))
- `ring-neg-in-carr` — forall([s], is-ring(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], (neg(s))(a) in carr(s)))
- `ring-power-of-one` — forall([n_ in nn, r], is-commutative-ring(r) implies ring-power(r, one(r), n_) = one(r))
- `ringoid-rel-is-equivalence` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies is-equivalence(ringoid-rel(r), carr(r)))
- `ringoid-setoid-is-setoid` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies is-setoid(ringoid-setoid(r)))
- `rolle` — forall([h, a, b], h in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and b in rr and a < b implies forall([x in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, h, x)) implies forall([x], x in rr and a < x and x < b implies forsome([l], is-diff-at(h, x, l))) implies h(a) = h(b) implies forsome([theta in rr], a < theta and theta < b and is-diff-at(h, theta, 0)))
- `rolle-neutral` — forall([h, lo, hi], h in fun(rr, rr) and lo in rr and hi in rr and lo < hi implies forall([x in ccint(lo, hi)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, h, x)) implies forall([x], x in rr and lo < x and x < hi implies forsome([l], is-diff-at(h, x, l))) implies h(lo) = h(hi) implies forsome([theta in rr], lo < theta and theta < hi and is-diff-at(h, theta, 0)))
- `rpow-star-add` — forall([x_, s_, t_], x_ in rr implies s_ in rr implies t_ in rr implies 0 < x_ implies rpow-star(x_, s_ + t_) = rpow-star(x_, s_) * rpow-star(x_, t_))
- `rpow-star-in-rr` — forall([x_, s_ in rr], 0 < x_ implies rpow-star(x_, s_) in rr)
- `rpow-star-mono-base` — forall([x_, y_, s_], x_ in rr implies y_ in rr implies s_ in rr implies 0 < x_ implies x_ < y_ implies 0 < s_ implies rpow-star(x_, s_) < rpow-star(y_, s_))
- `rpow-star-mono-exp` — forall([x_, s_, t_], x_ in rr implies s_ in rr implies t_ in rr implies 1 < x_ implies s_ < t_ implies rpow-star(x_, s_) < rpow-star(x_, t_))
- `rpow-star-mul-base` — forall([x_, y_, s_], x_ in rr implies y_ in rr implies s_ in rr implies 0 < x_ implies 0 < y_ implies rpow-star(x_ * y_, s_) = rpow-star(x_, s_) * rpow-star(y_, s_))
- `rpow-star-neg` — forall([x_, s_], x_ in rr implies s_ in rr implies 0 < x_ implies rpow-star(x_, -s_) = recip-star(rpow-star(x_, s_)))
- `rpow-star-one` — forall([x_ in rr], 0 < x_ implies rpow-star(x_, 1) = x_)
- `rpow-star-pos` — forall([x_, s_ in rr], 0 < x_ implies 0 < rpow-star(x_, s_))
- `rpow-star-pow` — forall([x_, s_, t_], x_ in rr implies s_ in rr implies t_ in rr implies 0 < x_ implies rpow-star(rpow-star(x_, s_), t_) = rpow-star(x_, s_ * t_))
- `rpow-star-unfold` — forall([x_, s_], rpow-star(x_, s_) == if(0 < x_, r-exp(s_ * log(x_)), if(0 < s_, 0, 1)))
- `rpow-star-value` — forall([x_, s_ in rr], 0 < x_ implies rpow-star(x_, s_) = r-exp(s_ * log(x_)))
- `rpow-star-zero` — forall([x_ in rr], 0 < x_ implies rpow-star(x_, 0) = 1)
- `rpow-star-zero-base` — forall([s_ in rr], 0 < s_ implies rpow-star(0, s_) = 0)
- `rpow-star-zero-zero` — rpow-star(0, 0) = 1
- `rr-abs-bound` — forall([x in rr, c in rr], abs(x) <= c iff -c <= x and x <= c)
- `rr-abs-cases` — forall([x in rr], 0 <= x and abs(x) = x or not(0 <= x) and abs(x) = -x)
- `rr-abs-closed` — forall([a in rr], abs(a) in rr)
- `rr-abs-mult` — forall([a, b], a in rr and b in rr implies abs(a * b) = abs(a) * abs(b))
- `rr-abs-neg` — forall([a in rr], abs(-a) = abs(a))
- `rr-abs-nonneg` — forall([a in rr], 0 <= abs(a))
- `rr-abs-of-neg` — forall([x in rr], x < 0 implies abs(x) = -x)
- `rr-abs-of-nonneg` — forall([x in rr], 0 <= x implies abs(x) = x)
- `rr-abs-of-nonpos` — forall([x in rr], x <= 0 implies abs(x) = -x)
- `rr-abs-prod-bound` — forall([u, v, p, q, m, k, t, e], u in rr implies v in rr implies p in rr implies q in rr implies m in rr implies k in rr implies t in rr implies e in rr implies ((abs(u) <= m and abs(q) <= k) and abs(u - p) <= t and abs(v - q) <= t) and (m + k) * t <= e implies abs(u * v - p * q) <= e)
- `rr-abs-reverse-triangle` — forall([x in rr, y in rr], abs(abs(x) - abs(y)) <= abs(x - y))
- `rr-abs-sub-sym` — forall([u, v], u in rr implies v in rr implies abs(u - v) = abs(v - u))
- `rr-abs-sum-bound` — forall([u, v, p, q, h_, e], u in rr implies v in rr implies p in rr implies q in rr implies h_ in rr implies e in rr implies (abs(u - p) <= h_ and abs(v - q) <= h_) and h_ + h_ = e implies abs(u + v - (p + q)) <= e)
- `rr-abs-triangle` — forall([a, b], a in rr and b in rr implies abs(a + b) <= abs(a) + abs(b))
- `rr-abs-triangle-c` — forall([a, b], a in rr implies b in rr implies abs(a + b) <= abs(a) + abs(b))
- `rr-abs-zero` — forall([a in rr], abs(a) = 0 iff a = 0)
- `rr-add-in-rr` — forall([a_, b_], a_ in rr implies b_ in rr implies a_ + b_ in rr)
- `rr-add-nonneg` — forall([x, y], x in rr implies y in rr implies 0 <= x and 0 <= y implies 0 <= x + y)
- `rr-additive-ag-iden` — iden(normed-field-additive-ag(rr-normed-field)) == 0
- `rr-additive-ag-opr` — opr(normed-field-additive-ag(rr-normed-field)) == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(rr, rr), x_ + y_)
- `rr-cancel-mul-right` — forall([u, v, c], u in rr implies v in rr implies c in rr implies not(c = 0) implies u * c = v * c implies u = v)
- `rr-cauchy-converges` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr)], forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([n_ in nn], forall([m_ in nn, p_ in nn], n_ <= m_ implies n_ <= p_ implies abs(f(m_) - f(p_)) <= eps))) implies converges(rr-ms, f))
- `rr-complete` — is-complete(rr-ms)
- `rr-converges-to-abs` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr), lv in rr], forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([n_ in nn], forall([k_ in nn], n_ <= k_ implies abs(f(k_) - lv) <= eps))) implies converges-to(rr-ms, f, lv))
- `rr-diff-zero-eq` — forall([u in rr, v in rr], 0 = u - v implies u = v)
- `rr-double-nonneg` — forall([x in rr], 0 <= x + x implies 0 <= x)
- `rr-half-ball-nonzero` — forall([c in rr, m in rr, y_ in rr], 0 < m implies m + m = abs(c) implies abs(y_ - c) <= m implies not(y_ = 0))
- `rr-halving-identity` — forall([b in rr], 1 - 2 * b + b = 1 - b)
- `rr-is-metric-space` — is-metric-space(rr-ms)
- `rr-le-abs` — forall([x in rr], x <= abs(x))
- `rr-le-add` — forall([x, y, u, v], x in rr implies y in rr implies u in rr implies v in rr implies x <= y and u <= v implies x + u <= y + v)
- `rr-le-all-pos-nonpos` — forall([x in rr], forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies x <= eps) implies x <= 0)
- `rr-le-cases` — forall([u in rr, v in rr], u <= v implies u < v or u = v)
- `rr-le-diff-nonpos` — forall([u in rr, v in rr], u <= v implies u - v <= 0)
- `rr-le-from-diff-nonneg` — forall([x, y], x in rr implies y in rr implies 0 <= y - x implies x <= y)
- `rr-le-lt-trans` — forall([x in rr, y in rr, z in rr], x <= y and y < z implies x < z)
- `rr-le-max-left` — forall([x in rr, y in rr], x <= max(x, y))
- `rr-le-max-right` — forall([x in rr, y in rr], y <= max(x, y))
- `rr-le-min` — forall([x in rr, y in rr, z in rr], z <= x implies z <= y implies z <= min(x, y))
- `rr-le-ne-lt` — forall([u in rr, v in rr], u <= v and not(u = v) implies u < v)
- `rr-le-neg` — forall([u in rr, v in rr], u <= v implies -v <= -u)
- `rr-le-of-sq-le` — forall([t_, s_], t_ in rr implies s_ in rr implies 0 <= s_ implies t_ * t_ <= s_ * s_ implies t_ <= s_)
- `rr-le-scale-nonneg` — forall([c, x, y], c in rr implies x in rr implies y in rr implies 0 <= c and x <= y implies c * x <= c * y)
- `rr-le-total` — forall([u in rr, v in rr], u <= v or v <= u)
- `rr-le-trans` — forall([x in rr, y in rr, z in rr], x <= y and y <= z implies x <= z)
- `rr-le-trans-c` — forall([x in rr, y in rr, z in rr], x <= y implies y <= z implies x <= z)
- `rr-limit-abs-le` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr), lv in rr, bd in rr], converges-to(rr-ms, f, lv) implies forall([n_ in nn], abs(f(n_)) <= bd) implies abs(lv) <= bd)
- `rr-limit-add` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr), g in fun(nn, rr), h in fun(nn, rr), lv in rr, mv in rr], forall([j_ in nn], h(j_) = f(j_) + g(j_)) implies converges-to(rr-ms, f, lv) implies converges-to(rr-ms, g, mv) implies converges-to(rr-ms, h, lv + mv))
- `rr-limit-le` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr), g in fun(nn, rr), lv in rr, mv in rr], forall([n_ in nn], f(n_) <= g(n_)) implies converges-to(rr-ms, f, lv) implies converges-to(rr-ms, g, mv) implies lv <= mv)
- `rr-limit-ptwise-eq` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr), h in fun(nn, rr), lv in rr], forall([j_ in nn], h(j_) = f(j_)) implies converges-to(rr-ms, f, lv) implies converges-to(rr-ms, h, lv))
- `rr-limit-scale` — forall([c in rr, f in fun(nn, rr), h in fun(nn, rr), lv in rr], forall([j_ in nn], h(j_) = f(j_) * c) implies converges-to(rr-ms, f, lv) implies converges-to(rr-ms, h, lv * c))
- `rr-limit-sub` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr), g in fun(nn, rr), h in fun(nn, rr), lv in rr, mv in rr], forall([j_ in nn], h(j_) = f(j_) - g(j_)) implies converges-to(rr-ms, f, lv) implies converges-to(rr-ms, g, mv) implies converges-to(rr-ms, h, lv - mv))
- `rr-limit-tail-abs-le` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr), lv in rr, v_ in rr, c in rr, n_ in nn], converges-to(rr-ms, f, lv) implies forall([k in nn], n_ <= k implies abs(f(k) - v_) <= c) implies abs(lv - v_) <= c)
- `rr-limit-unique` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr), lm1 in rr, lm2 in rr], converges-to(rr-ms, f, lm1) implies converges-to(rr-ms, f, lm2) implies lm1 = lm2)
- `rr-lt-add` — forall([x, y, u, v], x in rr implies y in rr implies u in rr implies v in rr implies x < y and u <= v implies x + u < y + v)
- `rr-lt-asymm` — forall([a in rr, b in rr], a < b implies not(b < a))
- `rr-lt-diff-neg` — forall([u in rr, v in rr], u < v implies u - v < 0)
- `rr-lt-diff-pos` — forall([u in rr, v in rr], u < v implies 0 < v - u)
- `rr-lt-implies-le` — forall([x in rr, y in rr], x < y implies x <= y)
- `rr-lt-irrefl` — forall([a in rr], not(a < a))
- `rr-lt-le-trans` — forall([x in rr, y in rr, z in rr], x < y and y <= z implies x < z)
- `rr-lt-scale-pos` — forall([c, x, y], c in rr implies x in rr implies y in rr implies 0 < c and x < y implies c * x < c * y)
- `rr-lt-trans` — forall([x in rr, y in rr, z in rr], x < y and y < z implies x < z)
- `rr-lt-trichotomy` — forall([u in rr, v in rr], u < v or u = v or v < u)
- `rr-magnitude-is-abs` — forall([a in rr], magnitude(a) = abs(a))
- `rr-max-cases` — forall([x in rr, y in rr], max(x, y) = x or max(x, y) = y)
- `rr-max-closed` — forall([x in rr, y in rr], max(x, y) in rr)
- `rr-max-le` — forall([x in rr, y in rr, z in rr], x <= z implies y <= z implies max(x, y) <= z)
- `rr-max-lipschitz` — forall([u in rr, v in rr, c in rr], abs(max(u, c) - max(v, c)) <= abs(u - v))
- `rr-min-cases` — forall([x in rr, y in rr], min(x, y) = x or min(x, y) = y)
- `rr-min-closed` — forall([x in rr, y in rr], min(x, y) in rr)
- `rr-min-le-left` — forall([x in rr, y in rr], min(x, y) <= x)
- `rr-min-le-right` — forall([x in rr, y in rr], min(x, y) <= y)
- `rr-min-lipschitz` — forall([u in rr, v in rr, c in rr], abs(min(u, c) - min(v, c)) <= abs(u - v))
- `rr-min-one-mono` — forall([a_ in rr, b_ in rr], a_ <= b_ implies min(1, a_) <= min(1, b_))
- `rr-min-one-subadditive` — forall([x in rr, y in rr], 0 <= x implies 0 <= y implies min(1, x + y) <= min(1, x) + min(1, y))
- `rr-min-pos` — forall([u, v], u in rr implies v in rr implies 0 < u implies 0 < v implies forsome([w_ in rr], 0 < w_ and w_ <= u and w_ <= v))
- `rr-mono-le-limit` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr)], forall([k in nn], f(k) <= f(succ(k))) implies forall([lm_], converges-to(rr-ms, f, lm_) implies forall([m in nn], f(m) <= lm_)))
- `rr-ms-dist` — forall([u_, v_], u_ in rr implies v_ in rr implies (dist(rr-ms))(u_, v_) == abs(u_ - v_))
- `rr-mul-in-rr` — forall([a_, b_], a_ in rr implies b_ in rr implies a_ * b_ in rr)
- `rr-mul-one-mid` — forall([u in rr], (u * 1) * 1 = u)
- `rr-mul-pos` — forall([a in rr, b in rr], 0 < a implies 0 < b implies 0 < a * b)
- `rr-mul-zero` — forall([a in rr], a * 0 = 0)
- `rr-mul-zero-mid` — forall([u in rr, v in rr], (u * 0) * v = 0)
- `rr-neg-abs-le` — forall([x in rr], -abs(x) <= x)
- `rr-neg-eq-zero` — forall([u in rr], -u = 0 implies u = 0)
- `rr-no-zero-divisors` — forall([a, b], a in rr implies b in rr implies a * b = 0 implies a = 0 or b = 0)
- `rr-nonneg-cancel-pos` — forall([c, d], c in rr implies d in rr implies 0 < c implies 0 <= c * d implies 0 <= d)
- `rr-null-scale` — forall([c in rr, f in fun(nn, rr), h in fun(nn, rr)], 0 <= c implies converges-to(rr-ms, f, 0) implies forall([j_ in nn], h(j_) = c * f(j_)) implies converges-to(rr-ms, h, 0))
- `rr-null-squeeze` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr), h in fun(nn, rr)], converges-to(rr-ms, f, 0) implies forall([j_ in nn], 0 <= h(j_) and h(j_) <= f(j_)) implies converges-to(rr-ms, h, 0))
- `rr-null-sum` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr), g in fun(nn, rr), h in fun(nn, rr)], converges-to(rr-ms, f, 0) implies converges-to(rr-ms, g, 0) implies forall([j_ in nn], h(j_) = f(j_) + g(j_)) implies converges-to(rr-ms, h, 0))
- `rr-nvs-is-normed-vector-space` — is-normed-vector-space(rr-nvs)
- `rr-pos-halvable` — forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([d], pos-rr(d) and d + d = eps))
- `rr-pos-ne-zero` — forall([c in rr], 0 < c implies not(c = 0))
- `rr-power-one` — forall([k_ in nn], 1 ^ k_ = 1)
- `rr-prod-le-prod` — forall([a, b, u, v], a in rr implies b in rr implies u in rr implies v in rr implies (0 <= a and a <= b) and 0 <= u and u <= v implies a * u <= b * v)
- `rr-recip-antitone` — forall([u in rr, v in rr], 0 < u implies u <= v implies recip(v) <= recip(u))
- `rr-recip-one` — recip(1) = 1
- `rr-recip-pos` — forall([a in rr], 0 < a implies 0 < recip(a))
- `rr-recip-recip-pos` — forall([c_ in rr], 0 < c_ implies recip(recip(c_)) = c_)
- `rr-recip-solve` — forall([c, u, v], c in rr implies u in rr implies v in rr implies not(c = 0) implies u = c * v implies v = recip(c) * u)
- `rr-scalar-ring-add` — add(normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field)) == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(rr, rr), x_ + y_)
- `rr-scalar-ring-carr` — carr(normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field)) == rr
- `rr-scalar-ring-mul` — mul(normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field)) == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(rr, rr), x_ * y_)
- `rr-scalar-ring-neg` — neg(normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field)) == vnb-lambda(x_, rr, -x_)
- `rr-scalar-ring-one` — one(normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field)) == 1
- `rr-scalar-ring-zero` — zero(normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field)) == 0
- `rr-scale-eps` — forall([c in rr, eps in rr], 0 <= c implies pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([d], pos-rr(d) and forall([t in rr], 0 <= t implies t <= d implies c * t <= eps)))
- `rr-scaled-inverse-unique` — forall([a in rr, u in rr, v in rr], a * u = 1 implies (2 * a) * v = 1 implies u = 2 * v)
- `rr-sq-add-le` — forall([u in rr, v in rr], (u + v) * (u + v) <= (u * u + u * u) + (v * v + v * v))
- `rr-sq-nonneg` — forall([x in rr], 0 <= x * x)
- `rr-sub-in-rr` — forall([u, v], u in rr implies v in rr implies u - v in rr)
- `rr-sub-ne-zero` — forall([u in rr, v in rr], not(u = v) implies not(u - v = 0))
- `rr-sup-approx` — forall([s_, d_], s_ subset rr implies forsome([x_], x_ in s_) implies rr-bounded-above(s_) implies d_ in rr implies 0 < d_ implies forsome([w_ in s_], sup(s_) - d_ < w_))
- `rr-upper-of-two` — forall([u, v], u in rr implies v in rr implies forsome([w_ in rr], u <= w_ and v <= w_))
- `rr-upper-of-two-below` — forall([u, v, m], u in rr implies v in rr implies m in rr implies u < m implies v < m implies forsome([w_ in rr], u <= w_ and v <= w_ and w_ < m))
- `rr-zero-add` — forall([u in rr], 0 + u = u)
- `rr-zero-lt-one` — 0 < 1
- `rr-zero-lt-two` — 0 < 2
- `rr-zero-mul` — forall([v in rr], 0 * v = 0)
- `rr-zero-plus` — forall([t_ in rr], 0 + t_ = t_)
- `scale-continuous-at` — forall([c in rr, f, a], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, a) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(x, rr, c * f(x)), a))
- `scale-lam-in-fun` — forall([c, f], c in rr implies f in fun(rr, rr) implies vnb-lambda(x, rr, c * f(x)) in fun(rr, rr))
- `seg-curve-apply` — forall([m, f, a, eta, t_ in rr], (seg-curve(m, f, a, eta))(t_) == f((vadd(m))(a, (act(m))(t_, eta))))
- `seg-curve-in-fun` — forall([m, f, a, eta], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies f in fun(vec(m), rr) implies a in vec(m) implies eta in vec(m) implies seg-curve(m, f, a, eta) in fun(rr, rr))
- `seg-mem-lt` — forall([n_ in nn, j_ in nn], j_ in ord-segment(n_) iff j_ < n_)
- `seg-mem-succ-le` — forall([n_ in nn, j_ in nn], j_ in ord-segment(succ(n_)) iff j_ <= n_)
- `seg0-empty` — empty-set = ord-segment(0)
- `seq-limit-converges-to` — forall([f], converges(rr-ms, f) implies converges-to(rr-ms, f, seq-limit(f)))
- `seq-limit-in-rr` — forall([f], seq-limit(f) in rr)
- `seq-limit-value` — forall([f, lv], converges-to(rr-ms, f, lv) implies seq-limit(f) = lv)
- `sequential-implies-continuous-at` — forall([s, t, f, a], is-metric-space(s) implies is-metric-space(t) implies f in fun(pts(s), pts(t)) implies a in pts(s) implies forall([sq in sqn(pts(s))], converges-to(s, sq, a) implies converges-to(t, compose(f, sq), f(a))) implies is-continuous-at(s, t, f, a))
- `series-block-le` — forall([k in nn, n_ in nn, f in fun(nn, rr), g in fun(nn, rr)], forall([i_ in nn], 0 <= f(i_) and f(i_) <= g(i_)) implies n_ <= k implies series-partial-sum(f, k) - series-partial-sum(f, n_) <= series-partial-sum(g, k) - series-partial-sum(g, n_))
- `series-cauchy-criterion` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr)], series-converges(f) implies forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([bnd in nn], forall([m in nn, n_ in nn], bnd <= m and m <= n_ implies abs(series-partial-sum(f, n_) - series-partial-sum(f, m)) <= eps))))
- `series-converges-sum` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr), g in fun(nn, rr), h in fun(nn, rr)], forall([i_ in nn], 0 <= f(i_)) implies forall([i_ in nn], 0 <= g(i_)) implies forall([i_ in nn], h(i_) = f(i_) + g(i_)) implies series-converges(f) implies series-converges(g) implies series-converges(h))
- `series-converges-to-add` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr), g in fun(nn, rr), h in fun(nn, rr), lv in rr, mv in rr], forall([i_ in nn], h(i_) = f(i_) + g(i_)) implies series-converges-to(f, lv) implies series-converges-to(g, mv) implies series-converges-to(h, lv + mv))
- `series-converges-to-converges` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr), lv in rr], series-converges-to(f, lv) implies series-converges(f))
- `series-head-null` — forall([n_ in nn, u], forall([j_ in nn], u(j_) in fun(nn, rr)) implies forall([i_ in nn], converges-to(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(j_, nn, (u(j_))(i_)), 0)) implies forall([hd in fun(nn, rr)], forall([j_ in nn], hd(j_) = series-partial-sum(u(j_), n_)) implies converges-to(rr-ms, hd, 0)))
- `series-limit-add` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr), g in fun(nn, rr), h in fun(nn, rr)], forall([i_ in nn], h(i_) = f(i_) + g(i_)) implies series-converges(f) implies series-converges(g) implies series-limit(h) = series-limit(f) + series-limit(g))
- `series-limit-converges-to` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr)], series-converges(f) implies series-converges-to(f, series-limit(f)))
- `series-limit-in-rr` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr)], series-converges(f) implies series-limit(f) in rr)
- `series-nonneg-zero-sum` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr)], forall([n_ in nn], 0 <= f(n_)) implies series-converges-to(f, 0) implies forall([n_ in nn], f(n_) = 0))
- `series-partial-sum-abs-le` — forall([k in nn, f in fun(nn, rr)], abs(series-partial-sum(f, k)) <= series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(n_, nn, abs(f(n_))), k))
- `series-partial-sum-add` — forall([k in nn, f in fun(nn, rr), g in fun(nn, rr), h in fun(nn, rr)], forall([i_ in nn], h(i_) = f(i_) + g(i_)) implies series-partial-sum(h, k) = series-partial-sum(f, k) + series-partial-sum(g, k))
- `series-partial-sum-add-ptwise` — forall([n_ in nn, f_, g_, h_], forall([k_ in nn], f_(k_) in rr) implies forall([k_ in nn], g_(k_) in rr) implies forall([k_ in nn], h_(k_) = f_(k_) + g_(k_)) implies series-partial-sum(h_, n_) = series-partial-sum(f_, n_) + series-partial-sum(g_, n_))
- `series-partial-sum-antiderivable` — forall([n_ in nn, s_, ph_, a, b], a in rr and b in rr and a < b implies ph_ in fun(rr, rr) implies forall([j_ in nn], is-antiderivable(s_(j_), a, b)) implies forall([x_ in rr], ph_(x_) == series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(j_, nn, (s_(j_))(x_)), n_)) implies is-antiderivable(ph_, a, b))
- `series-partial-sum-bounded` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr)], forall([i_ in nn], 0 <= f(i_)) implies series-converges(f) implies forsome([bnd in rr], forall([kx in nn], series-partial-sum(f, kx) <= bnd)))
- `series-partial-sum-in-rr` — forall([k in nn, f in fun(nn, rr)], series-partial-sum(f, k) in rr)
- `series-partial-sum-in-rr-ptwise` — forall([n_ in nn, f_], forall([k_ in nn], f_(k_) in rr) implies series-partial-sum(f_, n_) in rr)
- `series-partial-sum-is-ring-sum` — forall([n_ in nn, f_], series-partial-sum(f_, n_) == sum(normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field), f_, n_))
- `series-partial-sum-le-termwise` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr), g in fun(nn, rr)], forall([n_ in nn], f(n_) <= g(n_)) implies forall([k in nn], series-partial-sum(f, k) <= series-partial-sum(g, k)))
- `series-partial-sum-le-termwise-ptwise` — forall([n_ in nn, f_, g_], forall([k_ in nn], f_(k_) in rr) implies forall([k_ in nn], g_(k_) in rr) implies forall([k_ in nn], f_(k_) <= g_(k_)) implies series-partial-sum(f_, n_) <= series-partial-sum(g_, n_))
- `series-partial-sum-mono` — forall([k in nn, m in nn, f in fun(nn, rr)], forall([i_ in nn], 0 <= f(i_)) implies m <= k implies series-partial-sum(f, m) <= series-partial-sum(f, k))
- `series-partial-sum-monotone-nonneg` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr)], forall([n_ in nn], 0 <= f(n_)) implies forall([k in nn], series-partial-sum(f, k) <= series-partial-sum(f, succ(k))))
- `series-partial-sum-nonneg` — forall([k in nn, f in fun(nn, rr)], forall([i_ in nn], 0 <= f(i_)) implies 0 <= series-partial-sum(f, k))
- `series-partial-sum-scale-ptwise` — forall([n_ in nn, c_, f_, h_], c_ in rr implies forall([k_ in nn], f_(k_) in rr) implies forall([k_ in nn], h_(k_) = c_ * f_(k_)) implies series-partial-sum(h_, n_) = c_ * series-partial-sum(f_, n_))
- `series-partial-sum-seq-apply` — forall([f, j in nn], (vnb-lambda(k, nn, series-partial-sum(f, k)))(j) == series-partial-sum(f, j))
- `series-partial-sum-seq-in-fun` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr)], vnb-lambda(k, nn, series-partial-sum(f, k)) in fun(nn, rr))
- `series-partial-sum-succ` — forall([f, k in nn], series-partial-sum(f, k) in rr implies f(k) in rr implies series-partial-sum(f, succ(k)) == series-partial-sum(f, k) + f(k))
- `series-partial-sum-unfold` — forall([f, k], series-partial-sum(f, k) == sum-ag(normed-field-additive-ag(rr-normed-field), f, k))
- `series-partial-sum-weighted-expansion` — forall([n_ in nn, x_, y_, g_, w_, p_, q_, s_], x_ in rr implies y_ in rr implies forall([k_ in zz], g_(k_) in rr) implies forall([k_ in zz], w_(k_) in rr) implies forall([k_ in nn], q_(k_) in rr) implies forall([k_ in nn], s_(k_) in rr) implies forall([k_ in nn], p_(k_) = w_(k_) * (x_ * g_(k_ - 1) + y_ * g_(k_))) implies forall([k_ in nn], q_(k_) = w_(succ(k_)) * g_(k_)) implies forall([k_ in nn], s_(k_) = w_(k_) * g_(k_)) implies series-partial-sum(p_, succ(n_)) = x_ * (w_(0) * g_(0 - 1) + series-partial-sum(q_, n_)) + y_ * (series-partial-sum(s_, n_) + w_(n_) * g_(n_)))
- `series-partial-sum-zero` — forall([f], series-partial-sum(f, 0) == 0)
- `series-partial-sum-zero-seq` — forall([k_ in nn], series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(n_, nn, 0), k_) = 0)
- `series-tail-small` — forall([g in fun(nn, rr)], series-converges(g) implies forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([bnd in nn], forall([k in nn], bnd <= k implies series-partial-sum(g, k) - series-partial-sum(g, bnd) <= eps))))
- `series-term-le-sum` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr)], forall([n_ in nn], 0 <= f(n_)) implies forall([lm_], series-converges-to(f, lm_) implies forall([n_ in nn], f(n_) <= lm_)))
- `singleton-membership` — forall([y_, x_], x_ in singleton(y_) iff x_ in set and x_ = y_)
- `singleton-unfold` — forall([y_], singleton(y_) == {y_})
- `size-unfold` — forall([m], [length(m), length(nth(1, m))] == size(m))
- `smith-diagonalization` — forall([a], is-euclidean-ring(a) implies forall([k in nn, n, p], n in nn implies p in mat(k, n, carr(a)) implies forsome([d], mat-equiv(a, k, n, p, d) and is-diagonal(a, k, n, d))))
- `smith-normal-form` — forall([a], is-euclidean-ring(a) implies forall([k in nn, n, p], n in nn implies p in mat(k, n, carr(a)) implies forsome([d, kk in nn], mat-equiv(a, k, n, p, d) and smith-staircase(a, k, n, d, kk))))
- `span-is-submodule` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([n, u in mat(n, 1, vec(md))], is-submodule(md, span(md, n, u))))
- `spans-fg-base` — forall([md], is-module(md) and is-euclidean-ring(scal(md)) implies forall([u in mat(0, 1, vec(md)), bm], is-submodule(md, bm) implies spans(md, 0, u, bm) implies forall([sm], is-submodule(md, sm) implies sm subset bm implies forsome([k], (k in nn and k <= 0) and forsome([w in mat(k, 1, vec(md))], spans(md, k, w, sm))))))
- `spans-fg-step` — forall([md], is-module(md) and is-euclidean-ring(scal(md)) implies forall([n in nn], forall([u in mat(n, 1, vec(md)), bm], is-submodule(md, bm) implies spans(md, n, u, bm) implies forall([sm], is-submodule(md, sm) implies sm subset bm implies forsome([k], (k in nn and k <= n) and forsome([w in mat(k, 1, vec(md))], spans(md, k, w, sm))))) implies forall([u in mat(succ(n), 1, vec(md)), bm], is-submodule(md, bm) implies spans(md, succ(n), u, bm) implies forall([sm], is-submodule(md, sm) implies sm subset bm implies forsome([k], (k in nn and k <= succ(n)) and forsome([w in mat(k, 1, vec(md))], spans(md, k, w, sm)))))))
- `spans-span` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([n, u in mat(n, 1, vec(md))], spans(md, n, u, span(md, n, u))))
- `spans-submodule-fg` — forall([md], is-module(md) and is-euclidean-ring(scal(md)) implies forall([n in nn, u in mat(n, 1, vec(md)), bm], is-submodule(md, bm) implies spans(md, n, u, bm) implies forall([sm], is-submodule(md, sm) implies sm subset bm implies forsome([k], (k in nn and k <= n) and forsome([w in mat(k, 1, vec(md))], spans(md, k, w, sm))))))
- `spans-transport` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([n, u, pm, sm], u in mat(n, 1, vec(md)) implies is-invertible-mat(scal(md), n, pm) implies is-submodule(md, sm) implies spans(md, n, u, sm) implies spans(md, n, matact(md, pm, u), sm)))
- `sq-continuous-at` — forall([t in rr], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(z_, rr, z_ * z_), t))
- `sq-fun-in-fun` — vnb-lambda(z_, rr, z_ * z_) in fun(rr, rr)
- `sqrt-char` — forall([a, x_], a in rr and 0 <= a implies x_ in rr and 0 <= x_ implies x_ * x_ = a implies sqrt(a) = x_)
- `sqrt-exists` — forall([a], a in rr and 0 <= a implies forsome([x_ in rr], 0 <= x_ and x_ * x_ = a))
- `sqrt-mono` — forall([a], a in rr and 0 <= a implies forall([b], b in rr and a <= b implies sqrt(a) <= sqrt(b)))
- `sqrt-mul` — forall([a], a in rr and 0 <= a implies forall([b], b in rr and 0 <= b implies sqrt(a * b) = sqrt(a) * sqrt(b)))
- `sqrt-nonneg` — forall([a], a in rr and 0 <= a implies sqrt(a) in rr and 0 <= sqrt(a))
- `sqrt-of-sq` — forall([a in rr], sqrt(a * a) = abs(a))
- `sqrt-prop` — forall([a], a in rr and 0 <= a implies sqrt(a) in rr and 0 <= sqrt(a) and sqrt(a) * sqrt(a) = a)
- `sqrt-sq` — forall([a], a in rr and 0 <= a implies sqrt(a) * sqrt(a) = a)
- `sqrt-unique` — forall([x_, y_], x_ in rr and y_ in rr implies 0 <= x_ and 0 <= y_ implies x_ * x_ = y_ * y_ implies x_ = y_)
- `sqrt-zero` — sqrt(0) = 0
- `sqrt2-irrational` — forall([p in nn, q in nn], not(q = 0) implies not(p * p = 2 * (q * q)))
- `sqrt3-irrational` — forall([p in nn, q in nn], not(q = 0) implies not(p * p = 3 * (q * q)))
- `strict-increase-injective` — forall([f, s], is-strictly-increasing-on(f, s) implies forall([u in s, v in s], f(u) = f(v) implies u = v))
- `strictly-below-irreflexive` — forall([porel, x_], not(is-strictly-below(porel, x_, x_)))
- `strictly-below-not-equal` — forall([porel, x_, y_], x_ = y_ implies not(is-strictly-below(porel, x_, y_)))
- `strictly-below-transitive` — forall([grd, porel, x_, y_, z_], is-partial-order(grd, porel) implies x_ in grd implies y_ in grd implies z_ in grd implies is-strictly-below(porel, x_, y_) implies is-strictly-below(porel, y_, z_) implies is-strictly-below(porel, x_, z_))
- `sub-continuous-at` — forall([g, h, a], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, g, a) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, h, a) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(x, rr, g(x) - h(x)), a))
- `sub-lam-in-fun` — forall([g, h], g in fun(rr, rr) implies h in fun(rr, rr) implies vnb-lambda(x, rr, g(x) - h(x)) in fun(rr, rr))
- `subclass-of-set-is-set` — forall([a, b in set], a subset b implies a in set)
- `submodule-fg` — forall([md], is-module(md) and is-euclidean-ring(scal(md)) implies forall([n in nn, u in mat(n, 1, vec(md))], generates(md, n, u) implies forall([sm], is-submodule(md, sm) implies forsome([k], (k in nn and k <= n) and forsome([w in mat(k, 1, vec(md))], spans(md, k, w, sm))))))
- `submodule-intersection` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([s1], is-submodule(md, s1) implies forall([s2], is-submodule(md, s2) implies is-submodule(md, intersection(s1, s2)))))
- `submodule-nvs-module-view` — forall([m_, t_], is-submodule(m_, t_) implies is-submodule(normed-vector-space-as-module(m_), t_))
- `subseq-of-convergent` — forall([s, f, phi, l], converges-to(s, f, l) implies strictly-mono-nn(phi) implies converges-to(s, subseq(f, phi), l))
- `subsequence-principle` — forall([s, f, l], is-metric-space(s) implies f in fun(nn, pts(s)) implies l in pts(s) implies forall([phi], strictly-mono-nn(phi) implies forsome([psi], strictly-mono-nn(psi) and converges-to(s, subseq(subseq(f, phi), psi), l))) implies converges-to(s, f, l))
- `subset-mem` — forall([a, b, x_], a subset b implies x_ in a implies x_ in b)
- `subset-mem-fwd` — forall([a, b, x], a subset b implies x in a implies x in b)
- `subset-refl` — forall([a_], a_ subset a_)
- `subset-trans` — forall([a, b, c], a subset b implies b subset c implies a subset c)
- `subsqn-preserves-sqn` — forall([a, f, y], f in sqn(a) implies subsqn(y, f) implies y in sqn(a))
- `sum-ag-rr-in-rr` — forall([k in nn, f in fun(nn, rr)], sum-ag(normed-field-additive-ag(rr-normed-field), f, k) in rr)
- `sum-continuous-at` — forall([g, h, a], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, g, a) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, h, a) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(x, rr, g(x) + h(x)), a))
- `sum-expansion` — forall([n in nn, r], is-commutative-ring(r) implies forall([x in carr(r), y in carr(r), g in fun(zz, carr(r))], sum(r, vnb-lambda(k, nn, (add(r))((mul(r))(x, g(k - 1)), (mul(r))(y, g(k)))), succ(n)) = (add(r))((add(r))((mul(r))((add(r))(x, y), sum(r, g, n)), (mul(r))(x, g(0 - 1))), (mul(r))(y, g(n)))))
- `sum-lam-in-fun` — forall([g, h], g in fun(rr, rr) implies h in fun(rr, rr) implies vnb-lambda(x, rr, g(x) + h(x)) in fun(rr, rr))
- `supp-in-set` — forall([a_, m_, f_], carr(m_) in set implies supp(a_, m_, f_) in set)
- `supp-membership` — forall([a_, m_, f_, x_], x_ in supp(a_, m_, f_) iff x_ in carr(m_) and not(f_(x_) = zero(a_)))
- `supp-unfold` — forall([a_, m_, f_], supp(a_, m_, f_) == {x_ in carr(m_): not(f_(x_) = zero(a_))})
- `swap-to-corner` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([m, n, a, i0, j0], a in mat(m, n, carr(r)) implies 1 in interval(1, m) implies 1 in interval(1, n) implies i0 in interval(1, m) implies j0 in interval(1, n) implies not(1 = i0) implies not(1 = j0) implies forsome([b], mat-equiv(r, m, n, a, b) and entry(b, 1, 1) = entry(a, i0, j0))))
- `swap-to-corner-gen` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([m, n, a, i0, j0], a in mat(m, n, carr(r)) implies 1 in interval(1, m) implies 1 in interval(1, n) implies i0 in interval(1, m) implies j0 in interval(1, n) implies forsome([b], mat-equiv(r, m, n, a, b) and entry(b, 1, 1) = entry(a, i0, j0))))
- `taylor-center-partial-sum` — forall([n in nn, f, x in rr], forall([k], k in nn and k <= n implies (nth-deriv(f, k))(x) in rr) implies series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(k, nn, ((nth-deriv(f, k))(x) * (x - x) ^ k) * recip(factorial(k))), succ(n)) = f(x))
- `taylor-center-term-at-succ` — forall([f, x in rr, m in nn], (nth-deriv(f, succ(m)))(x) in rr implies (vnb-lambda(k, nn, ((nth-deriv(f, k))(x) * (x - x) ^ k) * recip(factorial(k))))(succ(m)) = 0)
- `taylor-center-term-at-zero` — forall([f, x in rr], f(x) in rr implies (vnb-lambda(k, nn, ((nth-deriv(f, k))(x) * (x - x) ^ k) * recip(factorial(k))))(0) = f(x))
- `taylor-lagrange` — forall([f, a, x, n], f in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and x in rr and n in nn and a < x implies taylor-differentiable(f, a, x, n) implies forsome([theta in rr], a < theta and theta < x and factorial(succ(n)) * (f(x) - taylor-poly(f, a, n, x)) = (nth-deriv(f, succ(n)))(theta) * (x - a) ^ succ(n)))
- `taylor-poly-at-center` — forall([f, x, n], x in rr implies n in nn implies forall([k], k in nn and k <= n implies (nth-deriv(f, k))(x) in rr) implies taylor-poly(f, x, n, x) = f(x))
- `totally-bounded-has-cauchy-subsequence` — forall([s], totally-bounded(s) implies forall([f in fun(nn, pts(s))], forsome([phi], strictly-mono-nn(phi) and is-cauchy-seq(s, subseq(f, phi)))))
- `trinum-mono` — forall([b_ in nn, a_ in nn], a_ <= b_ implies trinum(a_) <= trinum(b_))
- `trinum-type` — forall([n_ in nn], trinum(n_) in nn)
- `triple-entry-left` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, r, row, col], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(a)) implies r in mat(k, l, carr(a)) implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies entry(matmul(a, matmul(a, p, q), r), row, col) = finsum(ring-additive-ag(a), vnb-lambda(c, interval(1, k), finsum(ring-additive-ag(a), vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), (vnb-lambda(z, cartesian(interval(1, k), interval(1, n)), (mul(a))((mul(a))(entry(p, row, nth(2, z)), entry(q, nth(2, z), nth(1, z))), entry(r, nth(1, z), col))))([c, j])), interval(1, n))), interval(1, k))))
- `triple-entry-right` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, r, row, col], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(a)) implies r in mat(k, l, carr(a)) implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies entry(matmul(a, p, matmul(a, q, r)), row, col) = finsum(ring-additive-ag(a), vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), finsum(ring-additive-ag(a), vnb-lambda(c, interval(1, k), (vnb-lambda(z, cartesian(interval(1, k), interval(1, n)), (mul(a))((mul(a))(entry(p, row, nth(2, z)), entry(q, nth(2, z), nth(1, z))), entry(r, nth(1, z), col))))([c, j])), interval(1, k))), interval(1, n))))
- `trunc-metric-bounded` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([u_, v_], u_ in pts(s) implies v_ in pts(s) implies (dist(trunc-metric(s)))(u_, v_) <= 1))
- `trunc-metric-dist` — forall([s, u_, v_], u_ in pts(s) implies v_ in pts(s) implies (dist(trunc-metric(s)))(u_, v_) == min(1, (dist(s))(u_, v_)))
- `trunc-metric-id-uniformly-continuous` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies is-uniformly-continuous(s, trunc-metric(s), vnb-lambda(a_, pts(s), a_)))
- `trunc-metric-id-uniformly-continuous-back` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies is-uniformly-continuous(trunc-metric(s), s, vnb-lambda(a_, pts(s), a_)))
- `trunc-metric-is-metric-space` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies is-metric-space(trunc-metric(s)))
- `trunc-metric@preimage` — forall([s, f, v], preimage(trunc-metric(s), f, v) == preimage(s, f, v))
- `tuples-induction` — forall([a, c], [] in c and forall([x, m], x in a and m in tuples(a) and m in c implies cons(x, m) in c) implies forall([l in tuples(a)], l in c))
- `unif-cauchy-cont-implies-uniform-limit` — forall([s, fam in fun(nn, fun(pts(s), rr))], forall([k in nn], is-continuous(s, rr-ms, fam(k))) implies is-unif-cauchy(s, fam) implies forsome([g in fun(pts(s), rr)], is-continuous(s, rr-ms, g) and converges-uniformly(s, fam, g)))
- `uniform-cauchy-limit` — forall([g in fun(nn, fun(rr, rr)), s_], forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([n_ in nn], forall([k_ in nn, l_ in nn], n_ <= k_ implies n_ <= l_ implies forall([x_ in rr], x_ in s_ implies abs((g(k_))(x_) - (g(l_))(x_)) <= eps)))) implies forsome([h in fun(rr, rr)], forall([x_ in rr], x_ in s_ implies converges-to(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(k_, nn, (g(k_))(x_)), h(x_))) and forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([n_ in nn], forall([k_ in nn], n_ <= k_ implies forall([x_ in rr], x_ in s_ implies abs(h(x_) - (g(k_))(x_)) <= eps))))))
- `uniform-limit-continuous` — forall([s, fam, g], fam in fun(nn, fun(pts(s), rr)) implies g in fun(pts(s), rr) implies forall([k in nn], is-continuous(s, rr-ms, fam(k))) implies converges-uniformly(s, fam, g) implies is-continuous(s, rr-ms, g))
- `uniform-limit-continuous-at` — forall([fam in fun(nn, fun(rr, rr)), g in fun(rr, rr), t_ in rr, rho in rr], 0 < rho implies forall([k in nn], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, fam(k), t_)) implies forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([cp_ in nn], forall([k in nn], cp_ <= k implies forall([x_ in rr], abs(x_ - t_) <= rho implies abs(g(x_) - (fam(k))(x_)) <= eps)))) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, g, t_))
- `uniform-witness-diff-at` — forall([gfam in fun(nn, fun(rr, rr)), wfam in fun(nn, fun(rr, rr)), fc in fun(rr, rr), lv in rr, a in rr, b in rr, t_ in rr, rho in rr], 0 < rho implies forall([y_ in rr], abs(y_ - t_) <= rho implies y_ in ccint(a, b)) implies t_ in ccint(a, b) implies forall([k_ in nn], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, wfam(k_), t_)) implies forall([k_ in nn, y_ in rr], y_ in ccint(a, b) implies (gfam(k_))(y_) - (gfam(k_))(t_) = (wfam(k_))(y_) * (y_ - t_)) implies forall([y_ in rr], y_ in ccint(a, b) implies converges-to(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(k_, nn, (gfam(k_))(y_)), fc(y_))) implies forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([n_ in nn], forall([k_ in nn, l_ in nn], n_ <= k_ implies n_ <= l_ implies forall([x_ in rr], x_ in ccint(a, b) implies abs((wfam(k_))(x_) - (wfam(l_))(x_)) <= eps)))) implies converges-to(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(k_, nn, (wfam(k_))(t_)), lv) implies is-diff-at(fc, t_, lv))
- `union-as-disjoint` — forall([a_, b_], union(a_, b_) = union(a_, complement-in(b_, a_)))
- `union-assoc` — forall([a_, b_, c_], union(union(a_, b_), c_) = union(a_, union(b_, c_)))
- `union-comm` — forall([a_, b_], union(a_, b_) = union(b_, a_))
- `union-complement-in` — forall([a_, b_], a_ subset b_ implies b_ = union(a_, complement-in(b_, a_)))
- `union-empty-right` — forall([a_], union(a_, empty-set) = a_)
- `union-right-subset` — forall([a_, s_], s_ subset union(a_, s_))
- `union-singleton-absorb` — forall([s_, y_ in s_], union(pair(y_, y_), s_) = s_)
- `union-singleton-mem` — forall([y_, s_], y_ in set implies y_ in union(pair(y_, y_), s_))
- `union-singleton-subset` — forall([b_, y_, s_], y_ in b_ implies s_ subset b_ implies union(pair(y_, y_), s_) subset b_)
- `vector-taylor-remainder-bound` — forall([m, f, a, x, n], is-normed-vector-space(m) and is-finite-dimensional(normed-vector-space-as-module(m)) and f in fun(rr, vec(m)) and a in rr and x in rr and n in nn and a < x implies taylor-differentiable-v(m, f, a, x, n) implies forsome([theta in rr], a < theta and theta < x and factorial(succ(n)) * (vnrm(m))((vadd(m))(f(x), (vneg(m))(taylor-poly-v(m, f, a, x, n)))) <= (vnrm(m))((nth-deriv-v(m, f, succ(n)))(theta)) * (x - a) ^ succ(n)))
- `vnrm-real` — forall([m, w_], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies w_ in vec(m) implies (vnrm(m))(w_) in rr)
- `whole-module-is-submodule` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies is-submodule(md, vec(md)))
- `zen-exhausts` — forall([s in set], forsome([alpha in ord], not(forsome([x], x in {y_ in s: forall([c_], ord-lt(c_, alpha) implies not(zen(s, c_) = y_))}))))
- `zen-hits` — forall([alpha in ord, s], forsome([x], x in {y_ in s: forall([c_], ord-lt(c_, alpha) implies not(zen(s, c_) = y_))}) implies zen(s, alpha) in {y_ in s: forall([c_], ord-lt(c_, alpha) implies not(zen(s, c_) = y_))})
- `zen-step` — forall([alpha in ord, x], zen(x, alpha) = choice({y_ in x: forall([c_], ord-lt(c_, alpha) implies not(zen(x, c_) = y_))}))
- `zero-is-antiderivable` — forall([a, b], a in rr and b in rr and a < b implies is-antiderivable(vnb-lambda(x, rr, 0), a, b))
- `zero-seq-in-fun` — vnb-lambda(n_, nn, 0) in fun(nn, rr)
- `zero-series-converges` — series-converges(vnb-lambda(n_, nn, 0))
- `zorn-chain-plus-keepset` — forall([phi, grd, porel, kset, alpha], is-partial-order(grd, porel) implies is-chain(grd, porel, kset) implies is-chain(grd, porel, union(kset, keep-set(phi, grd, porel, kset, alpha))))
- `zorn-empty-is-chain` — forall([grd, porel], grd in set implies is-chain(grd, porel, empty-set))
- `zorn-lemma` — forall([grd, porel], is-partial-order(grd, porel) implies forsome([w_], w_ in grd) implies forall([ch_], is-chain(grd, porel, ch_) implies forsome([u_], is-upper-bound(grd, porel, ch_, u_))) implies forsome([mx_], is-maximal(grd, porel, mx_)))
- `zorn-zkept-is-chain` — forall([alpha in ord, phi, grd, porel], is-partial-order(grd, porel) implies is-chain(grd, porel, zkept(phi, grd, porel, alpha)))
- `zorn-zkept-limit-is-chain` — forall([phi, grd, porel, lam], is-partial-order(grd, porel) implies limit-ord(lam) implies forall([beta in ord-segment(lam)], is-chain(grd, porel, zkept(phi, grd, porel, beta))) implies is-chain(grd, porel, big-union(beta2, ord-segment(lam), zkept(phi, grd, porel, beta2))))
- `zorn-zkept-monotone` — forall([b in ord, phi, grd, porel, a in ord], ord-le(a, b) implies zkept(phi, grd, porel, a) subset zkept(phi, grd, porel, b))
- `zup-injective` — forall([grd, porel], is-partial-order(grd, porel) and forsome([w_], w_ in grd) and forall([ch_], is-chain(grd, porel, ch_) implies forsome([u_], is-upper-bound(grd, porel, ch_, u_))) and forall([x_ in grd], forsome([y_ in grd], is-strictly-below(porel, x_, y_))) implies forall([a1_, a2_], a1_ in ord implies a2_ in ord implies zup(grd, porel, a1_) = zup(grd, porel, a2_) implies a1_ = a2_))
- `zup-tower` — forall([a_ in ord, grd, porel], is-partial-order(grd, porel) and forsome([w_], w_ in grd) and forall([ch_], is-chain(grd, porel, ch_) implies forsome([u_], is-upper-bound(grd, porel, ch_, u_))) and forall([x_ in grd], forsome([y_ in grd], is-strictly-below(porel, x_, y_))) implies zup(grd, porel, a_) in grd and forall([b_], ord-lt(b_, a_) implies zup(grd, porel, b_) in grd and is-strictly-below(porel, zup(grd, porel, b_), zup(grd, porel, a_))))
- `zz-add-cancel` — forall([a in zz, b in zz, c in zz], a + c = b + c implies a = b)
- `zz-bezout` — forall([a, b], a in zz implies b in zz implies forsome([d in zz-bezout-set(a, b)], zz-divides(d, a) and zz-divides(d, b)))
- `zz-bezout-set-is-ideal` — forall([a, b], a in zz implies b in zz implies is-ideal(zz-ring, zz-bezout-set(a, b)))
- `zz-in-rr` — forall([k_ in zz], k_ in rr)
- `zz-is-commutative-ring` — is-commutative-ring(zz-ring)
- `zz-is-integral-domain` — is-integral-domain(zz-ring)
- `zz-is-ring` — is-ring(zz-ring)
- `zz-mul-cancel-zero` — forall([a in zz, b in zz], a * b = 0 implies not(b = 0) implies a = 0)
- `zz-no-zero-divisors` — forall([a in zz, b in zz], a * b = 0 implies a = 0 or b = 0)
- `zz-sub-in-zz` — forall([u_, v_], u_ in zz implies v_ in zz implies u_ - v_ in zz)

## Proof Support Set

Results we accept without a machine proof in VNB.  Logically treated as theorems.

- `abelian-group-assoc` — forall([s], is-abelian-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s), c in carr(s)], (opr(s))((opr(s))(a, b), c) = (opr(s))(a, (opr(s))(b, c))))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `abelian-group-inverse-unique` — forall([s], is-abelian-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], (opr(s))(a, b) = iden(s) implies b = (inv(s))(a)))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `abelian-group-right-id` — forall([s], is-abelian-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], (opr(s))(a, iden(s)) = a))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `abs-nonneg-le` — forall([a, c], a in rr implies 0 <= a implies abs(a) <= c implies a <= c)  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `absolute-summable-implies-summable` — forall([grp], is-normed-ag(grp) and is-complete(nag-metric-space(grp)) implies forall([f], f in fun(dom(f), carr(grp)) and is-absolutely-summable(grp, f) implies is-summable(grp, f)))  _[warrant: informal]_
- `amgm-2-sqrt` — forall([a], a in rr and 0 <= a implies forall([b], b in rr and 0 <= b implies sqrt(a * b) <= (/)(a + b, 2)))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `ascoli-arzela-sequential` — forall([s, fam], is-compact(s) implies fam in fun(nn, fun(pts(s), rr)) implies forall([k in nn], is-continuous(s, rr-ms, fam(k))) implies is-equicontinuous(s, rr-ms, fam) implies pointwise-bounded(s, fam) implies forsome([del], strictly-mono-nn(del) and forsome([g in fun(pts(s), rr)], is-continuous(s, rr-ms, g) and converges-uniformly(s, subseq(fam, del), g))))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `baire-category` — forall([s], is-complete(s) implies is-nonmeager(s, pts(s)))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `ball-2r-triangle` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([x in pts(s), r], r in rr and 0 <= r and not(0 = r) implies forall([y in ball(s, x, r), z in ball(s, x, r)], (dist(s))(y, z) <= r + r and not((dist(s))(y, z) = r + r))))  _[warrant: proof]_
- `ball-center-in` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([x in pts(s), r], r in rr and 0 <= r and not(0 = r) implies x in ball(s, x, r)))  _[warrant: proof]_
- `ball-cover-is-open-cover` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([r], r in rr and 0 <= r and not(0 = r) implies is-open-cover(s, ball-cover(s, r))))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `ball-cover-mem-fwd` — forall([s, r, u in ball-cover(s, r)], forsome([c in pts(s)], ball(s, c, r) = u))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `ball-is-open` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([x in pts(s), r], r in rr and 0 <= r and not(0 = r) implies is-open(s, ball(s, x, r))))  _[warrant: proof]_
- `ball-is-set` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([x, r], ball(s, x, r) in set))  _[warrant: proof]_
- `ball-mem-from-le` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([x, y, d, r], y in pts(s) and d in rr and r in rr and (dist(s))(x, y) <= d and d < r implies y in ball(s, x, r)))  _[warrant: proof]_
- `ball-membership` — forall([s, x, r, y], y in ball(s, x, r) iff y in pts(s) and (dist(s))(x, y) <= r and not((dist(s))(x, y) = r))  _[warrant: proof]_
- `ball-point-le` — forall([s, c, r, w, p in w], ball(s, c, r) = w implies (dist(s))(c, p) <= r)  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `ball-point-ne` — forall([s, c, r, w, p in w], ball(s, c, r) = w implies not((dist(s))(c, p) = r))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `bdd-fn-le-arg` — forall([t in rr], 0 <= t implies (/)(t, 1 + t) <= t)  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `bdd-fn-lt-one` — forall([t in rr], 0 <= t implies (/)(t, 1 + t) < 1)  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `bdd-fn-mono` — forall([s in rr, t in rr], 0 <= s and s <= t implies (/)(s, 1 + s) <= (/)(t, 1 + t))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `bdd-fn-nonneg` — forall([t in rr], 0 <= t implies 0 <= (/)(t, 1 + t))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `bdd-fn-subadd` — forall([a in rr, b in rr], 0 <= a and 0 <= b implies (/)(a + b, 1 + (a + b)) <= (/)(a, 1 + a) + (/)(b, 1 + b))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `bdd-linfun-abs-real` — forall([m, f, x], is-bounded-linear-functional(m, f) implies x in vec(m) implies abs(f(x)) in rr)  _[warrant: reference]_
- `bdd-metric-bounded` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([x in pts(s), y in pts(s)], (dist(bdd-metric(s)))(x, y) < 1))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `bdd-metric-carrier` — forall([s], pts(bdd-metric(s)) == pts(s))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `bdd-metric-distance` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([x in pts(s), y in pts(s)], (dist(bdd-metric(s)))(x, y) = (/)((dist(s))(x, y), 1 + (dist(s))(x, y))))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `bdd-metric-id-bicontinuous` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies is-continuous(s, bdd-metric(s), vnb-lambda(x, pts(s), x)) and is-continuous(bdd-metric(s), s, vnb-lambda(x, pts(bdd-metric(s)), x)))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `bdd-metric-is-bounded-metric-space` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies is-bounded-metric-space(bdd-metric(s)))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `bdd-metric-is-metric-space` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies is-metric-space(bdd-metric(s)))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `bdd-metric-preserves-metric-top` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies metric-top(bdd-metric(s)) == metric-top(s))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `bernoulli-rpow` — forall([x], x in rr and 0 < 1 + x implies forall([b], b in qq and 1 <= b implies 1 + b * x <= rpow(1 + x, b)))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `blf-app-real` — forall([m, g, v], is-bounded-linear-functional(m, g) implies v in vec(m) implies g(v) in rr)  _[warrant: reference]_
- `block-family` — forall([s], totally-bounded(s) implies forall([f in fun(nn, pts(s)), rad], null-rr-seq(rad) implies forsome([blk in fun(nn, inf-subsets(nn))], forall([k in nn], blk(succ(k)) subset blk(k)) and forall([k in nn], forsome([c in pts(s)], forall([i in blk(k)], f(i) in ball(s, c, rad(k))))))))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `block-type` — forall([m, n, x, p, k, l], k in nn implies l in nn implies p in mat(m, n, x) implies k <= m implies l <= n implies block(p, k, l) in mat(k, l, x))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `border-entry-11` — forall([a, b, m, p, q], entry(border(a, b, m, p, q), 1, 1) = b)  _[warrant: reference]_
- `border-entry-1j` — forall([a, b, m, p, q, j in interval(1, succ(q))], not(j = 1) implies entry(border(a, b, m, p, q), 1, j) = zero(a))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `border-entry-block` — forall([a, b, m, p, q, i, j], i in interval(1, p) implies j in interval(1, q) implies entry(border(a, b, m, p, q), succ(i), succ(j)) = entry(m, i, j))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `border-entry-block2` — forall([a, b, m, p, q, i, j], i in interval(1, succ(p)) implies not(i = 1) implies j in interval(1, succ(q)) implies not(j = 1) implies entry(border(a, b, m, p, q), i, j) = entry(m, nn-minus(i, 1), nn-minus(j, 1)))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `border-entry-i1` — forall([a, b, m, p, q, i in interval(1, succ(p))], not(i = 1) implies entry(border(a, b, m, p, q), i, 1) = zero(a))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `border-type` — forall([a, b, m, p, q], p in nn implies q in nn implies b in carr(a) implies m in mat(p, q, carr(a)) implies border(a, b, m, p, q) in mat(succ(p), succ(q), carr(a)))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `bounded-metrizable-iff-gauge-countable` — forall([s], is-top-space(s) implies forsome([md], is-bounded-metric-space(md) and metric-top(md) == s) iff is-hausdorff(s) and is-gauge-countable(s))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `bt-lt-succ` — forall([n in nn], n < succ(n))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `bt-mul-comm` — forall([r], is-commutative-ring(r) implies forall([a in carr(r), b in carr(r)], (mul(r))(a, b) = (mul(r))(b, a)))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `bt-neg1-in-zz` — 0 - 1 in zz  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `bt-neg1-neg` — 0 - 1 < 0  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `bt-nn-in-zz` — forall([n in nn], n in zz)  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `bt-one-in-carr` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies one(r) in carr(r))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `bt-succ-in-nn` — forall([n in nn], succ(n) in nn)  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `bt-succ-minus-1` — forall([n in zz], succ(n) - 1 = n)  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `bt-sum-in-carr-zz` — forall([r], is-commutative-ring(r) implies forall([g in fun(zz, carr(r)), n in nn], sum(r, g, n) in carr(r)))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `caratheodory-measurable-sigma-algebra` — forall([omega, mu], is-outer-measure(omega, mu) implies is-sigma-algebra(omega, caratheodory-sets(omega, mu)))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `caratheodory-measure` — forall([omega, mu], is-outer-measure(omega, mu) implies is-measure(omega, caratheodory-sets(omega, mu), res(mu, caratheodory-sets(omega, mu))))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `caratheodory-null-measurable` — forall([omega, mu, es], is-outer-measure(omega, mu) implies es subset omega implies mu(es) = 0 implies is-caratheodory-measurable(omega, mu, es))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `caratheodory-superadditive-suffices` — forall([omega, mu, es], is-outer-measure(omega, mu) implies es subset omega implies forall([a_ in power(omega)], eplus(mu(intersection(a_, es)), mu(difference(a_, es))) <= mu(a_)) implies is-caratheodory-measurable(omega, mu, es))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `card-power-nn` — forall([x], x in set and card(x) in nn implies card(power(x)) in nn)  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `card-singleton` — forall([x in set], card(pair(x, x)) = succ(0))  _[warrant: informal]_
- `card-subset-nn` — forall([x], x in set and card(x) in nn implies forall([s], s in set and forall([z in s], z in x) implies card(s) in nn))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `carrier-is-open` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies is-open(s, pts(s)))  _[warrant: proof]_
- `cauchy-block-estimate` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([c in pts(s), r], pos-rr(r) implies forall([u], u = ball(s, c, r) implies forall([y in u, z in u, d], pos-rr(d) implies forall([eps in rr], r <= d implies d + d = eps implies (dist(s))(y, z) <= eps)))))  _[warrant: proof]_
- `cauchy-cluster-converges` — forall([s, f], is-cauchy-seq(s, f) and forsome([x], cluster-point(s, f, x)) implies converges(s, f))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `cauchy-rapid-subsequence` — forall([s, f, rad], is-cauchy-seq(s, f) and rad in fun(nn, rr) and forall([k in nn], pos-rr(rad(k))) implies forsome([phi in fun(nn, nn)], forall([m in nn, n_ in nn], m < n_ implies phi(m) < phi(n_)) and forall([k in nn], (dist(s))(f(phi(k)), f(phi(succ(k)))) <= rad(k))))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `cauchy-schwarz-finite` — forall([s], s in set and card(s) in nn implies forall([a in fun(s, rr), b in fun(s, rr)], finsum(commutative-ring-additive-ag(rr-normed-field), vnb-lambda(i, s, a(i) * b(i)), s) * finsum(commutative-ring-additive-ag(rr-normed-field), vnb-lambda(i, s, a(i) * b(i)), s) <= finsum(commutative-ring-additive-ag(rr-normed-field), vnb-lambda(i, s, a(i) * a(i)), s) * finsum(commutative-ring-additive-ag(rr-normed-field), vnb-lambda(i, s, b(i) * b(i)), s)))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `cauchy-schwarz-sqrt` — forall([s], s in set and card(s) in nn implies forall([a in fun(s, rr), b in fun(s, rr)], abs(finsum(commutative-ring-additive-ag(rr-normed-field), vnb-lambda(i, s, a(i) * b(i)), s)) <= sqrt(finsum(commutative-ring-additive-ag(rr-normed-field), vnb-lambda(i, s, a(i) * a(i)), s)) * sqrt(finsum(commutative-ring-additive-ag(rr-normed-field), vnb-lambda(i, s, b(i) * b(i)), s))))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `cauchy-seq-is-fun` — forall([s, f], is-cauchy-seq(s, f) implies f in fun(nn, pts(s)))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `cauchy-setoid-is-setoid` — forall([m], is-metric-space(m) implies is-setoid(cauchy-setoid(m)))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `centre-set-contains-choice` — forall([s, r, f, u in f], choice(centres(s, u, r)) in centre-set(s, r, f))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `centre-set-finite` — forall([s, r, f], card(f) in nn implies card(centre-set(s, r, f)) in nn)  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `centres-ball-eq` — forall([s, b, r, c in centres(s, b, r)], ball(s, c, r) = b)  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `centres-in-carrier` — forall([s, b, r, c in centres(s, b, r)], c in pts(s))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `centres-mem-build` — forall([s, b, r, c in pts(s)], ball(s, c, r) = b implies c in centres(s, b, r))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `choose-0-succ` — forall([k in nn], choose(0, succ(k)) = 0)  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `choose-in-nn` — forall([n in nn, m in nn], choose(n, m) in nn)  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `choose-n-0` — forall([n in nn], choose(n, 0) = succ(0))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `choose-succ` — forall([n in nn, k in nn], choose(succ(n), succ(k)) = choose(n, k) + choose(n, succ(k)))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `choose-times-factorial` — forall([n in nn, m], m in nn and m <= n implies choose(n, m) * factorial(m) = falling(n, m))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `chosen-centre-is-centre` — forall([s, r, u in ball-cover(s, r)], choice(centres(s, u, r)) in pts(s) and ball(s, choice(centres(s, u, r)), r) = u)  _[warrant: proof]_
- `class-disjoint` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([a in pts(s), b in pts(s)], class(s, a) = class(s, b) or intersection(class(s, a), class(s, b)) = empty-set))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `class-eq-iff` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([a in pts(s), b in pts(s)], class(s, a) = class(s, b) iff related(s, a, b)))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `class-in-quotient` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([a in pts(s)], class(s, a) in quotient(s)))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `class-is-set` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([a in pts(s)], class(s, a) in set))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `class-self` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([a in pts(s)], a in class(s, a)))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `class-subset-carrier` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([a in pts(s)], class(s, a) subset pts(s)))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `closed-graph-theorem` — forall([m1, fam1, m2, fam2, tt], is-frechet-structure(m1, fam1) implies is-frechet-structure(m2, fam2) implies has-closed-graph(m1, fam1, m2, fam2, tt) implies is-cont-lin(m1, fam1, m2, fam2, tt))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `closed-preimage-implies-continuous` — forall([s, t, f], is-metric-space(s) and is-metric-space(t) and f in fun(pts(s), pts(t)) implies forall([a], is-closed(t, a) implies is-closed(s, preimage(s, f, a))) implies is-continuous(s, t, f))  _[warrant: proof]_
- `co-eq-le-trans` — forall([a, b, c], a = b implies b <= c implies a <= c)  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `co-eq-lt-trans` — forall([a, b, c], a = b implies b < c implies a < c)  _[warrant: well-known]_
