- `ter-outf-type` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, r, row, col], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(a)) implies r in mat(k, l, carr(a)) implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), (mul(a))(entry(p, row, j), entry(matmul(a, q, r), j, col))) in fun(interval(1, n), carr(ring-additive-ag(a)))))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `ter-red-type` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, r, row, col, x], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(a)) implies r in mat(k, l, carr(a)) implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies x in interval(1, n) implies vnb-lambda(c, interval(1, k), (mul(a))((mul(a))(entry(p, row, x), entry(q, x, c)), entry(r, c, col))) in fun(interval(1, k), carr(ring-additive-ag(a)))))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `ter-tout-type` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, r, row, col], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(a)) implies r in mat(k, l, carr(a)) implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), finsum(ring-additive-ag(a), vnb-lambda(c, interval(1, k), (vnb-lambda(z, cartesian(interval(1, k), interval(1, n)), (mul(a))((mul(a))(entry(p, row, nth(2, z)), entry(q, nth(2, z), nth(1, z))), entry(r, nth(1, z), col))))([c, j])), interval(1, k))) in fun(interval(1, n), carr(ring-additive-ag(a)))))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `tuple-cons-decompose` — forall([a, n, l], n in nn and l in tuples(a) and length(l) = succ(n) implies forsome([x in a, m in tuples(a)], length(m) = n and l = cons(x, m)))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `tuple-length-zero` — forall([a, l], l in tuples(a) and length(l) = 0 implies l = [])  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `unif-cauchy-has-uniform-limit` — forall([s, fam in fun(nn, fun(pts(s), rr))], is-unif-cauchy(s, fam) implies forsome([g in fun(pts(s), rr)], converges-uniformly(s, fam, g)))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `uniformly-continuous-is-continuous` — forall([s, t, f], is-uniformly-continuous(s, t, f) implies is-continuous(s, t, f))  _[warrant: informal]_
- `union-empty-left` — forall([a in set], union(empty-set, a) = a)  _[warrant: informal]_
- `union-of-opens-open` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([a, g], forall([i in a], is-open(s, g(i))) implies is-open(s, big-union(i, a, g(i)))))  _[warrant: proof]_
- `unitrow-entry-at` — forall([a, n, i in interval(1, n)], entry(unitrow(a, n, i), 1, i) = one(a))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `unitrow-entry-off` — forall([a, n, i, j], i in interval(1, n) implies j in interval(1, n) implies not(j = i) implies entry(unitrow(a, n, i), 1, j) = zero(a))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `unitrow-type` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([n, i], unitrow(a, n, i) in mat(1, n, carr(a))))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `vec-is-set` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies vec(m) in set)  _[warrant: reference]_
- `vnrm-nonneg` — forall([m, w_], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies w_ in vec(m) implies 0 <= (vnrm(m))(w_))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `vspace-vec-is-set` — forall([m], is-vector-space(m) implies vec(m) in set)  _[warrant: reference]_
- `vtaylor-clear` — forall([rr in rr, gv in rr, nv in rr, pw in rr, n in nn], factorial(succ(n)) * rr = gv * pw implies gv <= nv implies 0 <= pw implies factorial(succ(n)) * rr <= nv * pw)  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `vtaylor-poly-in-vec` — forall([m, f, a, x, n], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies f in fun(rr, vec(m)) implies a in rr implies x in rr implies n in nn implies taylor-poly-v(m, f, a, x, n) in vec(m))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `vtaylor-remainder-in-vec` — forall([m, f, a, x, n], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies f in fun(rr, vec(m)) implies a in rr implies x in rr implies n in nn implies (vadd(m))(f(x), (vneg(m))(taylor-poly-v(m, f, a, x, n))) in vec(m))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `well-ordering-principle` — forall([s in set], forsome([phi], phi in bijection(ord-segment(card(s)), s)))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `young-inequality` — forall([a], a in rr and 0 < a implies forall([b], b in rr and 0 < b implies forall([p], p in qq and 1 < p implies forall([q], q in qq and 1 < q implies (/)(1, p) + (/)(1, q) = 1 implies a * b <= (/)(rpow(a, p), p) + (/)(rpow(b, q), q)))))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `zeromat-type` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n], zeromat(a, m, n) in mat(m, n, carr(a))))  _[warrant: reference]_

## Stated without proof

Not proved in VNB; split by *origin*.  "Axiom" is reserved for the first group — it is NOT the umbrella for this section.

### Axioms — the fixed VNB base

The foundational axioms of VNB set theory (make-vnb-base-theory core + theorem-library/axioms).  Fixed, though not finite (separation/replacement are schemas).  Everything else rests on these.

> **The axioms below are NOT the whole trusted base.**  The set- & class-formation *schemas* (separation `{x∈A|p}`, comprehension `{x|p}`, indexed union `⋃_{z∈A}body`) carry a formula schema in their body and so live in the proof checker as primitive inference rules, not as formulas in this table.  They are documented in `KERNEL-RULES.md`, which is the honest statement of the trusted base: these axioms **plus** the kernel rules.

- `app-graph` — forall([fn_, arg_], fn_(arg_) == iota(val_, [arg_, val_] in fn_))
- `apply-tupling-1` — forall([f, a], f(a) == f([a]))
- `apply-tupling-2` — forall([f, a, b], f(a, b) == f([a, b]))
- `apply-tupling-3` — forall([f, a, b, c], f(a, b, c) == f([a, b, c]))
- `burali-forti` — not(ord in set)
- `card-empty` — card(empty-set) = 0
- `card-finite-bij` — forall([a], a in set and card(a) in nn implies forsome([phi], phi in bijection(ord-segment(card(a)), a)))
- `card-image-injection` — forall([dm, cod, phi], phi in injection(dm, cod) and dm in set and card(dm) in nn implies card(image(phi, dm)) = card(dm))
- `card-in-ord` — forall([a in set], card(a) in ord)
- `card-insert` — forall([a in set, x], x in set and not(x in a) implies card(union(a, pair(x, x))) = succ_ord(card(a)))
- `card-segment` — forall([n in nn], card(ord-segment(n)) = n)
- `card-union-disjoint` — forall([a], a in set and card(a) in nn implies forall([b], b in set and card(b) in nn and intersection(a, b) = empty-set implies card(union(a, b)) = card(a) + card(b)))
- `cartesian-set-iff` — forall([a, b], cartesian(a, b) in set iff a in set and b in set)
- `cc-add-assoc` — forall([a, b, c], a in cc and b in cc and c in cc implies (a + b) + c = a + (b + c))
- `cc-add-closed` — forall([a, b], a in cc and b in cc implies a + b in cc)
- `cc-add-comm` — forall([a, b], a in cc and b in cc implies a + b = b + a)
- `cc-add-zero` — forall([a in cc], a + 0 = a)
- `cc-conjugate-add` — forall([a, b], a in cc and b in cc implies conjugate(a + b) = conjugate(a) + conjugate(b))
- `cc-conjugate-closed` — forall([a in cc], conjugate(a) in cc)
- `cc-conjugate-fixes-rr` — forall([a in rr], conjugate(a) = a)  _[warrant: reference]_
- `cc-conjugate-i` — conjugate(1i) = -1i  _[warrant: reference]_
- `cc-conjugate-involution` — forall([a in cc], conjugate(conjugate(a)) = a)
- `cc-conjugate-mul` — forall([a, b], a in cc and b in cc implies conjugate(a * b) = conjugate(a) * conjugate(b))
- `cc-distributive` — forall([a, b, c], a in cc and b in cc and c in cc implies a * (b + c) = a * b + a * c)
- `cc-generated-by-rr` — forall([z in cc], forsome([x in rr, y in rr], z = x + y * 1i))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `cc-i-in` — 1i in cc  _[warrant: reference]_
- `cc-i-squared` — 1i * 1i = -1  _[warrant: reference]_
- `cc-is-set` — cc in set
- `cc-mul-assoc` — forall([a, b, c], a in cc and b in cc and c in cc implies (a * b) * c = a * (b * c))
- `cc-mul-closed` — forall([a, b], a in cc and b in cc implies a * b in cc)
- `cc-mul-comm` — forall([a, b], a in cc and b in cc implies a * b = b * a)
- `cc-neg-closed` — forall([a in cc], -a in cc)
- `cc-neg-inverse` — forall([a in cc], a + -a = 0)
- `cc-one-in` — 1 in cc
- `cc-one-mul` — forall([a in cc], 1 * a = a)
- `cc-recip-closed` — forall([a], a in cc and not(a = 0) implies recip(a) in cc)
- `cc-recip-inverse` — forall([a], a in cc and not(a = 0) implies a * recip(a) = 1)
- `cc-self-conj-nonneg` — forall([a in cc], a * conjugate(a) in rr and 0 <= a * conjugate(a))
- `cc-self-conj-real` — forall([a in cc], a * conjugate(a) in rr)
- `cc-zero-in` — 0 in cc
- `choice-axiom` — forall([a], forsome([x], x in a) implies choice(a) in a)
- `class-extensionality` — forall([a, b], forall([x], x in a iff x in b) implies a = b)
- `complement-in-membership` — forall([a, b, x], x in complement-in(a, b) iff x in a and not(x in b))
- `complement-in-set-closure` — forall([a, b], a in set implies complement-in(a, b) in set)
- `dom-fun-membership` — forall([a, f in fun(a), x], x in dom(f) iff x in a)
- `dom-membership` — forall([f, x], x in dom(f) iff x in set and f(x) = f(x))
- `dom-of-fun` — forall([a, f], f in fun(a) and a in set implies dom(f) in set and dom(f) = a)
- `empty-set-has-no-members` — forall([x], not(x in empty-set))
- `empty-set-is-set` — empty-set in set
- `eq-subst-membership` — forall([a, b, s], a = b and b in s implies a in s)
- `equality-symmetry` — forall([a, b], a = b implies b = a)
- `equality-transitivity` — forall([a, b, c], a = b and b = c implies a = c)
- `extensionality` — forall([a, b], a in set and b in set implies a = b iff forall([x], x in a iff x in b))
- `finite-set-induction` — forall([c], empty-set in c and forall([s], s in set and card(s) in nn and s in c implies forall([x], x in set and not(x in s) implies union(s, pair(x, x)) in c)) implies forall([s], s in set and card(s) in nn implies s in c))
- `fun-apply-type` — forall([f, a, b, x], f in fun(a, b) and x in a implies f(x) in b)
- `fun-codomain-iff` — forall([a, b, f], f in fun(a, b) iff f in fun(a) and forall([x in a], f(x) in b))
- `fun-domain-apply-def` — forall([a, f, x], f in fun(a) implies f(x) = f(x) iff x in a)
- `fun-domain-elements-are-sets` — forall([a, f], f in fun(a) and a in set implies f in set)
- `fun-domain-extensionality` — forall([a, f, g], f in fun(a) implies g in fun(a) implies forall([x in a], f(x) = g(x)) implies f = g)
- `fun-elements-are-sets` — forall([a, b, f], f in fun(a, b) and a in set implies f in set)
- `fun-no-junk` — forall([a, f in fun(a), z], z in f iff forsome([x, y], z = [x, y] and x in a and f(x) = y))
- `fun-set-iff` — forall([a, b], fun(a, b) in set iff a in set and b in set)
- `image-set` — forall([phi, s in set], image(phi, s) in set)
- `intersection-decompose` — forall([x, as], x in intersection(restvar(as)) iff splice(and, e, as, x in e))
- `intersection-membership` — forall([a, b, x], x in intersection(a, b) iff x in a and x in b)
- `intersection-set-closure` — forall([a, b], a in set or b in set implies intersection(a, b) in set)
- `is-fun-def` — forall([f], is-fun(f) iff forsome([a in set], f in fun(a)))
- `length-in-nn` — forall([a, l in tuples(a)], length(l) in nn)
- `length-of-empty` — length([]) = 0
- `limit-ord-is-sup` — forall([lambda], limit-ord(lambda) implies sup-ord(ord-segment(lambda)) = lambda)
- `list-sethood` — forall([a, l], a in set and l in tuples(a) implies l in set)
- `make-set-empty` — {} = empty-set
- `make-set-membership` — forall([x, l], x in make-set(l) iff x in set and forsome([i in nn], 1 <= i and i <= length(l) and nth(i, l) = x))
- `make-set-sethood` — forall([a, l], l in tuples(a) and a in set implies make-set(l) in set)
- `membership-implies-sethood` — forall([a, b], a in b implies a in set)
- `nn-add-assoc` — forall([a, b, c], a in nn and b in nn and c in nn implies (a + b) + c = a + (b + c))
- `nn-add-closed` — forall([a, b], a in nn and b in nn implies a + b in nn)
- `nn-add-comm` — forall([a, b], a in nn and b in nn implies a + b = b + a)
- `nn-add-zero` — forall([a in nn], a + 0 = a)
- `nn-distributive` — forall([a, b, c], a in nn and b in nn and c in nn implies a * (b + c) = a * b + a * c)
- `nn-induction` — forall([c], 0 in c and forall([n], n in nn and n in c implies succ(n) in c) implies forall([n in nn], n in c))
- `nn-is-set` — nn in set
- `nn-mul-assoc` — forall([a, b, c], a in nn and b in nn and c in nn implies (a * b) * c = a * (b * c))
- `nn-mul-closed` — forall([a, b], a in nn and b in nn implies a * b in nn)
- `nn-mul-comm` — forall([a, b], a in nn and b in nn implies a * b = b * a)
- `nn-one-mul` — forall([a in nn], 1 * a = a)
- `nn-subset-ord` — forall([n in nn], n in ord)
- `nn-subset-zz` — forall([n in nn], n in zz)
- `nn-succ-closed` — forall([n in nn], succ(n) in nn)
- `nn-zero-in` — 0 in nn
- `nth-in-range` — forall([a, i, l], i in nn and l in tuples(a) and 1 <= i and i <= length(l) implies nth(i, l) in a)
- `ord-le-antisymm` — forall([alpha, beta], ord-le(alpha, beta) and ord-le(beta, alpha) implies alpha = beta)
- `ord-le-closure` — forall([alpha, beta], ord-le(alpha, beta) implies alpha in ord and beta in ord)
- `ord-le-nn-compat` — forall([m, n], m in nn and n in nn implies ord-le(m, n) iff m <= n)
- `ord-le-refl` — forall([alpha in ord], ord-le(alpha, alpha))
- `ord-le-total` — forall([alpha, beta], alpha in ord and beta in ord implies ord-le(alpha, beta) or ord-le(beta, alpha))
- `ord-le-trans` — forall([alpha, beta, gamma], ord-le(alpha, beta) and ord-le(beta, gamma) implies ord-le(alpha, gamma))
- `ord-segment-is-set` — forall([alpha in ord], ord-segment(alpha) in set)
- `ord-segment-limit` — forall([lambda], limit-ord(lambda) implies forall([x], ord-lt(x, lambda) implies forsome([beta], ord-lt(x, beta) and ord-lt(beta, lambda))))
- `ord-segment-membership` — forall([alpha, x], alpha in ord implies x in ord-segment(alpha) iff ord-lt(x, alpha))
- `ord-segment-succ` — forall([alpha in ord, x], x in ord-segment(succ_ord(alpha)) iff x in ord-segment(alpha) or x = alpha)
- `ord-segment-zero` — ord-segment(0) = empty-set
- `ord-succ-above` — forall([alpha in ord], ord-lt(alpha, succ_ord(alpha)))
- `ord-succ-immediate` — forall([alpha, beta], alpha in ord and beta in ord and ord-lt(alpha, beta) implies ord-le(succ_ord(alpha), beta))
- `ord-succ-in` — forall([alpha in ord], succ_ord(alpha) in ord)
- `ord-succ-injective` — forall([alpha, beta], alpha in ord and beta in ord and succ_ord(alpha) = succ_ord(beta) implies alpha = beta)
- `ord-succ-nn` — forall([n in nn], succ_ord(n) = succ(n))
- `ord-zero-least` — forall([alpha in ord], ord-le(0, alpha))
- `pairing` — forall([a, b], a in set and b in set implies pair(a, b) in set)
- `pairing-membership` — forall([a, b], a in set and b in set implies forall([x], x in pair(a, b) iff x = a or x = b))
- `partial-fun-binary-membership` — forall([a, c, f], f in partial-fun(a, c) iff forsome([b in power(a)], f in fun(b, c)))
- `partial-fun-binary-sethood` — forall([a, c], a in set and c in set implies partial-fun(a, c) in set)
- `partial-fun-membership` — forall([a, f], f in partial-fun(a) iff forsome([b in power(a)], f in fun(b)))
- `power-neg` — forall([x, n], x in cc and n in nn and not(x = 0) implies x ^ (-n) = recip(x ^ n))
- `power-set` — forall([a in set], power(a) in set)
- `power-set-membership` — forall([a, x], x in power(a) iff x in set and forall([z in x], z in a))
- `power-succ` — forall([x, n], x in cc and n in nn implies x ^ succ(n) = x * x ^ n)
- `power-typing-nonneg` — forall([x, n], x in cc and n in nn implies x ^ n in cc)
- `power-zero` — forall([x in cc], x ^ 0 = 1)
- `qq-add-assoc` — forall([a, b, c], a in qq and b in qq and c in qq implies (a + b) + c = a + (b + c))
- `qq-add-closed` — forall([a, b], a in qq and b in qq implies a + b in qq)
- `qq-add-comm` — forall([a, b], a in qq and b in qq implies a + b = b + a)
- `qq-add-zero` — forall([a in qq], a + 0 = a)
- `qq-distributive` — forall([a, b, c], a in qq and b in qq and c in qq implies a * (b + c) = a * b + a * c)
- `qq-is-fraction` — forall([q in qq], forsome([a in zz, b in zz], not(b = 0) and q = a * recip(b)))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `qq-is-set` — qq in set
- `qq-mul-assoc` — forall([a, b, c], a in qq and b in qq and c in qq implies (a * b) * c = a * (b * c))
- `qq-mul-closed` — forall([a, b], a in qq and b in qq implies a * b in qq)
- `qq-mul-comm` — forall([a, b], a in qq and b in qq implies a * b = b * a)
- `qq-neg-closed` — forall([a in qq], -a in qq)
- `qq-neg-inverse` — forall([a in qq], a + -a = 0)
- `qq-one-in` — 1 in qq
- `qq-one-mul` — forall([a in qq], 1 * a = a)
- `qq-recip-closed` — forall([a], a in qq and not(a = 0) implies recip(a) in qq)
- `qq-recip-inverse` — forall([a], a in qq and not(a = 0) implies a * recip(a) = 1)
- `qq-subset-rr` — forall([n in qq], n in rr)
- `qq-zero-in` — 0 in qq
- `quasi-eq-def` — forall([a, b], a = a implies a == b iff a = b)
- `quasi-eq-reflexivity` — forall([a], a == a)
- `quasi-eq-subst-membership` — forall([a, b, s], a == b and b in s implies a in s)  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `quasi-eq-symmetry` — forall([a, b], a == b implies b == a)
- `quasi-eq-transitivity` — forall([a, b, c], a == b and b == c implies a == c)
- `res-apply` — forall([a, b, f, x], f in fun(a) and b subset a and x in b implies (res(f, b))(x) = f(x))
- `res-codomain` — forall([a, b, c, f], f in fun(a, c) and b subset a implies res(f, b) in fun(b, c))
- `res-typing` — forall([a, b, f], f in fun(a) and b subset a implies res(f, b) in fun(b))
- `rr-add-assoc` — forall([a, b, c], a in rr and b in rr and c in rr implies (a + b) + c = a + (b + c))
- `rr-add-closed` — forall([a, b], a in rr and b in rr implies a + b in rr)
- `rr-add-comm` — forall([a, b], a in rr and b in rr implies a + b = b + a)
- `rr-add-zero` — forall([a in rr], a + 0 = a)
- `rr-distributive` — forall([a, b, c], a in rr and b in rr and c in rr implies a * (b + c) = a * b + a * c)
- `rr-is-set` — rr in set
- `rr-leq-add-compat` — forall([a, b, c], a in rr and b in rr and c in rr implies a <= b implies a + c <= b + c)
- `rr-leq-antisymmetric` — forall([a, b], a in rr and b in rr implies a <= b and b <= a implies a = b)
- `rr-leq-mul-nonneg` — forall([a, b], a in rr and b in rr implies 0 <= a and 0 <= b implies 0 <= a * b)
- `rr-leq-reflexive` — forall([a in rr], a <= a)
- `rr-leq-total` — forall([a, b], a in rr and b in rr implies a <= b or b <= a)
- `rr-leq-transitive` — forall([a, b, c], a in rr and b in rr and c in rr implies a <= b and b <= c implies a <= c)
- `rr-mul-assoc` — forall([a, b, c], a in rr and b in rr and c in rr implies (a * b) * c = a * (b * c))
- `rr-mul-closed` — forall([a, b], a in rr and b in rr implies a * b in rr)
- `rr-mul-comm` — forall([a, b], a in rr and b in rr implies a * b = b * a)
- `rr-neg-closed` — forall([a in rr], -a in rr)
- `rr-neg-inverse` — forall([a in rr], a + -a = 0)

- `rr-one-mul` — forall([a in rr], 1 * a = a)
- `rr-recip-closed` — forall([a], a in rr and not(a = 0) implies recip(a) in rr)
- `rr-recip-inverse` — forall([a], a in rr and not(a = 0) implies a * recip(a) = 1)
- `rr-subset-cc` — forall([n in rr], n in cc)

- `rr-sup-upper` — forall([s], s subset rr implies forsome([x], x in s) implies rr-bounded-above(s) implies rr-upper-bound(s, sup(s)))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `rr-zero-in` — 0 in rr
- `subset-def` — forall([a, b], a subset b iff forall([x in a], x in b))
- `subset-set` — forall([a], a subset set)
- `sup-ord-empty` — sup-ord(empty-set) = 0
- `sup-ord-in` — forall([a], a in set and forall([x in a], x in ord) implies sup-ord(a) in ord)
- `sup-ord-least` — forall([a], a in set and forall([x in a], x in ord) implies forall([beta], beta in ord and forall([alpha in a], ord-le(alpha, beta)) implies ord-le(sup-ord(a), beta)))
- `sup-ord-succ-segment` — forall([alpha in ord], sup-ord(ord-segment(succ_ord(alpha))) = alpha)
- `sup-ord-upper` — forall([a], a in set and forall([x in a], x in ord) implies forall([alpha in a], ord-le(alpha, sup-ord(a))))
- `transfinite-induction` — forall([c], forall([alpha], alpha in ord and forall([beta], ord-lt(beta, alpha) implies beta in c) implies alpha in c) implies forall([alpha in ord], alpha in c))
- `tuples-sethood` — forall([a in set], tuples(a) in set)
- `union-decompose` — forall([x, as], x in union(restvar(as)) iff splice(or, e, as, x in e))
- `union-membership` — forall([a, b, x], x in union(a, b) iff x in a or x in b)
- `union-set-closure` — forall([a, b], a in set and b in set implies union(a, b) in set)
- `zz-add-assoc` — forall([a, b, c], a in zz and b in zz and c in zz implies (a + b) + c = a + (b + c))
- `zz-add-closed` — forall([a, b], a in zz and b in zz implies a + b in zz)
- `zz-add-comm` — forall([a, b], a in zz and b in zz implies a + b = b + a)
- `zz-add-zero` — forall([a in zz], a + 0 = a)
- `zz-distributive` — forall([a, b, c], a in zz and b in zz and c in zz implies a * (b + c) = a * b + a * c)
- `zz-generated-by-nn` — forall([a in zz], forsome([n in nn], a = n or a = -n))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `zz-is-set` — zz in set
- `zz-mul-assoc` — forall([a, b, c], a in zz and b in zz and c in zz implies (a * b) * c = a * (b * c))
- `zz-mul-closed` — forall([a, b], a in zz and b in zz implies a * b in zz)
- `zz-mul-comm` — forall([a, b], a in zz and b in zz implies a * b = b * a)
- `zz-neg-closed` — forall([a in zz], -a in zz)
- `zz-neg-inverse` — forall([a in zz], a + -a = 0)
- `zz-one-in` — 1 in zz
- `zz-one-mul` — forall([a in zz], 1 * a = a)

- `zz-zero-in` — 0 in zz

- `converges-along` — forall([s, g, b, p], converges-along(s, g, b, p) iff is-metric-space(s) and g in fun(nn, pts(s)) and p in pts(s) and forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([n in nn], forall([i in nn], i in b implies n <= i implies (dist(s))(g(i), p) <= eps))))
- `converges-on` — forall([s, fam, dseq], converges-on(s, fam, dseq) iff is-metric-space(s) and fam in fun(nn, fun(pts(s), rr)) and dseq in fun(nn, pts(s)) and forall([m in nn], converges(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(k, nn, (fam(k))(dseq(m))))))
- `converges-to` — forall([s, f, l], converges-to(s, f, l) iff is-metric-space(s) and f in fun(nn, pts(s)) and l in pts(s) and forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([n in nn], forall([n_ in nn], n <= n_ implies (dist(s))(f(n_), l) <= eps))))
- `converges-uniformly` — forall([s, seq, g], converges-uniformly(s, seq, g) iff is-metric-space(s) and seq in fun(nn, fun(pts(s), rr)) and g in fun(pts(s), rr) and forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([cap in nn], forall([k], k in nn and cap <= k implies forall([x in pts(s)], abs((seq(k))(x) - g(x)) < eps)))))
- `cseq-equiv` — forall([m, f, g], cseq-equiv(m, f, g) iff converges-to(rr-ms, dist-seq(m, f, g), 0))
- `deg-bound` — forall([a, p, n], deg-bound(a, p, n) iff forall([k_ in nn], n < k_ implies p(k_) = zero(a)))
- `det-cofactor` — forall([r, n, a], is-ring(r) implies n in nn implies a in mat(succ(n), succ(n), carr(r)) implies det(r, succ(n), a) == finsum(ring-additive-ag(r), vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, succ(n)), (mul(r))(mpow(ring-multiplicative-monoid(r), (neg(r))(one(r)), succ(j)), (mul(r))(entry(a, 1, j), det(r, n, minor(a, 1, j, n))))), interval(1, succ(n))))
- `det-zero` — forall([r, a], det(r, 0, a) == one(r))
- `dual-carrier` — forall([m], vec(dual(m)) == dual-vec(m))
- `dual-norm-value` — forall([m, f in dual-vec(m)], (vnrm(dual(m)))(f) == dual-norm(m, f))
- `dual-scal` — forall([m], scal(dual(m)) == rr-normed-field)
- `dual-vec-membership` — forall([m, f], f in dual-vec(m) iff f in fun(vec(m), rr) and is-bounded-linear-functional(m, f))
- `econverges-to` — forall([f, l], econverges-to(f, l) iff f in fun(nn, rr-pos-star) and l in rr-pos-star and eliminf(f) = l and elimsup(f) = l)
- `ell-one-def` — ell-one = {x_ in sqn(cc): series-converges(vnb-lambda(k_, nn, magnitude(x_(k_))))}
- `ell-two-def` — ell-two = {x_ in sqn(cc): series-converges(vnb-lambda(k_, nn, magnitude(x_(k_)) * magnitude(x_(k_))))}
- `empty-in-tuples` — forall([a], [] in tuples(a))
- `etimes-pos-inf-left` — forall([y in rr-pos-star], not(y = 0) implies etimes(pos-inf, y) = pos-inf)
- `etimes-pos-inf-right` — forall([x in rr-pos-star], not(x = 0) implies etimes(x, pos-inf) = pos-inf)
- `etimes-pos-inf-zero` — etimes(pos-inf, 0) = 0
- `etimes-real` — forall([x, y], x in rr implies y in rr implies etimes(x, y) = bintimes(x, y))
- `etimes-zero-pos-inf` — etimes(0, pos-inf) = 0
- `euclidean-ring-class` — forall([s], s in euclidean-ring iff is-euclidean-ring(s))
- `euclidean-ring-has-gauge-field-as-euclidean-ring` — forall([r], is-field(r) implies forsome([dg in fun(carr(r), nn)], has-div-remainder(field-as-euclidean-ring(r), dg)))
- `extends-on` — forall([s, g, f], extends-on(s, g, f) iff forall([x_ in s], g(x_) = f(x_)))
- `factorial-succ` — forall([k in nn], factorial(succ(k)) = succ(k) * factorial(k))
- `factorial-zero` — factorial(0) = succ(0)
- `falling-succ` — forall([n, k in nn], falling(n, succ(k)) == (n - k) * falling(n, k))
- `falling-zero` — forall([n], falling(n, 0) == succ(0))
- `fam-of-list-apply` — forall([l, i], i in nn and succ(i) <= length(l) implies (fam-of-list(l))(i) = nth(succ(i), l))
- `field-additive-ag-is-abelian-group` — forall([r], is-field(r) implies is-abelian-group(field-additive-ag(r)))
- `field-as-euclidean-ring-is-euclidean-ring` — forall([r], is-field(r) implies is-euclidean-ring(field-as-euclidean-ring(r)))
- `field-as-integral-domain-is-integral-domain` — forall([r], is-field(r) implies is-integral-domain(field-as-integral-domain(r)))
- `field-class` — forall([s], s in field iff is-field(s))
- `field-multiplicative-group-is-group` — forall([r], is-field(r) implies is-group(field-multiplicative-group(r)))
- `field-non-zero-carrier` — forall([s], is-field(s) implies non-zero(s) = difference(carr(s), singleton(zero(s))))
- `field-ring-class` — forall([s], s in field-ring iff is-field-ring(s))
- `finsum-add-ag-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f in fun(s, carr(r)), h in fun(s, carr(r))], finsum(ring-additive-ag(r), vnb-lambda(z, s, (add(r))(f(z), h(z))), s) = (add(r))(finsum(ring-additive-ag(r), f, s), finsum(ring-additive-ag(r), h, s)))))
- `finsum-all-id-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f in fun(s, carr(r))], forall([z in s], f(z) = zero(r)) implies finsum(ring-additive-ag(r), f, s) = zero(r))))
- `finsum-congruence-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f, g], forall([z in s], f(z) = g(z)) implies finsum(ring-additive-ag(r), f, s) = finsum(ring-additive-ag(r), g, s))))
- `finsum-embed-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([s2 in set], card(s2) in nn implies forall([f in fun(s2, carr(r))], s subset s2 implies forall([z], z in s2 and not(z in s) implies f(z) = zero(r)) implies finsum(ring-additive-ag(r), f, s2) = finsum(ring-additive-ag(r), f, s)))))
- `finsum-fubini-c-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([x in set], card(x) in nn implies forall([y in set], card(y) in nn implies forall([f in fun(cartesian(x, y), carr(r))], finsum(ring-additive-ag(r), vnb-lambda(i, x, finsum(ring-additive-ag(r), vnb-lambda(j, y, f([i, j])), y)), x) = finsum(ring-additive-ag(r), vnb-lambda(j, y, finsum(ring-additive-ag(r), vnb-lambda(i, x, f([i, j])), x)), y)))))
- `finsum-fubini-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([x], x in set and card(x) in nn implies forall([y], y in set and card(y) in nn implies forall([f in fun(cartesian(x, y), carr(r))], finsum(ring-additive-ag(r), vnb-lambda(i, x, finsum(ring-additive-ag(r), vnb-lambda(j, y, f([i, j])), y)), x) = finsum(ring-additive-ag(r), vnb-lambda(j, y, finsum(ring-additive-ag(r), vnb-lambda(i, x, f([i, j])), x)), y)))))
- `finsum-insert-ag-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([x in set], card(x) in nn implies forall([k in set], not(k in x) implies forall([f in fun(union(x, pair(k, k)), carr(r))], finsum(ring-additive-ag(r), f, union(x, pair(k, k))) = (add(r))(finsum(ring-additive-ag(r), f, x), f(k))))))
- `finsum-interval-peel-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([n in nn, f in fun(interval(1, succ(n)), carr(r))], finsum(ring-additive-ag(r), f, interval(1, succ(n))) = (add(r))(finsum(ring-additive-ag(r), f, interval(1, n)), f(succ(n)))))
- `finsum-interval-shift-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([n in nn, f in fun(interval(1, succ(n)), carr(r))], finsum(ring-additive-ag(r), f, interval(1, succ(n))) = (add(r))(f(1), finsum(ring-additive-ag(r), vnb-lambda(z, interval(1, n), f(succ(z))), interval(1, n)))))
- `finsum-ord-peel-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([n in nn, f in fun(ord-segment(succ(n)), carr(r))], finsum(ring-additive-ag(r), f, ord-segment(succ(n))) = (add(r))(finsum(ring-additive-ag(r), f, ord-segment(n)), f(n))))
- `finsum-reindex-ag-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([t], t in set and card(t) in nn implies forall([phi in bijection(t, s), f in fun(s, carr(r))], finsum(ring-additive-ag(r), f, s) = finsum(ring-additive-ag(r), vnb-lambda(z, t, f(phi(z))), t)))))
- `finsum-single-support-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f in fun(s, carr(r)), i0 in s], forall([j in s], not(j = i0) implies f(j) = zero(r)) implies finsum(ring-additive-ag(r), f, s) = f(i0))))
- `finsum-singleton-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([x in set, f in fun(pair(x, x), carr(r))], finsum(ring-additive-ag(r), f, pair(x, x)) = f(x)))
- `finsum-two-support-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f in fun(s, carr(r)), i0 in s, i1 in s], not(i0 = i1) implies forall([j in s], not(j = i0) implies not(j = i1) implies f(j) = zero(r)) implies finsum(ring-additive-ag(r), f, s) = (add(r))(f(i0), f(i1)))))
- `finsum-type-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f in fun(s, carr(r))], finsum(ring-additive-ag(r), f, s) in carr(r))))
- `fr-cauchy` — forall([m, fam, seq], fr-cauchy(m, fam, seq) iff forall([k in nn, eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([cap in nn], forall([i], i in nn and cap <= i implies forall([j], j in nn and cap <= j implies (fam(k))((vadd(m))(seq(i), (vneg(m))(seq(j)))) < eps)))))
- `fr-conv` — forall([m, fam, seq, lim], fr-conv(m, fam, seq, lim) iff forall([k in nn, eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([cap in nn], forall([i], i in nn and cap <= i implies (fam(k))((vadd(m))(seq(i), (vneg(m))(lim))) < eps))))
- `gauge-is-degree-field-as-euclidean-ring` — forall([r], is-field(r) implies gauge(field-as-euclidean-ring(r)) in fun(carr(r), nn) and has-div-remainder(field-as-euclidean-ring(r), gauge(field-as-euclidean-ring(r))))
- `generates` — forall([md, n, u], generates(md, n, u) iff forall([x_ in vec(md)], forsome([c_ in mat(1, n, carr(scal(md)))], x_ = entry(matact(md, c_, u), 1, 1))))
- `good-sub` — forall([m, s, f, t], good-sub(m, s, f, t) iff forsome([g_], npe(m, s, f, t, g_)))
- `group-class` — forall([s], s in group iff is-group(s))
- `has-closed-graph` — forall([m1, fam1, m2, fam2, tt], has-closed-graph(m1, fam1, m2, fam2, tt) iff is-linear-map(m1, m2, tt) and forall([seq in fun(nn, vec(m1)), p1 in vec(m1), p2 in vec(m2)], fr-conv(m1, fam1, seq, p1) and fr-conv(m2, fam2, vnb-lambda(i, nn, tt(seq(i))), p2) implies p2 = tt(p1)))
- `has-div-remainder` — forall([s, dg], has-div-remainder(s, dg) iff forall([a_ in carr(s), b in carr(s)], not(b = zero(s)) implies forsome([q in carr(s), r in carr(s)], a_ = (add(s))((mul(s))(q, b), r) and (r = zero(s) or succ(dg(r)) <= dg(b)))))
- `has-fip` — forall([s, c], has-fip(s, c) iff is-metric-space(s) and forall([a in c], is-closed(s, a)) and forall([f], f subset c and card(f) in nn implies forsome([p], p in big-intersection(carr, f, carr))))
- `has-inverses` — forall([op, unit, invop, crr], has-inverses(op, unit, invop, crr) iff forall([u in crr], op(invop(u), u) = unit and op(u, invop(u)) = unit))
- `imag-part-def` — forall([z], imag-part(z) == (z - conjugate(z)) * recip(2 * 1i))
- `image-membership-iff` — forall([phi, s, w], w in image(phi, s) iff forsome([x in s], phi(x) = w))
- `inf-subsets-membership` — forall([a, s], s in inf-subsets(a) iff s subset a and not(card(s) in nn))
- `injection-membership-iff` — forall([x, y, phi], phi in injection(x, y) iff phi in fun(x, y) and forall([a in x, b in x], phi(a) = phi(b) implies a = b))
- `injective-star` — forall([f], injective-star(f) iff forall([u_, v_], f(u_) = f(v_) implies u_ = v_))
- `integral-domain-class` — forall([s], s in integral-domain iff is-integral-domain(s))
- `ip-normed-ag-carrier` — forall([v], carr(ip-normed-ag(v)) == vec(v))
- `ip-normed-ag-norm-value` — forall([v, x in vec(v)], (nrm(ip-normed-ag(v)))(x) == ip-norm(v, x))
- `is-abelian-group` — forall([s], is-abelian-group(s) iff length(s) = 4 and carr(s) in set and opr(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and iden(s) in carr(s) and inv(s) in fun(carr(s), carr(s)) and is-associative(opr(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(opr(s), iden(s), carr(s)) and has-inverses(opr(s), iden(s), inv(s), carr(s)) and is-commutative(opr(s), carr(s)))
- `is-absolutely-summable` — forall([grp, f], is-absolutely-summable(grp, f) iff esum(vnb-lambda(i, dom(f), (nrm(grp))(f(i)))) in rr)
- `is-algebra-of-sets` — forall([omega, ca], is-algebra-of-sets(omega, ca) iff omega in set and ca subset power(omega) and forsome([a_], a_ in ca) and forall([a_ in ca], complement-in(omega, a_) in ca) and forall([a_ in ca, b_ in ca], union(a_, b_) in ca) and forall([a_ in ca, b_ in ca], intersection(a_, b_) in ca))
- `is-antiderivable` — forall([phi, a, b], is-antiderivable(phi, a, b) iff forsome([f_], is-antiderivative(f_, phi, a, b)))
- `is-antiderivative` — forall([f, phi, a, b], is-antiderivative(f, phi, a, b) iff f in fun(rr, rr) and phi in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and b in rr and a < b and forall([x_ in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x_)) and forall([th_], th_ in rr and a < th_ and th_ < b implies is-diff-at(f, th_, phi(th_))))
- `is-associative` — forall([op, crr], is-associative(op, crr) iff forall([u in crr, v in crr, w in crr], op(op(u, v), w) = op(u, op(v, w))))
- `is-bounded-linear-functional` — forall([m, f], is-bounded-linear-functional(m, f) iff is-linear-functional(m, f) and forsome([c_ in rr], 0 <= c_ and forall([x_ in vec(m)], abs(f(x_)) <= c_ * (vnrm(m))(x_))))
- `is-bounded-linear-functional-on` — forall([m, s, f], is-bounded-linear-functional-on(m, s, f) iff is-linear-functional-on(m, s, f) and forsome([c_ in rr], 0 <= c_ and forall([x_ in s], abs(f(x_)) <= c_ * (vnrm(m))(x_))))
- `is-bounded-metric-space` — forall([s], is-bounded-metric-space(s) iff is-metric-space(s) and forsome([b in rr], forall([x, y], x in pts(s) implies y in pts(s) implies (dist(s))(x, y) <= b)))
- `is-c-metric-space` — forall([s], is-c-metric-space(s) iff length(s) = 2 and pts(s) in set and dists(s) in fun(nn, fun(cartesian(pts(s), pts(s)), rr)) and forall([k_ in nn], is-pseudometric((dists(s))(k_), pts(s))) and forall([u in pts(s), v in pts(s)], forall([k_ in nn], ((dists(s))(k_))(u, v) = 0) implies u = v))
- `is-caratheodory-measurable` — forall([omega, mu, es], is-caratheodory-measurable(omega, mu, es) iff es subset omega and forall([a_ in power(omega)], mu(a_) = eplus(mu(intersection(a_, es)), mu(difference(a_, es)))))
- `is-cauchy-seq` — forall([s, f], is-cauchy-seq(s, f) iff is-metric-space(s) and f in fun(nn, pts(s)) and forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([n in nn], forall([m in nn, n_ in nn], n <= m and n <= n_ implies (dist(s))(f(m), f(n_)) <= eps))))
- `is-chain` — forall([grd, porel, ch], is-chain(grd, porel, ch) iff ch subset grd and forall([x in ch, y in ch], [x, y] in porel or [y, x] in porel))
- `is-closed` — forall([s, a], is-closed(s, a) iff is-metric-space(s) and a subset pts(s) and is-open(s, complement-in(pts(s), a)))
- `is-comm-monoid` — forall([s], is-comm-monoid(s) iff length(s) = 3 and carr(s) in set and opr(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and iden(s) in carr(s) and is-associative(opr(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(opr(s), iden(s), carr(s)) and is-commutative(opr(s), carr(s)))
- `is-commutative` — forall([op, crr], is-commutative(op, crr) iff forall([u in crr, v in crr], op(u, v) = op(v, u)))
- `is-commutative-ring-def` — forall([s], is-commutative-ring(s) iff is-ring(s) and forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], (mul(s))(a, b) = (mul(s))(b, a)))
- `is-compact` — forall([s], is-compact(s) iff is-metric-space(s) and forall([c], is-open-cover(s, c) implies forsome([f], f subset c and card(f) in nn and is-open-cover(s, f))))
- `is-complete` — forall([s], is-complete(s) iff is-metric-space(s) and forall([f], is-cauchy-seq(s, f) implies converges(s, f)))
- `is-complex-inner-product-space` — forall([s], is-complex-inner-product-space(s) iff length(s) = 7 and is-ring(scal(s)) and vec(s) in set and vadd(s) in fun(cartesian(vec(s), vec(s)), vec(s)) and vzero(s) in vec(s) and vneg(s) in fun(vec(s), vec(s)) and act(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(scal(s)), vec(s)), vec(s)) and ip(s) in fun(cartesian(vec(s), vec(s)), cc) and is-associative(vadd(s), vec(s)) and is-commutative(vadd(s), vec(s)) and is-identity(vadd(s), vzero(s), vec(s)) and has-inverses(vadd(s), vzero(s), vneg(s), vec(s)) and scal(s) = normed-field-as-commutative-ring(cc-normed-field) and carr(scal(s)) = cc and forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s), y_ in vec(s)], (act(s))(r_, (vadd(s))(x_, y_)) = (vadd(s))((act(s))(r_, x_), (act(s))(r_, y_))) and forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), s_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s)], (act(s))((add(scal(s)))(r_, s_), x_) = (vadd(s))((act(s))(r_, x_), (act(s))(s_, x_))) and forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), s_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s)], (act(s))((mul(scal(s)))(r_, s_), x_) = (act(s))(r_, (act(s))(s_, x_))) and forall([x_ in vec(s)], (act(s))(one(scal(s)), x_) = x_) and forall([x_ in vec(s), y_ in vec(s), z_ in vec(s)], (ip(s))((vadd(s))(x_, y_), z_) = (ip(s))(x_, z_) + (ip(s))(y_, z_)) and forall([a_ in cc, x_ in vec(s), y_ in vec(s)], (ip(s))((act(s))(a_, x_), y_) = a_ * (ip(s))(x_, y_)) and forall([x_ in vec(s), y_ in vec(s)], (ip(s))(y_, x_) = conjugate((ip(s))(x_, y_))) and forall([x_ in vec(s)], (ip(s))(x_, x_) in rr) and forall([x_ in vec(s)], 0 <= (ip(s))(x_, x_)) and forall([x_ in vec(s)], (ip(s))(x_, x_) = 0 iff x_ = vzero(s)))
- `is-cont-lin` — forall([m1, fam1, m2, fam2, tt], is-cont-lin(m1, fam1, m2, fam2, tt) iff is-linear-map(m1, m2, tt) and forall([seq in fun(nn, vec(m1)), lim in vec(m1)], fr-conv(m1, fam1, seq, lim) implies fr-conv(m2, fam2, vnb-lambda(i, nn, tt(seq(i))), tt(lim))))
- `is-continuous` — forall([s, t, f], is-continuous(s, t, f) iff is-metric-space(s) and is-metric-space(t) and f in fun(pts(s), pts(t)) and forall([a in pts(s)], is-continuous-at(s, t, f, a)))
- `is-continuous-at` — forall([s, t, f, a], is-continuous-at(s, t, f, a) iff is-metric-space(s) and is-metric-space(t) and f in fun(pts(s), pts(t)) and a in pts(s) and forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([delta], pos-rr(delta) and forall([b in pts(s)], (dist(s))(a, b) <= delta implies (dist(t))(f(a), f(b)) <= eps))))
- `is-countable-pseudometric-family` — forall([fam, ground], is-countable-pseudometric-family(fam, ground) iff forall([n_ in nn], is-pseudometric-space(fam(n_)) and pts(fam(n_)) == ground))
- `is-dense-seq` — forall([s, dseq], is-dense-seq(s, dseq) iff is-metric-space(s) and dseq in fun(nn, pts(s)) and forall([x in pts(s), eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([m in nn], (dist(s))(x, dseq(m)) < eps)))
- `is-diagonal` — forall([a, m, n, d], is-diagonal(a, m, n, d) iff forall([i, j], i in interval(1, m) implies j in interval(1, n) implies not(i = j) implies entry(d, i, j) = zero(a)))
- `is-diff-at` — forall([f, a, l], is-diff-at(f, a, l) iff f in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and l in rr and forsome([phi in fun(rr, rr)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, phi, a) and phi(a) = l and forall([x in rr], f(x) - f(a) = phi(x) * (x - a))))
- `is-diff-at-v` — forall([m, f, a, l], is-diff-at-v(m, f, a, l) iff is-normed-vector-space(m) and f in fun(rr, vec(m)) and a in rr and l in vec(m) and forsome([phi in fun(rr, vec(m))], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, nvs-metric-space(m), phi, a) and phi(a) = l and forall([x_ in rr], (vadd(m))(f(x_), (vneg(m))(f(a))) = (act(m))(x_ - a, phi(x_)))))
- `is-dir-diff-at` — forall([m, f, a, eta, dl], is-dir-diff-at(m, f, a, eta, dl) iff is-diff-at(seg-curve(m, f, a, eta), 0, dl))
- `is-distributive` — forall([addop, mulop, crr], is-distributive(addop, mulop, crr) iff forall([u in crr, v in crr, w in crr], mulop(u, addop(v, w)) = addop(mulop(u, v), mulop(u, w)) and mulop(addop(u, v), w) = addop(mulop(u, w), mulop(v, w))))
- `is-eps-cauchy-seq` — forall([s, eps, y], is-eps-cauchy-seq(s, eps, y) iff is-metric-space(s) and y in fun(nn, pts(s)) and forall([m in nn, n_ in nn], (dist(s))(y(m), y(n_)) <= eps))
- `is-equicontinuous` — forall([s, t, fam], is-equicontinuous(s, t, fam) iff is-metric-space(s) and is-metric-space(t) and fam in fun(nn, fun(pts(s), pts(t))) and forall([x in pts(s), eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([del], pos-rr(del) and forall([k in nn, y in pts(s)], (dist(s))(x, y) < del implies (dist(t))((fam(k))(x), (fam(k))(y)) < eps))))
- `is-equivalence` — forall([rho, crr], is-equivalence(rho, crr) iff rho in power(cartesian(crr, crr)) and forall([u in crr], [u, u] in rho) and forall([u in crr, v in crr], [u, v] in rho implies [v, u] in rho) and forall([u in crr, v in crr, w in crr], [u, v] in rho and [v, w] in rho implies [u, w] in rho))
- `is-euclidean-ring-def` — forall([s], is-euclidean-ring(s) iff is-integral-domain(s) and forsome([dg in fun(carr(s), nn)], forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], not(b = zero(s)) implies forsome([q in carr(s), r in carr(s)], a = (add(s))((mul(s))(q, b), r) and (r = zero(s) or succ(dg(r)) <= dg(b))))))
- `is-field` — forall([s], is-field(s) iff length(s) = 8 and carr(s) in set and add(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and mul(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and neg(s) in fun(carr(s), carr(s)) and zero(s) in carr(s) and one(s) in carr(s) and non-zero(s) in set and non-zero(s) = difference(carr(s), singleton(zero(s))) and mul-inv(s) in fun(non-zero(s), non-zero(s)) and is-associative(add(s), carr(s)) and is-commutative(add(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(add(s), zero(s), carr(s)) and has-inverses(add(s), zero(s), neg(s), carr(s)) and is-associative(mul(s), carr(s)) and is-commutative(mul(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(mul(s), one(s), carr(s)) and is-distributive(add(s), mul(s), carr(s)))
- `is-field-ring-def` — forall([s], is-field-ring(s) iff is-commutative-ring(s) and not(one(s) = zero(s)) and forall([a_ in carr(s)], not(a_ = zero(s)) implies forsome([b_ in carr(s)], (mul(s))(a_, b_) = one(s))))
- `is-finite-cover` — forall([c, a], is-finite-cover(c, a) iff c in set and card(c) in nn and a subset big-union(u, c, u))
- `is-finite-dimensional` — forall([m], is-finite-dimensional(m) iff is-vector-space(m) and is-noetherian(m))
- `is-frechet-structure` — forall([m, fam], is-frechet-structure(m, fam) iff is-seminorm-family(m, fam) and forall([seq in fun(nn, vec(m))], fr-cauchy(m, fam, seq) implies forsome([lim in vec(m)], fr-conv(m, fam, seq, lim))))
- `is-gauge-countable` — forall([s], is-gauge-countable(s) iff is-top-space(s) and forsome([fam], is-countable-pseudometric-family(fam, pts(s)) and pseudo-gauge-top(fam, pts(s)) == s))
- `is-group` — forall([s], is-group(s) iff length(s) = 4 and carr(s) in set and opr(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and iden(s) in carr(s) and inv(s) in fun(carr(s), carr(s)) and is-associative(opr(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(opr(s), iden(s), carr(s)) and has-inverses(opr(s), iden(s), inv(s), carr(s)))
- `is-group-norm` — forall([nm, op, invop, unit, crr], is-group-norm(nm, op, invop, unit, crr) iff nm in fun(crr, rr) and forall([u in crr], 0 <= nm(u) and (nm(u) = 0 iff u = unit) and nm(invop(u)) = nm(u) and forall([v in crr], nm(op(u, v)) <= nm(u) + nm(v))))
- `is-hausdorff` — forall([s], is-hausdorff(s) iff is-top-space(s) and forall([a, b], a in pts(s) implies b in pts(s) implies not(a == b) implies forsome([u, v], u in opens(s) and v in opens(s) and a in u and b in v and intersection(u, v) == empty-set)))
- `is-hom-abelian-group-def` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-abelian-group(a, b, f) iff is-abelian-group(a) and is-abelian-group(b) and f in fun(carr(a), carr(b)) and forall([x1_ in carr(a), x2_ in carr(a)], f((opr(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (opr(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))) and f(iden(a)) = iden(b) and forall([x1_ in carr(a)], f((inv(a))(x1_)) = (inv(b))(f(x1_))))
- `is-hom-c-metric-space-def` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-c-metric-space(a, b, f) iff is-c-metric-space(a) and is-c-metric-space(b) and f in fun(pts(a), pts(b)) and forall([x1_ in nn], (dists(a))(x1_) = (dists(b))(x1_)))
- `is-hom-comm-monoid-def` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-comm-monoid(a, b, f) iff is-comm-monoid(a) and is-comm-monoid(b) and f in fun(carr(a), carr(b)) and forall([x1_ in carr(a), x2_ in carr(a)], f((opr(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (opr(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))) and f(iden(a)) = iden(b))
- `is-hom-commutative-ring-def` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-commutative-ring(a, b, f) iff is-commutative-ring(a) and is-commutative-ring(b) and is-hom-ring(a, b, f))
- `is-hom-complex-inner-product-space-def` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-complex-inner-product-space(a, b, f) iff is-complex-inner-product-space(a) and is-complex-inner-product-space(b) and f in fun(vec(a), vec(b)) and scal(a) = scal(b) and forall([x1_ in vec(a), x2_ in vec(a)], f((vadd(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (vadd(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))) and f(vzero(a)) = vzero(b) and forall([x1_ in vec(a)], f((vneg(a))(x1_)) = (vneg(b))(f(x1_))) and forall([x1_ in carr(scal(a)), x2_ in vec(a)], f((act(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (act(b))(x1_, f(x2_))) and forall([x1_ in vec(a), x2_ in vec(a)], (ip(a))(x1_, x2_) = (ip(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))))
- `is-hom-euclidean-ring-def` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-euclidean-ring(a, b, f) iff is-euclidean-ring(a) and is-euclidean-ring(b) and is-hom-integral-domain(a, b, f))
- `is-hom-field-def` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-field(a, b, f) iff is-field(a) and is-field(b) and f in fun(carr(a), carr(b)) and forall([x1_ in carr(a), x2_ in carr(a)], f((add(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (add(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))) and forall([x1_ in carr(a), x2_ in carr(a)], f((mul(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (mul(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))) and forall([x1_ in carr(a)], f((neg(a))(x1_)) = (neg(b))(f(x1_))) and f(zero(a)) = zero(b) and f(one(a)) = one(b) and forall([x1_ in non-zero(a)], f((mul-inv(a))(x1_)) = (mul-inv(b))(f(x1_))))
- `is-hom-field-ring-def` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-field-ring(a, b, f) iff is-field-ring(a) and is-field-ring(b) and is-hom-commutative-ring(a, b, f))
- `is-hom-group-def` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-group(a, b, f) iff is-group(a) and is-group(b) and f in fun(carr(a), carr(b)) and forall([x1_ in carr(a), x2_ in carr(a)], f((opr(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (opr(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))) and f(iden(a)) = iden(b) and forall([x1_ in carr(a)], f((inv(a))(x1_)) = (inv(b))(f(x1_))))
- `is-hom-integral-domain-def` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-integral-domain(a, b, f) iff is-integral-domain(a) and is-integral-domain(b) and is-hom-commutative-ring(a, b, f))
- `is-hom-measurable-space-def` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-measurable-space(a, b, f) iff is-measurable-space(a) and is-measurable-space(b) and f in fun(pts(a), pts(b)) and sigma(a) = sigma(b))
- `is-hom-measure-space-def` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-measure-space(a, b, f) iff is-measure-space(a) and is-measure-space(b) and f in fun(pts(a), pts(b)) and sigma(a) = sigma(b) and forall([x1_ in sigma(a)], (meas(a))(x1_) = (meas(b))(x1_)))
- `is-hom-metric-space-def` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-metric-space(a, b, f) iff is-metric-space(a) and is-metric-space(b) and f in fun(pts(a), pts(b)) and forall([x1_ in pts(a), x2_ in pts(a)], (dist(a))(x1_, x2_) = (dist(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))))
- `is-hom-metrizable-top-space-def` — forall([s, t, f], is-hom-metrizable-top-space(s, t, f) iff is-metrizable-top-space(s) and is-metrizable-top-space(t) and f in fun(pts(s), pts(t)) and forall([u in opens(t)], preimage(s, f, u) in opens(s)))
- `is-hom-module-def` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-module(a, b, f) iff is-module(a) and is-module(b) and f in fun(vec(a), vec(b)) and scal(a) = scal(b) and forall([x1_ in vec(a), x2_ in vec(a)], f((vadd(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (vadd(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))) and f(vzero(a)) = vzero(b) and forall([x1_ in vec(a)], f((vneg(a))(x1_)) = (vneg(b))(f(x1_))) and forall([x1_ in carr(scal(a)), x2_ in vec(a)], f((act(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (act(b))(x1_, f(x2_))))
- `is-hom-monoid-def` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-monoid(a, b, f) iff is-monoid(a) and is-monoid(b) and f in fun(carr(a), carr(b)) and forall([x1_ in carr(a), x2_ in carr(a)], f((opr(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (opr(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))) and f(iden(a)) = iden(b))
- `is-hom-normed-ag-def` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-normed-ag(a, b, f) iff is-normed-ag(a) and is-normed-ag(b) and f in fun(carr(a), carr(b)) and forall([x1_ in carr(a), x2_ in carr(a)], f((opr(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (opr(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))) and f(iden(a)) = iden(b) and forall([x1_ in carr(a)], f((inv(a))(x1_)) = (inv(b))(f(x1_))) and forall([x1_ in carr(a)], (nrm(a))(x1_) = (nrm(b))(f(x1_))))
- `is-hom-normed-field-def` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-normed-field(a, b, f) iff is-normed-field(a) and is-normed-field(b) and f in fun(carr(a), carr(b)) and forall([x1_ in carr(a), x2_ in carr(a)], f((add(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (add(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))) and forall([x1_ in carr(a), x2_ in carr(a)], f((mul(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (mul(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))) and forall([x1_ in carr(a)], f((neg(a))(x1_)) = (neg(b))(f(x1_))) and f(zero(a)) = zero(b) and f(one(a)) = one(b) and forall([x1_ in carr(a)], (fnrm(a))(x1_) = (fnrm(b))(f(x1_))))
- `is-hom-normed-vector-space-def` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-normed-vector-space(a, b, f) iff is-normed-vector-space(a) and is-normed-vector-space(b) and f in fun(vec(a), vec(b)) and scal(a) = scal(b) and forall([x1_ in vec(a), x2_ in vec(a)], f((vadd(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (vadd(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))) and f(vzero(a)) = vzero(b) and forall([x1_ in vec(a)], f((vneg(a))(x1_)) = (vneg(b))(f(x1_))) and forall([x1_ in carr(scal(a)), x2_ in vec(a)], f((act(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (act(b))(x1_, f(x2_))) and forall([x1_ in vec(a)], (vnrm(a))(x1_) = (vnrm(b))(f(x1_))))
- `is-hom-pid-def` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-pid(a, b, f) iff is-pid(a) and is-pid(b) and is-hom-integral-domain(a, b, f))
- `is-hom-pseudometric-space-def` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-pseudometric-space(a, b, f) iff is-pseudometric-space(a) and is-pseudometric-space(b) and f in fun(pts(a), pts(b)) and forall([x1_ in pts(a), x2_ in pts(a)], (dist(a))(x1_, x2_) = (dist(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))))
- `is-hom-ring-def` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-ring(a, b, f) iff is-ring(a) and is-ring(b) and f in fun(carr(a), carr(b)) and forall([x1_ in carr(a), x2_ in carr(a)], f((add(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (add(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))) and forall([x1_ in carr(a), x2_ in carr(a)], f((mul(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (mul(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))) and forall([x1_ in carr(a)], f((neg(a))(x1_)) = (neg(b))(f(x1_))) and f(zero(a)) = zero(b) and f(one(a)) = one(b))
- `is-hom-ringoid-def` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-ringoid(a, b, f) iff is-ringoid(a) and is-ringoid(b) and f in fun(carr(a), carr(b)) and is-hom-ring(ringoid-as-ring(a), ringoid-as-ring(b), f) and forall([x_ in idl(a)], f(x_) in idl(b)))
- `is-hom-semigroup-def` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-semigroup(a, b, f) iff is-semigroup(a) and is-semigroup(b) and f in fun(carr(a), carr(b)) and forall([x1_ in carr(a), x2_ in carr(a)], f((opr(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (opr(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))))
- `is-hom-setoid-def` — forall([a, b, f1, f2], is-hom-setoid(a, b, f1, f2) iff is-setoid(a) and is-setoid(b) and f1 in fun(pts(a), pts(b)) and f2 in fun(rel(a), rel(b)))
- `is-hom-top-space-def` — forall([s, t, f], is-hom-top-space(s, t, f) iff is-top-space(s) and is-top-space(t) and f in fun(pts(s), pts(t)) and forall([u in opens(t)], preimage(s, f, u) in opens(s)))
- `is-hom-vector-space-def` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-vector-space(a, b, f) iff is-vector-space(a) and is-vector-space(b) and is-hom-module(a, b, f))
- `is-ideal` — forall([s, i], is-ideal(s, i) iff is-commutative-ring(s) and i subset carr(s) and zero(s) in i and forall([a in i, b in i], (add(s))(a, b) in i) and forall([a in i], (neg(s))(a) in i) and forall([r in carr(s), a in i], (mul(s))(r, a) in i))
- `is-ideal-in` — forall([i_, carr_, add_, mul_, neg_, zero_], is-ideal-in(i_, carr_, add_, mul_, neg_, zero_) iff i_ subset carr_ and zero_ in i_ and forall([a, b], a in i_ implies b in i_ implies add_(a, b) in i_) and forall([a in i_], neg_(a) in i_) and forall([x_, a], x_ in carr_ implies a in i_ implies mul_(x_, a) in i_) and forall([x_, a], x_ in carr_ implies a in i_ implies mul_(a, x_) in i_))
- `is-identity` — forall([op, unit, crr], is-identity(op, unit, crr) iff forall([u in crr], op(unit, u) = u and op(u, unit) = u))
- `is-integral-domain-def` — forall([s], is-integral-domain(s) iff is-commutative-ring(s) and not(one(s) = zero(s)) and forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], (mul(s))(a, b) = zero(s) implies a = zero(s) or b = zero(s)))
- `is-invertible-mat` — forall([a, n, u], is-invertible-mat(a, n, u) iff u in mat(n, n, carr(a)) and forsome([v in mat(n, n, carr(a))], matmul(a, u, v) = identmat(a, n) and matmul(a, v, u) = identmat(a, n)))
- `is-isometry` — forall([s, t, f], is-isometry(s, t, f) iff is-metric-space(s) and is-metric-space(t) and f in fun(pts(s), pts(t)) and forall([x in pts(s), y in pts(s)], (dist(t))(f(x), f(y)) = (dist(s))(x, y)))
- `is-k-linear` — forall([m, f], is-k-linear(m, f) iff is-module(m) and f in fun(vec(m), carr(scal(m))) and forall([x in vec(m), y in vec(m)], f((vadd(m))(x, y)) = (add(scal(m)))(f(x), f(y))) and forall([lam in carr(scal(m)), x in vec(m)], f((act(m))(lam, x)) = (mul(scal(m)))(lam, f(x))))
- `is-k-linear-on` — forall([m, s, f], is-k-linear-on(m, s, f) iff is-module(m) and is-submodule(m, s) and f in fun(s, carr(scal(m))) and forall([x in s, y in s], f((vadd(m))(x, y)) = (add(scal(m)))(f(x), f(y))) and forall([lam in carr(scal(m)), x in s], f((act(m))(lam, x)) = (mul(scal(m)))(lam, f(x))))
- `is-linear-functional` — forall([m, f], is-linear-functional(m, f) iff f in fun(vec(m), rr) and forall([x_ in vec(m), y_ in vec(m)], f((vadd(m))(x_, y_)) = f(x_) + f(y_)) and forall([r_ in rr, x_ in vec(m)], f((act(m))(r_, x_)) = r_ * f(x_)))
- `is-linear-functional-on` — forall([m, s, f], is-linear-functional-on(m, s, f) iff f in fun(s, rr) and forall([x_ in s, y_ in s], f((vadd(m))(x_, y_)) = f(x_) + f(y_)) and forall([r_ in rr, x_ in s], f((act(m))(r_, x_)) = r_ * f(x_)))
- `is-linear-map` — forall([m1, m2, tt], is-linear-map(m1, m2, tt) iff is-module(m1) and is-module(m2) and scal(m1) = scal(m2) and tt in fun(vec(m1), vec(m2)) and forall([x in vec(m1), y in vec(m1)], tt((vadd(m1))(x, y)) = (vadd(m2))(tt(x), tt(y))) and forall([lam in carr(scal(m1)), x in vec(m1)], tt((act(m1))(lam, x)) = (act(m2))(lam, tt(x))))
- `is-maximal` — forall([grd, porel, mx], is-maximal(grd, porel, mx) iff mx in grd and forall([y in grd], [mx, y] in porel implies [y, mx] in porel))
- `is-meager` — forall([s, a], is-meager(s, a) iff is-metric-space(s) and a subset pts(s) and forsome([ee in fun(nn, power(pts(s)))], forall([n in nn], is-nowhere-dense(s, ee(n))) and a subset big-union(n, nn, ee(n))))
- `is-measurable-fn` — forall([omega, ca, f], is-measurable-fn(omega, ca, f) iff is-sigma-algebra(omega, ca) and f in fun(omega, rr-pos-star) and forall([alpha_ in rr], {x_ in omega: alpha_ < f(x_)} in ca))
- `is-measurable-map` — forall([omega, ca, omega2, cb, f], is-measurable-map(omega, ca, omega2, cb, f) iff is-sigma-algebra(omega, ca) and is-sigma-algebra(omega2, cb) and f in fun(omega, omega2) and forall([b_ in cb], {x_ in omega: f(x_) in b_} in ca))
- `is-measurable-space` — forall([s], is-measurable-space(s) iff length(s) = 2 and pts(s) in set and sigma(s) in power(power(pts(s))) and is-sigma-algebra(pts(s), sigma(s)))
- `is-measure` — forall([omega, ca, mu], is-measure(omega, ca, mu) iff is-sigma-algebra(omega, ca) and mu in fun(ca, rr-pos-star) and mu(empty-set) = 0 and forall([f_ in fun(nn, ca)], forall([i_, j_], i_ in nn implies j_ in nn implies not(i_ = j_) implies intersection(f_(i_), f_(j_)) = empty-set) implies mu(big-union(n_, nn, f_(n_))) = esum(vnb-lambda(n_, nn, mu(f_(n_))))))
- `is-measure-space` — forall([s], is-measure-space(s) iff length(s) = 3 and pts(s) in set and sigma(s) in power(power(pts(s))) and meas(s) in fun(sigma(s), rr-pos-star) and is-measure(pts(s), sigma(s), meas(s)))
- `is-metric` — forall([dst, crr], is-metric(dst, crr) iff forall([u in crr], dst(u, u) = 0 and forall([v in crr], 0 <= dst(u, v) and (dst(u, v) = 0 implies u = v) and dst(u, v) = dst(v, u) and forall([w in crr], dst(u, w) <= dst(u, v) + dst(v, w)))))
- `is-metric-space` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) iff length(s) = 2 and pts(s) in set and dist(s) in fun(cartesian(pts(s), pts(s)), rr) and is-metric(dist(s), pts(s)))
- `is-metrizable-top-space-def` — forall([s], is-metrizable-top-space(s) iff is-top-space(s) and forsome([md], is-metric-space(md) and s == [pts(md), {u in power(pts(md)): is-open(md, u)}]))
- `is-module` — forall([s], is-module(s) iff length(s) = 6 and is-ring(scal(s)) and vec(s) in set and vadd(s) in fun(cartesian(vec(s), vec(s)), vec(s)) and vzero(s) in vec(s) and vneg(s) in fun(vec(s), vec(s)) and act(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(scal(s)), vec(s)), vec(s)) and is-associative(vadd(s), vec(s)) and is-commutative(vadd(s), vec(s)) and is-identity(vadd(s), vzero(s), vec(s)) and has-inverses(vadd(s), vzero(s), vneg(s), vec(s)) and forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s), y_ in vec(s)], (act(s))(r_, (vadd(s))(x_, y_)) = (vadd(s))((act(s))(r_, x_), (act(s))(r_, y_))) and forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), s_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s)], (act(s))((add(scal(s)))(r_, s_), x_) = (vadd(s))((act(s))(r_, x_), (act(s))(s_, x_))) and forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), s_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s)], (act(s))((mul(scal(s)))(r_, s_), x_) = (act(s))(r_, (act(s))(s_, x_))) and forall([x_ in vec(s)], (act(s))(one(scal(s)), x_) = x_))
- `is-monoid` — forall([s], is-monoid(s) iff length(s) = 3 and carr(s) in set and opr(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and iden(s) in carr(s) and is-associative(opr(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(opr(s), iden(s), carr(s)))
- `is-ms-sequence` — forall([ms], is-ms-sequence(ms) iff forall([n in nn], is-metric-space(ms(n))))
- `is-noetherian` — forall([m], is-noetherian(m) iff is-module(m) and forall([f_ in fun(nn, power(vec(m)))], forall([n_ in nn], is-submodule(m, f_(n_))) and forall([n_ in nn], f_(n_) subset f_(succ(n_))) implies forsome([k_ in nn], forall([n_], n_ in nn and k_ <= n_ implies f_(n_) = f_(k_)))))
- `is-nonmeager` — forall([s, a], is-nonmeager(s, a) iff is-metric-space(s) and a subset pts(s) and not(is-meager(s, a)))
- `is-norm` — forall([nm, addop, mulop, zr, crr], is-norm(nm, addop, mulop, zr, crr) iff nm in fun(crr, rr) and forall([a in crr], 0 <= nm(a) and (nm(a) = 0 iff a = zr) and forall([b in crr], nm(mulop(a, b)) = nm(a) * nm(b) and nm(addop(a, b)) <= nm(a) + nm(b))))
- `is-normed-ag` — forall([s], is-normed-ag(s) iff length(s) = 5 and carr(s) in set and opr(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and iden(s) in carr(s) and inv(s) in fun(carr(s), carr(s)) and nrm(s) in fun(carr(s), rr) and is-associative(opr(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(opr(s), iden(s), carr(s)) and has-inverses(opr(s), iden(s), inv(s), carr(s)) and is-commutative(opr(s), carr(s)) and is-group-norm(nrm(s), opr(s), inv(s), iden(s), carr(s)))
- `is-normed-field` — forall([s], is-normed-field(s) iff length(s) = 7 and carr(s) in set and add(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and mul(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and neg(s) in fun(carr(s), carr(s)) and zero(s) in carr(s) and one(s) in carr(s) and fnrm(s) in fun(carr(s), rr) and is-associative(add(s), carr(s)) and is-commutative(add(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(add(s), zero(s), carr(s)) and has-inverses(add(s), zero(s), neg(s), carr(s)) and is-associative(mul(s), carr(s)) and is-commutative(mul(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(mul(s), one(s), carr(s)) and is-distributive(add(s), mul(s), carr(s)) and is-norm(fnrm(s), add(s), mul(s), zero(s), carr(s)))
- `is-normed-vector-space` — forall([s], is-normed-vector-space(s) iff length(s) = 7 and is-ring(scal(s)) and vec(s) in set and vadd(s) in fun(cartesian(vec(s), vec(s)), vec(s)) and vzero(s) in vec(s) and vneg(s) in fun(vec(s), vec(s)) and act(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(scal(s)), vec(s)), vec(s)) and vnrm(s) in fun(vec(s), rr) and is-associative(vadd(s), vec(s)) and is-commutative(vadd(s), vec(s)) and is-identity(vadd(s), vzero(s), vec(s)) and has-inverses(vadd(s), vzero(s), vneg(s), vec(s)) and scal(s) = normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field) and forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s), y_ in vec(s)], (act(s))(r_, (vadd(s))(x_, y_)) = (vadd(s))((act(s))(r_, x_), (act(s))(r_, y_))) and forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), s_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s)], (act(s))((add(scal(s)))(r_, s_), x_) = (vadd(s))((act(s))(r_, x_), (act(s))(s_, x_))) and forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), s_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s)], (act(s))((mul(scal(s)))(r_, s_), x_) = (act(s))(r_, (act(s))(s_, x_))) and forall([x_ in vec(s)], (act(s))(one(scal(s)), x_) = x_) and forall([x_ in vec(s)], 0 <= (vnrm(s))(x_)) and forall([x_ in vec(s)], (vnrm(s))(x_) = 0 iff x_ = vzero(s)) and forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s)], (vnrm(s))((act(s))(r_, x_)) = abs(r_) * (vnrm(s))(x_)) and forall([x_ in vec(s), y_ in vec(s)], (vnrm(s))((vadd(s))(x_, y_)) <= (vnrm(s))(x_) + (vnrm(s))(y_)))
- `is-nowhere-dense` — forall([s, a], is-nowhere-dense(s, a) iff is-metric-space(s) and a subset pts(s) and interior(s, closure(s, a)) = empty-set)
- `is-open` — forall([s, u], is-open(s, u) iff is-metric-space(s) and u subset pts(s) and forall([y in u], forsome([r], pos-rr(r) and ball(s, y, r) subset u)))
- `is-open-cover` — forall([s, c], is-open-cover(s, c) iff is-metric-space(s) and forall([u in c], is-open(s, u)) and big-union(u, c, u) == pts(s))
- `is-open-gauge` — forall([fam, ground, u], is-open-gauge(fam, ground, u) iff u subset ground and forall([y in u], forsome([k_ in nn, r], pos-rr(r) and forall([x in ground], forall([n_ in nn], n_ <= k_ implies (dist(fam(n_)))(y, x) < r) implies x in u))))
- `is-open-lin-map` — forall([m1, fam1, m2, fam2, tt], is-open-lin-map(m1, fam1, m2, fam2, tt) iff is-linear-map(m1, m2, tt) and forall([n in nn, eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([n_ in nn, eps_], pos-rr(eps_) and fr-ball(m2, fam2, n_, eps_) subset image(tt, fr-ball(m1, fam1, n, eps)))))
- `is-ord` — forall([x], is-ord(x) iff x in ord)
- `is-outer-measure` — forall([omega, mu], is-outer-measure(omega, mu) iff omega in set and mu in fun(power(omega), rr-pos-star) and mu(empty-set) = 0 and forall([a_, b_], a_ in power(omega) implies b_ in power(omega) implies a_ subset b_ implies mu(a_) <= mu(b_)) and forall([f_ in fun(nn, power(omega))], mu(big-union(n_, nn, f_(n_))) <= esum(vnb-lambda(n_, nn, mu(f_(n_))))))
- `is-partial-order` — forall([grd, porel], is-partial-order(grd, porel) iff grd in set and porel in set and forall([x in grd], [x, x] in porel) and forall([x in grd, y in grd], [x, y] in porel and [y, x] in porel implies x = y) and forall([x in grd, y in grd, z in grd], [x, y] in porel and [y, z] in porel implies [x, z] in porel))
- `is-pid-def` — forall([s], is-pid(s) iff is-integral-domain(s) and forall([i], is-ideal(s, i) implies forsome([a in carr(s)], i = principal-ideal(s, a))))
- `is-pseudometric` — forall([dst, crr], is-pseudometric(dst, crr) iff forall([u in crr], dst(u, u) = 0 and forall([v in crr], 0 <= dst(u, v) and dst(u, v) = dst(v, u) and forall([w in crr], dst(u, w) <= dst(u, v) + dst(v, w)))))
- `is-pseudometric-space` — forall([s], is-pseudometric-space(s) iff length(s) = 2 and pts(s) in set and dist(s) in fun(cartesian(pts(s), pts(s)), rr) and is-pseudometric(dist(s), pts(s)))
- `is-ptwise-cauchy` — forall([s, fam], is-ptwise-cauchy(s, fam) iff is-metric-space(s) and fam in fun(nn, fun(pts(s), rr)) and forall([x in pts(s), eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([cap in nn], forall([k], k in nn and cap <= k implies forall([l], l in nn and cap <= l implies abs((fam(k))(x) - (fam(l))(x)) < eps)))))
- `is-r-net` — forall([s, f, a, r], is-r-net(s, f, a, r) iff forall([p in a], forsome([c in f], (dist(s))(c, p) <= r and not((dist(s))(c, p) = r))))
- `is-ring` — forall([s], is-ring(s) iff length(s) = 6 and carr(s) in set and add(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and mul(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and neg(s) in fun(carr(s), carr(s)) and zero(s) in carr(s) and one(s) in carr(s) and is-associative(add(s), carr(s)) and is-commutative(add(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(add(s), zero(s), carr(s)) and has-inverses(add(s), zero(s), neg(s), carr(s)) and is-associative(mul(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(mul(s), one(s), carr(s)) and is-distributive(add(s), mul(s), carr(s)))
- `is-ringoid` — forall([s], is-ringoid(s) iff length(s) = 7 and carr(s) in set and add(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and mul(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and neg(s) in fun(carr(s), carr(s)) and zero(s) in carr(s) and one(s) in carr(s) and idl(s) in power(carr(s)) and is-associative(add(s), carr(s)) and is-commutative(add(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(add(s), zero(s), carr(s)) and has-inverses(add(s), zero(s), neg(s), carr(s)) and is-associative(mul(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(mul(s), one(s), carr(s)) and is-distributive(add(s), mul(s), carr(s)) and is-ideal-in(idl(s), carr(s), add(s), mul(s), neg(s), zero(s)))
- `is-semigroup` — forall([s], is-semigroup(s) iff length(s) = 2 and carr(s) in set and opr(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and is-associative(opr(s), carr(s)))
- `is-seminorm` — forall([m, p], is-seminorm(m, p) iff is-module(m) and p in fun(vec(m), rr) and forall([x in vec(m)], 0 <= p(x)) and forall([x in vec(m), y in vec(m)], p((vadd(m))(x, y)) <= p(x) + p(y)) and forsome([fld], is-normed-field(fld) and scal(m) = normed-field-as-commutative-ring(fld) and forall([lam in carr(scal(m)), x in vec(m)], p((act(m))(lam, x)) = (fnrm(fld))(lam) * p(x))))
- `is-seminorm-family` — forall([m, fam], is-seminorm-family(m, fam) iff is-module(m) and forsome([fld], is-normed-field(fld) and scal(m) = normed-field-as-commutative-ring(fld)) and fam in fun(nn, fun(vec(m), rr)) and forall([k in nn], is-seminorm(m, fam(k))) and forall([x in vec(m)], not(x = vzero(m)) implies forsome([k in nn], not((fam(k))(x) = 0))))
- `is-separable` — forall([s], is-separable(s) iff is-metric-space(s) and forsome([dseq in fun(nn, pts(s))], forall([x in pts(s), eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([n in nn], (dist(s))(x, dseq(n)) < eps))))
- `is-set` — forall([x], is-set(x) iff x in set)
- `is-setoid` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) iff length(s) = 2 and pts(s) in set and rel(s) in set and is-equivalence(rel(s), pts(s)))
- `is-sigma-algebra` — forall([omega, ca], is-sigma-algebra(omega, ca) iff omega in set and ca subset power(omega) and omega in ca and forall([a_ in ca], complement-in(omega, a_) in ca) and forall([f_ in fun(nn, ca)], big-union(n_, nn, f_(n_)) in ca))
- `is-simple-fn` — forall([omega, ca, f], is-simple-fn(omega, ca, f) iff is-measurable-fn(omega, ca, f) and card(image(f, omega)) in nn and forall([x_ in omega], f(x_) in rr))
- `is-strictly-below` — forall([porel, x, y], is-strictly-below(porel, x, y) iff [x, y] in porel and not([y, x] in porel))
- `is-strictly-increasing-on` — forall([f, s], is-strictly-increasing-on(f, s) iff f in fun(rr, rr) and s subset rr and forall([u in s, v in s], u < v implies f(u) < f(v)))
- `is-submodule` — forall([m, s], is-submodule(m, s) iff s subset vec(m) and vzero(m) in s and forall([x_ in s, y_ in s], (vadd(m))(x_, y_) in s) and forall([x_ in s], (vneg(m))(x_) in s) and forall([r_ in carr(scal(m)), x_ in s], (act(m))(r_, x_) in s))
- `is-subsequence` — forall([s, y, f], is-subsequence(s, y, f) iff f in fun(nn, pts(s)) and forsome([phi], strictly-mono-nn(phi) and y = subseq(f, phi)))
- `is-subspace` — forall([m, s], is-subspace(m, s) iff is-submodule(m, s))
- `is-summable` — forall([grp, f], is-summable(grp, f) iff forsome([r in carr(grp)], sums-to(grp, f, r)))
- `is-top-space` — forall([s], is-top-space(s) iff length(s) = 2 and pts(s) in set and opens(s) in power(power(pts(s))) and empty-set in opens(s) and pts(s) in opens(s) and forall([u in opens(s), v in opens(s)], intersection(u, v) in opens(s)) and forall([fam], fam subset opens(s) implies big-union(u, fam, u) in opens(s)))
- `is-unif-cauchy` — forall([s, fam], is-unif-cauchy(s, fam) iff is-metric-space(s) and fam in fun(nn, fun(pts(s), rr)) and forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([cap in nn], forall([k], k in nn and cap <= k implies forall([l], l in nn and cap <= l implies forall([x in pts(s)], abs((fam(k))(x) - (fam(l))(x)) < eps))))))
- `is-uniformly-continuous` — forall([s, t, f], is-uniformly-continuous(s, t, f) iff is-metric-space(s) and is-metric-space(t) and f in fun(pts(s), pts(t)) and forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([delta], pos-rr(delta) and forall([a in pts(s), b in pts(s)], (dist(s))(a, b) <= delta implies (dist(t))(f(a), f(b)) <= eps))))
- `is-upper-bound` — forall([grd, porel, ch, b], is-upper-bound(grd, porel, ch, b) iff b in grd and forall([x in ch], [x, b] in porel))
- `is-vector-space-def` — forall([s], is-vector-space(s) iff is-module(s) and is-field-ring(scal(s)))
- `keep-set-membership` — forall([phi, grd, porel, kset, alpha, y_], y_ in keep-set(phi, grd, porel, kset, alpha) iff y_ in grd and y_ = phi(alpha) and forall([z in kset], [z, y_] in porel))
- `lastcoeff-set-membership` — forall([md, p, u, sm, r_], r_ in lastcoeff-set(md, p, u, sm) iff r_ in carr(scal(md)) and forsome([c_ in mat(1, succ(p), carr(scal(md)))], entry(c_, 1, succ(p)) = r_ and entry(matact(md, c_, u), 1, 1) in sm))
- `length-cons` — forall([x, l], length(cons(x, l)) = succ(length(l)))
- `limit-ord-iff` — forall([lambda], limit-ord(lambda) iff lambda in ord and not(lambda = 0) and not(forsome([alpha in ord], lambda = succ_ord(alpha))))
- `little-o-at` — forall([g, a], little-o-at(g, a) iff g in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and forsome([eps in fun(rr, rr)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, eps, a) and eps(a) = 0 and forall([x in rr], g(x) = eps(x) * (x - a))))
- `magnitude-def` — forall([z], magnitude(z) == sqrt(real-part(z) * real-part(z) + imag-part(z) * imag-part(z)))
- `makeset-cons` — forall([x, l], make-set(cons(x, l)) = union(pair(x, x), make-set(l)))
- `mat-equiv` — forall([a, m, n, c, d], mat-equiv(a, m, n, c, d) iff forsome([u], is-invertible-mat(a, m, u) and forsome([v], is-invertible-mat(a, n, v) and d = matmul(a, matmul(a, u, c), v))))
- `matrix-membership` — forall([s, m], m in matrix(s) iff m in tuples(tuples(s)) and forall([i], i in nn and 1 <= i and i <= length(m) implies forall([j], j in nn and 1 <= j and j <= length(m) implies length(nth(i, m)) = length(nth(j, m)))))
- `measurable-space-class` — forall([s], s in measurable-space iff is-measurable-space(s))
- `measure-space-as-measurable-space-is-measurable-space` — forall([r], is-measure-space(r) implies is-measurable-space(measure-space-as-measurable-space(r)))
- `measure-space-class` — forall([s], s in measure-space iff is-measure-space(s))
- `metric-space-class` — forall([s], s in metric-space iff is-metric-space(s))
- `metrizable-top-space-class` — forall([s], s in metrizable-top-space iff is-metrizable-top-space(s))
- `module-act-distrib-scalar` — forall([m], is-module(m) implies forall([r_ in carr(scal(m)), s_ in carr(scal(m)), x_ in vec(m)], (act(m))((add(scal(m)))(r_, s_), x_) = (vadd(m))((act(m))(r_, x_), (act(m))(s_, x_))))
- `module-act-distrib-scalar-complex-inner-product-space-as-module` — forall([r], is-complex-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([r_ in carr(scal(r)), s_ in carr(scal(r)), x_ in vec(r)], (act(r))((add(scal(r)))(r_, s_), x_) = (vadd(r))((act(r))(r_, x_), (act(r))(s_, x_))))
- `module-act-distrib-scalar-normed-vector-space-as-module` — forall([r], is-normed-vector-space(r) implies forall([r_ in carr(scal(r)), s_ in carr(scal(r)), x_ in vec(r)], (act(r))((add(scal(r)))(r_, s_), x_) = (vadd(r))((act(r))(r_, x_), (act(r))(s_, x_))))
- `module-act-distrib-vec` — forall([m], is-module(m) implies forall([r_ in carr(scal(m)), x_ in vec(m), y_ in vec(m)], (act(m))(r_, (vadd(m))(x_, y_)) = (vadd(m))((act(m))(r_, x_), (act(m))(r_, y_))))
- `module-act-distrib-vec-complex-inner-product-space-as-module` — forall([r], is-complex-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([r_ in carr(scal(r)), x_ in vec(r), y_ in vec(r)], (act(r))(r_, (vadd(r))(x_, y_)) = (vadd(r))((act(r))(r_, x_), (act(r))(r_, y_))))
- `module-act-distrib-vec-normed-vector-space-as-module` — forall([r], is-normed-vector-space(r) implies forall([r_ in carr(scal(r)), x_ in vec(r), y_ in vec(r)], (act(r))(r_, (vadd(r))(x_, y_)) = (vadd(r))((act(r))(r_, x_), (act(r))(r_, y_))))
- `module-act-mul-compat` — forall([m], is-module(m) implies forall([r_ in carr(scal(m)), s_ in carr(scal(m)), x_ in vec(m)], (act(m))((mul(scal(m)))(r_, s_), x_) = (act(m))(r_, (act(m))(s_, x_))))
- `module-act-mul-compat-complex-inner-product-space-as-module` — forall([r], is-complex-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([r_ in carr(scal(r)), s_ in carr(scal(r)), x_ in vec(r)], (act(r))((mul(scal(r)))(r_, s_), x_) = (act(r))(r_, (act(r))(s_, x_))))
- `module-act-mul-compat-normed-vector-space-as-module` — forall([r], is-normed-vector-space(r) implies forall([r_ in carr(scal(r)), s_ in carr(scal(r)), x_ in vec(r)], (act(r))((mul(scal(r)))(r_, s_), x_) = (act(r))(r_, (act(r))(s_, x_))))

- `nag-metric-space-is-metric-space-normed-vector-space-as-normed-ag` — forall([r], is-normed-vector-space(r) implies is-metric-space(nag-metric-space(normed-vector-space-as-normed-ag(r))))
- `nary-minus-2` — forall([x, y], x - y == binplus(x, binneg(y)))
- `nary-neg-1` — forall([x], -x == binneg(x))
- `nary-plus-2` — forall([x, y], x + y == binplus(x, y))
- `nary-times-2` — forall([x, y], x * y == bintimes(x, y))
- `nf-metric-carrier` — forall([nf], pts(nf-metric-space(nf)) == carr(nf))
- `nf-metric-distance` — forall([nf], is-normed-field(nf) implies forall([x in carr(nf), y in carr(nf)], (dist(nf-metric-space(nf)))(x, y) = (fnrm(nf))((add(nf))(x, (neg(nf))(y)))))
- `nn-add-monoid-def` — nn-add-monoid = [nn, vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(nn, nn), x_ + y_), 0]
- `nn-add-monoid@carr` — carr(nn-add-monoid) == nn
- `nn-add-monoid@iden` — iden(nn-add-monoid) == 0
- `nn-add-monoid@opr` — opr(nn-add-monoid) == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(nn, nn), x_ + y_)
- `nn-add-succ` — forall([a in nn, b in nn], a + succ(b) = succ(a + b))
- `nn-minus-def` — forall([k in nn, l in nn], nn-minus(k, l) = if(l <= k, k - l, 0))

- `normed-ag-class` — forall([s], s in normed-ag iff is-normed-ag(s))
- `normed-ag-nrm-definite-normed-vector-space-as-normed-ag` — forall([r], is-normed-vector-space(r) implies forall([a in vec(r)], (vnrm(r))(a) = 0 iff a = vzero(r)))
- `normed-ag-nrm-nonneg-normed-vector-space-as-normed-ag` — forall([r], is-normed-vector-space(r) implies forall([a in vec(r)], 0 <= (vnrm(r))(a)))
- `normed-ag-nrm-subadditive-normed-vector-space-as-normed-ag` — forall([r], is-normed-vector-space(r) implies forall([a in vec(r), b in vec(r)], (vnrm(r))((vadd(r))(a, b)) <= (vnrm(r))(a) + (vnrm(r))(b)))
- `normed-ag-nrm-type-normed-vector-space-as-normed-ag` — forall([r], is-normed-vector-space(r) implies vnrm(r) in fun(vec(r), rr))
