- `normed-vector-space-class` — forall([s], s in normed-vector-space iff is-normed-vector-space(s))
- `npe` — forall([m, s, f, t, g], npe(m, s, f, t, g) iff is-submodule(m, t) and s subset t and is-linear-functional-on(m, t, g) and extends-on(s, g, f) and forall([w_ in t], abs(g(w_)) <= dual-norm-on(m, s, f) * (vnrm(m))(w_)))
- `nth-cons-1` — forall([x, l], nth(1, cons(x, l)) = x)
- `nth-cons-succ` — forall([i, x, l], i in nn and 1 <= i and i <= length(l) implies nth(succ(i), cons(x, l)) = nth(i, l))
- `nth-deriv-succ` — forall([f, n in nn], nth-deriv(f, succ(n)) == vnb-lambda(x, rr, deriv(nth-deriv(f, n), x)))
- `nth-deriv-v-succ` — forall([m, f, n in nn], nth-deriv-v(m, f, succ(n)) == vnb-lambda(x, rr, deriv-v(m, nth-deriv-v(m, f, n), x)))
- `nth-deriv-v-zero` — forall([m, f], nth-deriv-v(m, f, 0) == f)
- `nth-deriv-zero` — forall([f], nth-deriv(f, 0) == f)
- `null-rr-seq` — forall([rad], null-rr-seq(rad) iff rad in fun(nn, rr) and forall([k in nn], pos-rr(rad(k))) and forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([n in nn], forall([k], k in nn and n <= k implies rad(k) <= eps))))
- `ord-lt-iff` — forall([alpha, beta], ord-lt(alpha, beta) iff ord-le(alpha, beta) and not(alpha = beta))
- `pid-class` — forall([s], s in pid iff is-pid(s))
- `pointwise-bounded` — forall([s, fam], pointwise-bounded(s, fam) iff is-metric-space(s) and fam in fun(nn, fun(pts(s), rr)) and forall([x in pts(s)], forsome([bd in rr], forall([k in nn], abs((fam(k))(x)) <= bd))))
- `pos-rr` — forall([r], pos-rr(r) iff r in rr and 0 <= r and not(0 = r))
- `preimage-membership` — forall([s, f, v, a], a in preimage(s, f, v) iff a in pts(s) and f(a) in v)
- `principal-ideal-membership` — forall([s, a, x], x in principal-ideal(s, a) iff x in carr(s) and forsome([r in carr(s)], x = (mul(s))(r, a)))
- `prod-ord-succ` — forall([m, f, n in nn], prod-ord(m, f, succ(n)) == (opr(m))(prod-ord(m, f, n), f(n)))
- `prod-ord-zero` — forall([m, f], prod-ord(m, f, 0) == iden(m))
- `ps-absolutely-converges-at` — forall([coef, x], ps-absolutely-converges-at(coef, x) iff ps-converges-at(vnb-lambda(n, nn, abs(coef(n))), abs(x)))
- `ps-converges-at` — forall([coef, x], ps-converges-at(coef, x) iff converges(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(k, nn, ps-partial-sum(coef, x, k))))
- `ps-converges-to-at` — forall([coef, x, l], ps-converges-to-at(coef, x, l) iff converges-to(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(k, nn, ps-partial-sum(coef, x, k)), l))
- `ps-ratio-limit` — forall([coef, l], ps-ratio-limit(coef, l) iff converges-to(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(n, nn, abs(coef(succ(n)) * recip(coef(n)))), l))
- `pseudometric-space-class` — forall([s], s in pseudometric-space iff is-pseudometric-space(s))
- `ptwise-le` — forall([omega, f, g], ptwise-le(omega, f, g) iff forall([x_ in omega], f(x_) <= g(x_)))
- `qq-field-def` — qq-field = [qq, vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(qq, qq), x_ + y_), vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(qq, qq), x_ * y_), vnb-lambda(x_, qq, -x_), 0, 1, difference(qq, singleton(0)), vnb-lambda(x_, difference(qq, singleton(0)), recip(x_))]
- `qq-field@add` — add(qq-field) == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(qq, qq), x_ + y_)
- `qq-field@carr` — carr(qq-field) == qq
- `qq-field@mul` — mul(qq-field) == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(qq, qq), x_ * y_)
- `qq-field@mul-inv` — mul-inv(qq-field) == vnb-lambda(x_, difference(qq, singleton(0)), recip(x_))
- `qq-field@neg` — neg(qq-field) == vnb-lambda(x_, qq, -x_)
- `qq-field@non-zero` — non-zero(qq-field) == difference(qq, singleton(0))
- `qq-field@one` — one(qq-field) == 1
- `qq-field@zero` — zero(qq-field) == 0
- `qq-line-def` — qq-line = [field-as-integral-domain(qq-field), qq, vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(qq, qq), x_ + y_), 0, vnb-lambda(x_, qq, -x_), vnb-lambda([r_, x_], cartesian(qq, qq), r_ * x_)]
- `qq-line@act` — act(qq-line) == vnb-lambda([r_, x_], cartesian(qq, qq), r_ * x_)
- `qq-line@scal` — scal(qq-line) == field-as-integral-domain(qq-field)
- `qq-line@vadd` — vadd(qq-line) == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(qq, qq), x_ + y_)
- `qq-line@vec` — vec(qq-line) == qq
- `qq-line@vneg` — vneg(qq-line) == vnb-lambda(x_, qq, -x_)
- `qq-line@vzero` — vzero(qq-line) == 0
- `qq-ring-def` — qq-ring = [qq, vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(qq, qq), x_ + y_), vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(qq, qq), x_ * y_), vnb-lambda(x_, qq, -x_), 0, 1]
- `qq-ring@add` — add(qq-ring) == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(qq, qq), x_ + y_)
- `qq-ring@carr` — carr(qq-ring) == qq
- `qq-ring@mul` — mul(qq-ring) == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(qq, qq), x_ * y_)
- `qq-ring@neg` — neg(qq-ring) == vnb-lambda(x_, qq, -x_)
- `qq-ring@one` — one(qq-ring) == 1
- `qq-ring@zero` — zero(qq-ring) == 0
- `real-part-def` — forall([z], real-part(z) == (z + conjugate(z)) * recip(2))
- `reduce-one` — forall([op, f], reduce(op, f, 1) == f(0))
- `reduce-succ` — forall([op, f, n], n in nn and 1 <= n implies reduce(op, f, succ(n)) == op(reduce(op, f, n), f(n)))
- `rel-free` — forall([md, n, u], rel-free(md, n, u) iff forall([c_ in mat(1, n, carr(scal(md)))], entry(matact(md, c_, u), 1, 1) = vzero(md) implies forall([j_ in interval(1, n)], entry(c_, 1, j_) = zero(scal(md)))))
- `ring-add-assoc` — forall([s], is-ring(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s), c in carr(s)], (add(s))((add(s))(a, b), c) = (add(s))(a, (add(s))(b, c))))
- `ring-add-assoc-ringoid-as-ring` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a in carr(r), b in carr(r), c in carr(r)], (add(r))((add(r))(a, b), c) = (add(r))(a, (add(r))(b, c))))
- `ring-add-comm` — forall([s], is-ring(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], (add(s))(a, b) = (add(s))(b, a)))
- `ring-add-comm-ringoid-as-ring` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a in carr(r), b in carr(r)], (add(r))(a, b) = (add(r))(b, a)))
- `ring-add-left-id` — forall([s], is-ring(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], (add(s))(zero(s), a) = a))
- `ring-add-left-id-ringoid-as-ring` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a in carr(r)], (add(r))(zero(r), a) = a))
- `ring-add-left-inv` — forall([s], is-ring(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], (add(s))((neg(s))(a), a) = zero(s)))
- `ring-add-left-inv-ringoid-as-ring` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a in carr(r)], (add(r))((neg(r))(a), a) = zero(r)))
- `ring-additive-ag-is-abelian-group` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies is-abelian-group(ring-additive-ag(r)))
- `ring-carr-in-set-ringoid-as-ring` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies carr(r) in set)
- `ring-carrier-closed-add` — forall([r, a, b], is-ring(r) and a in carr(r) and b in carr(r) implies (add(r))(a, b) in carr(r))
- `ring-class` — forall([s], s in ring iff is-ring(s))
- `ring-left-dist` — forall([s], is-ring(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s), c in carr(s)], (mul(s))(a, (add(s))(b, c)) = (add(s))((mul(s))(a, b), (mul(s))(a, c))))
- `ring-left-dist-ringoid-as-ring` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a in carr(r), b in carr(r), c in carr(r)], (mul(r))(a, (add(r))(b, c)) = (add(r))((mul(r))(a, b), (mul(r))(a, c))))
- `ring-mul-assoc` — forall([s], is-ring(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s), c in carr(s)], (mul(s))((mul(s))(a, b), c) = (mul(s))(a, (mul(s))(b, c))))
- `ring-mul-assoc-ringoid-as-ring` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a in carr(r), b in carr(r), c in carr(r)], (mul(r))((mul(r))(a, b), c) = (mul(r))(a, (mul(r))(b, c))))
- `ring-mul-left-id` — forall([s], is-ring(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], (mul(s))(one(s), a) = a))
- `ring-mul-left-id-ringoid-as-ring` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a in carr(r)], (mul(r))(one(r), a) = a))
- `ring-mul-right-id` — forall([s], is-ring(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], (mul(s))(a, one(s)) = a))
- `ring-mul-right-id-ringoid-as-ring` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a in carr(r)], (mul(r))(a, one(r)) = a))
- `ring-mul-zero-left-ringoid-as-ring` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a in carr(r)], (mul(r))(zero(r), a) = zero(r)))
- `ring-mul-zero-right-ringoid-as-ring` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a in carr(r)], (mul(r))(a, zero(r)) = zero(r)))
- `ring-multiplicative-monoid-is-monoid` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies is-monoid(ring-multiplicative-monoid(r)))
- `ring-neg-mul-left-ringoid-as-ring` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a in carr(r), b in carr(r)], (mul(r))((neg(r))(a), b) = (neg(r))((mul(r))(a, b))))
- `ring-prod-is-ring-ringoid-as-ring` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([y], is-ring(y) implies is-ring(ring-prod(ringoid-as-ring(r), y))))
- `ring-prod-n-succ` — forall([f, n in nn], ring-prod-n(f, succ(n)) == ring-prod(ring-prod-n(f, n), f(n)))
- `ring-prod-n-zero` — forall([f], ring-prod-n(f, 0) == zero-ring)
- `ring-right-dist` — forall([s], is-ring(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s), c in carr(s)], (mul(s))((add(s))(a, b), c) = (add(s))((mul(s))(a, c), (mul(s))(b, c))))
- `ring-right-dist-ringoid-as-ring` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a in carr(r), b in carr(r), c in carr(r)], (mul(r))((add(r))(a, b), c) = (add(r))((mul(r))(a, c), (mul(r))(b, c))))
- `ring-zero-in` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies zero(r) in carr(r))
- `ring-zero-in-ringoid-as-ring` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies zero(r) in carr(r))
- `ringoid-add-right-inv` — forall([r, a], is-ringoid(r) implies a in carr(r) implies (add(r))(a, (neg(r))(a)) = zero(r))
- `ringoid-as-ring-is-ring` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies is-ring(ringoid-as-ring(r)))
- `ringoid-carr-in-set` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies carr(r) in set)
- `ringoid-class` — forall([s], s in ringoid iff is-ringoid(s))
- `ringoid-ideal-add` — forall([r, a, b], is-ringoid(r) implies a in idl(r) implies b in idl(r) implies (add(r))(a, b) in idl(r))
- `ringoid-ideal-mul-left` — forall([r, x_, a], is-ringoid(r) implies x_ in carr(r) implies a in idl(r) implies (mul(r))(x_, a) in idl(r))
- `ringoid-ideal-mul-right` — forall([r, a, x_], is-ringoid(r) implies a in idl(r) implies x_ in carr(r) implies (mul(r))(a, x_) in idl(r))
- `ringoid-ideal-neg` — forall([r, a], is-ringoid(r) implies a in idl(r) implies (neg(r))(a) in idl(r))
- `ringoid-ideal-subset` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies idl(r) subset carr(r))
- `ringoid-ideal-zero` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies zero(r) in idl(r))
- `ringoid-rel-mem` — forall([r, a, b], [a, b] in ringoid-rel(r) iff [a, b] in cartesian(carr(r), carr(r)) and (add(r))(a, (neg(r))(b)) in idl(r))
- `ringoid-setoid-pts` — forall([r], pts(ringoid-setoid(r)) == carr(r))
- `rq-add` — forall([r], add(ringoid-quotient-ring(r)) == descend2(ringoid-setoid(r), vnb-lambda([a, b], cartesian(carr(r), carr(r)), class(ringoid-setoid(r), (add(r))(a, b)))))
- `rq-carr` — forall([r], carr(ringoid-quotient-ring(r)) == quotient(ringoid-setoid(r)))
- `rq-mul` — forall([r], mul(ringoid-quotient-ring(r)) == descend2(ringoid-setoid(r), vnb-lambda([a, b], cartesian(carr(r), carr(r)), class(ringoid-setoid(r), (mul(r))(a, b)))))
- `rq-neg` — forall([r], neg(ringoid-quotient-ring(r)) == descend(ringoid-setoid(r), vnb-lambda(a, carr(r), class(ringoid-setoid(r), (neg(r))(a)))))
- `rq-one` — forall([r], one(ringoid-quotient-ring(r)) == class(ringoid-setoid(r), one(r)))
- `rq-zero` — forall([r], zero(ringoid-quotient-ring(r)) == class(ringoid-setoid(r), zero(r)))
- `rr-abs-def` — forall([x in rr], (0 <= x implies abs(x) = x) and (not(0 <= x) implies abs(x) = -x))
- `rr-bounded-above` — forall([s], rr-bounded-above(s) iff forsome([b], rr-upper-bound(s, b)))
- `rr-max-def` — forall([x in rr, y in rr], (y <= x implies max(x, y) = x) and (not(y <= x) implies max(x, y) = y))
- `rr-min-def` — forall([x in rr, y in rr], (x <= y implies min(x, y) = x) and (not(x <= y) implies min(x, y) = y))
- `rr-ms-def` — rr-ms = [rr, vnb-lambda([x, y], cartesian(rr, rr), abs(x - y))]
- `rr-ms@dist` — dist(rr-ms) == vnb-lambda([x, y], cartesian(rr, rr), abs(x - y))
- `rr-ms@pts` — pts(rr-ms) == rr
- `rr-normed-field-def` — rr-normed-field = [rr, vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(rr, rr), x_ + y_), vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(rr, rr), x_ * y_), vnb-lambda(x_, rr, -x_), 0, 1, vnb-lambda(x_, rr, abs(x_))]
- `rr-normed-field@add` — add(rr-normed-field) == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(rr, rr), x_ + y_)
- `rr-normed-field@carr` — carr(rr-normed-field) == rr
- `rr-normed-field@fnrm` — fnrm(rr-normed-field) == vnb-lambda(x_, rr, abs(x_))
- `rr-normed-field@mul` — mul(rr-normed-field) == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(rr, rr), x_ * y_)
- `rr-normed-field@neg` — neg(rr-normed-field) == vnb-lambda(x_, rr, -x_)
- `rr-normed-field@one` — one(rr-normed-field) == 1
- `rr-normed-field@zero` — zero(rr-normed-field) == 0
- `rr-nvs-def` — rr-nvs = [normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field), rr, vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(rr, rr), x_ + y_), 0, vnb-lambda(x_, rr, -x_), vnb-lambda([r_, x_], cartesian(rr, rr), r_ * x_), vnb-lambda(x_, rr, abs(x_))]
- `rr-nvs@act` — act(rr-nvs) == vnb-lambda([r_, x_], cartesian(rr, rr), r_ * x_)
- `rr-nvs@scal` — scal(rr-nvs) == normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field)
- `rr-nvs@vadd` — vadd(rr-nvs) == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(rr, rr), x_ + y_)
- `rr-nvs@vec` — vec(rr-nvs) == rr
- `rr-nvs@vneg` — vneg(rr-nvs) == vnb-lambda(x_, rr, -x_)
- `rr-nvs@vnrm` — vnrm(rr-nvs) == vnb-lambda(x_, rr, abs(x_))
- `rr-nvs@vzero` — vzero(rr-nvs) == 0
- `rr-pos-star-membership` — forall([x], x in rr-pos-star iff x in rr and 0 <= x or x = pos-inf)
- `rr-star-membership` — forall([x], x in rr-star iff x in rr or x = pos-inf or x = neg-inf)
- `rr-upper-bound` — forall([s, b], rr-upper-bound(s, b) iff b in rr and forall([x in s], x <= b))
- `semigroup-class` — forall([s], s in semigroup iff is-semigroup(s))
- `seq-compact` — forall([s], seq-compact(s) iff is-metric-space(s) and forall([f in fun(nn, pts(s))], forsome([phi], strictly-mono-nn(phi) and forsome([l in pts(s)], converges-to(s, subseq(f, phi), l)))))
- `series-converges` — forall([f], series-converges(f) iff converges(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(k, nn, series-partial-sum(f, k))))
- `series-converges-to` — forall([f, l], series-converges-to(f, l) iff converges-to(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(k, nn, series-partial-sum(f, k)), l))
- `setoid-class` — forall([s], s in setoid iff is-setoid(s))
- `sigma-generated-membership` — forall([omega, ce, a_], a_ in sigma-generated(omega, ce) iff a_ in power(omega) and forall([cb_], is-sigma-algebra(omega, cb_) and ce subset cb_ implies a_ in cb_))
- `smith-staircase` — forall([a, m, n, d, k], smith-staircase(a, m, n, d, k) iff is-diagonal(a, m, n, d) and k <= m and k <= n and forall([i_ in interval(1, k)], not(entry(d, i_, i_) = zero(a))) and forall([i_ in interval(1, m)], not(i_ <= k) implies forall([j_ in interval(1, n)], entry(d, i_, j_) = zero(a))))
- `span-membership` — forall([md, n, u, x_], x_ in span(md, n, u) iff x_ in vec(md) and forsome([c_ in mat(1, n, carr(scal(md)))], x_ = entry(matact(md, c_, u), 1, 1)))
- `spans` — forall([md, n, u, sm], spans(md, n, u, sm) iff forall([j_ in interval(1, n)], entry(u, j_, 1) in sm) and forall([x_ in sm], forsome([c_ in mat(1, n, carr(scal(md)))], x_ = entry(matact(md, c_, u), 1, 1))))
- `sqn-membership` — forall([a, f_], f_ in sqn(a) iff f_ in fun(nn, a))
- `sqn-sethood` — forall([a in set], sqn(a) in set)
- `strictly-mono-nn` — forall([phi], strictly-mono-nn(phi) iff phi in fun(nn, nn) and forall([m in nn, n_ in nn], m < n_ implies phi(m) < phi(n_)))
- `submodule-act-closed` — forall([m, s], is-submodule(m, s) implies forall([r_ in carr(scal(m)), x_ in s], (act(m))(r_, x_) in s))
- `submodule-vadd-closed` — forall([m, s], is-submodule(m, s) implies forall([x_ in s, y_ in s], (vadd(m))(x_, y_) in s))
- `submodule-vneg-closed` — forall([m, s], is-submodule(m, s) implies forall([x_ in s], (vneg(m))(x_) in s))
- `submodule-vzero-in` — forall([m, s], is-submodule(m, s) implies vzero(m) in s)
- `subsqn` — forall([y, f], subsqn(y, f) iff forsome([phi], strictly-mono-nn(phi) and y = subseq(f, phi)))
- `sum-ag-as-reduce-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([f in fun(nn, carr(r)), n], n in nn and 1 <= n implies sum-ag(ring-additive-ag(r), f, n) = reduce(add(r), f, n)))
- `sum-ag-singleton-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([f in fun(nn, carr(r))], sum-ag(ring-additive-ag(r), f, 1) = f(0)))
- `sum-ag-succ` — forall([ag, f, n in nn], sum-ag(ag, f, succ(n)) == (opr(ag))(sum-ag(ag, f, n), f(n)))
- `sum-ag-type-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([f in fun(nn, carr(r)), n in nn], sum-ag(ring-additive-ag(r), f, n) in carr(r)))
- `sum-ag-zero` — forall([ag, f], sum-ag(ag, f, 0) == iden(ag))
- `sum-succ` — forall([r, f, n in nn], sum(r, f, succ(n)) == (add(r))(sum(r, f, n), f(n)))
- `sum-zero` — forall([r, f], sum(r, f, 0) == zero(r))
- `summable-weight` — forall([w], summable-weight(w) iff w in fun(nn, rr) and forall([n in nn], 0 < w(n)) and series-converges(w))
- `sums-to` — forall([grp, f, r], sums-to(grp, f, r) iff forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([fin in set], card(fin) in nn and fin subset dom(f) and forall([ext], ext in set and card(ext) in nn and fin subset ext and ext subset dom(f) implies (nrm(grp))((opr(grp))(r, (inv(grp))(finsum(normed-ag-as-abelian-group(grp), f, ext)))) < eps))))
- `taylor-differentiable` — forall([f, a, x, n], taylor-differentiable(f, a, x, n) iff forall([k], k in nn and k <= n implies forall([t in ccint(a, x)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, nth-deriv(f, k), t))) and forall([k], k in nn and k <= n implies forall([t], a < t and t < x implies is-diff-at(nth-deriv(f, k), t, (nth-deriv(f, succ(k)))(t)))))
- `taylor-differentiable-v` — forall([m, f, a, x, n], taylor-differentiable-v(m, f, a, x, n) iff forall([k], k in nn and k <= n implies forall([t in ccint(a, x)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, nvs-metric-space(m), nth-deriv-v(m, f, k), t))) and forall([k], k in nn and k <= n implies forall([t], a < t and t < x implies is-diff-at-v(m, nth-deriv-v(m, f, k), t, (nth-deriv-v(m, f, succ(k)))(t)))))
- `taylor-poly-v-succ` — forall([m, f, a, x, n in nn], taylor-poly-v(m, f, a, x, succ(n)) == (vadd(m))(taylor-poly-v(m, f, a, x, n), (act(m))((x - a) ^ succ(n) * recip(factorial(succ(n))), (nth-deriv-v(m, f, succ(n)))(a))))
- `taylor-poly-v-zero` — forall([m, f, a, x], taylor-poly-v(m, f, a, x, 0) == (act(m))((x - a) ^ 0 * recip(factorial(0)), (nth-deriv-v(m, f, 0))(a)))
- `top-space-class` — forall([s], s in top-space iff is-top-space(s))
- `totally-bounded` — forall([s], totally-bounded(s) iff is-metric-space(s) and forall([r], r in rr and 0 <= r and not(0 = r) implies forsome([f], card(f) in nn and is-r-net(s, f, pts(s), r))))
- `trinum-succ` — forall([n_ in nn], trinum(succ(n_)) == trinum(n_) + succ(n_))
- `trinum-zero` — trinum(0) == 0
- `vector-space-class` — forall([s], s in vector-space iff is-vector-space(s))
- `zen-limit` — forall([x, lam], limit-ord(lam) implies zen(x, lam) = choice({y_ in x: forall([c_], ord-lt(c_, lam) implies not(zen(x, c_) = y_))}))
- `zen-succ` — forall([x, alpha in ord], zen(x, succ_ord(alpha)) = choice({y_ in x: forall([c_], ord-le(c_, alpha) implies not(zen(x, c_) = y_))}))
- `zen-zero` — forall([x], zen(x, 0) = choice(x))
- `zero-ring-def` — zero-ring = [{0}, vnb-lambda([p, q], cartesian({0}, {0}), 0), vnb-lambda([p, q], cartesian({0}, {0}), 0), vnb-lambda([p], {0}, 0), 0, 0]
- `zkept-limit` — forall([phi, grd, porel, lam], limit-ord(lam) implies zkept(phi, grd, porel, lam) = big-union(beta, ord-segment(lam), zkept(phi, grd, porel, beta)))
- `zkept-succ` — forall([phi, grd, porel, alpha in ord], zkept(phi, grd, porel, succ_ord(alpha)) = union(zkept(phi, grd, porel, alpha), keep-set(phi, grd, porel, zkept(phi, grd, porel, alpha), alpha)))
- `zkept-zero` — forall([phi, grd, porel], zkept(phi, grd, porel, 0) = empty-set)
- `zup-limit` — forall([grd, porel, lam], limit-ord(lam) implies zup(grd, porel, lam) = choice({y_ in grd: forall([c_], ord-lt(c_, lam) implies [zup(grd, porel, c_), y_] in porel)}))
- `zup-succ` — forall([grd, porel, alpha in ord], zup(grd, porel, succ_ord(alpha)) = choice({y_ in grd: is-strictly-below(porel, zup(grd, porel, alpha), y_)}))
- `zup-zero` — forall([grd, porel], zup(grd, porel, 0) = choice(grd))
- `zz-act-add-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([j in zz, k in zz, a in carr(r)], zz-act(ring-additive-ag(r), j + k, a) = (add(r))(zz-act(ring-additive-ag(r), j, a), zz-act(ring-additive-ag(r), k, a))))
- `zz-act-assoc-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([j in zz, k in zz, a in carr(r)], zz-act(ring-additive-ag(r), j * k, a) = zz-act(ring-additive-ag(r), j, zz-act(ring-additive-ag(r), k, a))))
- `zz-act-distrib-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([k in zz, a in carr(r), b in carr(r)], zz-act(ring-additive-ag(r), k, (add(r))(a, b)) = (add(r))(zz-act(ring-additive-ag(r), k, a), zz-act(ring-additive-ag(r), k, b))))
- `zz-act-neg-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([k in nn, a in carr(r)], zz-act(ring-additive-ag(r), -k, a) = (neg(r))(mpow(abelian-group-as-monoid(ring-additive-ag(r)), a, k))))
- `zz-act-neg-sign-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([k in zz, a in carr(r)], zz-act(ring-additive-ag(r), -k, a) = (neg(r))(zz-act(ring-additive-ag(r), k, a))))
- `zz-act-nonneg-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([k in nn, a in carr(r)], zz-act(ring-additive-ag(r), k, a) = mpow(abelian-group-as-monoid(ring-additive-ag(r)), a, k)))
- `zz-act-one-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([a in carr(r)], zz-act(ring-additive-ag(r), 1, a) = a))
- `zz-act-type-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([k in zz, a in carr(r)], zz-act(ring-additive-ag(r), k, a) in carr(r)))
- `zz-act-zero-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([a in carr(r)], zz-act(ring-additive-ag(r), 0, a) = zero(r)))
- `zz-bezout-set-membership` — forall([a, b, z_], z_ in zz-bezout-set(a, b) iff z_ in zz and forsome([x_ in zz, y_ in zz], z_ = x_ * a + y_ * b))
- `zz-coprime` — forall([a, b], zz-coprime(a, b) iff zz-is-gcd(a, b, 1))
- `zz-divides` — forall([a, b], zz-divides(a, b) iff a in zz and b in zz and forsome([c_ in zz], b = a * c_))
- `zz-is-gcd` — forall([a, b, d], zz-is-gcd(a, b, d) iff zz-divides(d, a) and zz-divides(d, b) and forall([e_ in zz], zz-divides(e_, a) implies zz-divides(e_, b) implies zz-divides(e_, d)))
- `zz-ring-def` — zz-ring = [zz, vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(zz, zz), x_ + y_), vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(zz, zz), x_ * y_), vnb-lambda(x_, zz, -x_), 0, 1]

- `zz-ring@carr` — carr(zz-ring) == zz
- `zz-ring@mul` — mul(zz-ring) == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(zz, zz), x_ * y_)
- `zz-ring@neg` — neg(zz-ring) == vnb-lambda(x_, zz, -x_)
- `zz-ring@one` — one(zz-ring) == 1
- `zz-ring@zero` — zero(zz-ring) == 0

- `zz-act-neg-sign` — forall([g], is-abelian-group(g) implies forall([k in zz, a in carr(g)], zz-act(g, -k, a) = (inv(g))(zz-act(g, k, a))))  _[warrant: informal]_
- `zz-act-nonneg` — forall([g], is-abelian-group(g) implies forall([k in nn, a in carr(g)], zz-act(g, k, a) = mpow(abelian-group-as-monoid(g), a, k)))
- `zz-act-one` — forall([g], is-abelian-group(g) implies forall([a in carr(g)], zz-act(g, 1, a) = a))  _[warrant: informal]_
- `zz-act-type` — forall([g], is-abelian-group(g) implies forall([k in zz, a in carr(g)], zz-act(g, k, a) in carr(g)))  _[warrant: informal]_
- `zz-act-zero` — forall([g], is-abelian-group(g) implies forall([a in carr(g)], zz-act(g, 0, a) = iden(g)))  _[warrant: informal]_
- `zz-is-euclidean-ring` — is-euclidean-ring(zz-ring)
